SHAFI NA ASHIRIN DA BIYU 22

Babyna kuma. Ta sake maimaitawa a cikin zuciyata katse ta yayi tare da fad’in “daman nasan zaki kirani NI’IMATULLAH nayi ta kiran number bakya d’agawa bansan maike faruwa ba amma ina fatan kin ga sak’ona kuma kina cikin k’oshin lafiya.”
“Naga sak’on ka amma ina son insan wane kai kuma mai kake nema a awajena?”
“Kamar yadda kika sani nine Salman saidai mafi yawancin abokaina suna kirana da paris sanan nin....”
“Dakata min ba wanan nakeson jiba ina son insan maine dalilin ka na kirana a waya sanan mai kake nema a gareni da ka hanani tutawa ka hanani sakewa ka shigo min rayuwa da takura mai na tsare maka?”
“Babu abin da kika tsare min nidai nakwai Na fad’a miki soyayyar ki nake nema.”
“Soyayya ta kake nema aikuwa bazaka tab’a samu ba wallahi sanan idan kace zaka soni mutuwa zakayi Na fad’a maka,soyayya ta ba abu mai sauk’i bace da zan amsa a wajen wanan in kaiwq wanan,sanan kai taabar bama kada kunya ka rasa wacce zakace zakaso sai aminiyar wadda akayi muku engage saboda toshewar kwakwalawa irin taku ta masu shaye shaye...”
“Ya isa Haka NI’IMATULLAH kin kirani dan ki cimun mutunci.” Sai a yanzu ma ta tuna cewa ita ta kira saboda tsabar tana cike da jin haushi yasa ta mata aikuwa k’ittt ta kashe wayar nan ya dame ta da kira har saida tayi blocking d’in layin saboda sam ita batama tab’a ganin mutum irin paris ba,gashi a haka kamar mai hankali amma ina ashe babu wanan hankali yanzu banda toshewar basira how did he say that,yasan cewa me nd NU’AIMA are best friend may be dai baisan irin amintar datake tsakanin muba shiyasa... Tafad’a tana jan tsaki dan ya b’ata mata rai bad’an kad’an ba...



Fitowa tayi da d’ayrin kirji ta d’auki bokiti ta d’ebi ruwa ta saka ruwan zafi sanan ta siria ta shiga band’aki tayi wanka. Bayan ta fito ta zauna ta shafa mayukanta masu dad’in k’amshi dasa laushin fata da gyara fata,kanta ta gayara sanan tayi parking d’nsa wata doguwar rigar abaya ta d’auka ta saka tayi mutuk’ar yi mata kyu sosai abin ba’a magana tana cikin shiryaawa babah jummai ta shigo d’akin rik’e da ruwan rubutu da addu’o’i a hannunta ta mik’a mata sanan tace malam yace idan zata kwanta bacci zatasha sanan ta shafe jikin ta”Yawwa NI’IMATULLAH sanan karki manta idan kinzo kwanciya bayan kin gama cin komai kin gama shan komai sai ki d’ebo ruwa a kofi ki karanta ayatul kursiyyu k’afa bakwai ki tofa ya zama shine k’arshen abin da zakisha haka ma inkin tashi daga bacci kafin kici komai ki tofa ta kafa bakwai kisha kinji,Allah ubangiji ya k’ara sauk’i ki dage na sanki bakya wasa da addu’a amma akwaiki da shiririta dan haka ki dage kinji,Allah yace tashi intaimake ka ubangiji ka taimake mu baki d’aya.”
“Ameen ya Allah babah,ameen ya Allah babah nagode sosai da sosai da kulawr ku a gareni yanda kuke kuka dani da k’annena ubangiji ya kula daku ranar tashin alk’iyama ubangiji ya baku aljanna.”
“Ameen ya Allah NI’IMA ameen ya Allah tare dake Allah yayi miki albarka ya baki miji nagari ameen ya Allah..

Tafad’a mata cewa zata gidan su NU’AIMA saboda NU’AIMA tana neman ta sanan ma ansa ranar bikin su wata d’aya mai kamawa.”
“Masha Allah ubangiji ya nuna mana yarinyar kirki,ubangiji ya nuna mana naki haka.”
“Ameen ya Allah babah.”
Tashi tayi ta nufi gidan su NU’AIMA,ba jimawa saigata taje mai gadi ya bud’e nata ta shiga da mitar ta sanan tayi hanyar palorn nasu...
Tana zuw ada sallama ta shiga inda momy ta tare ta tana mata fara’a sosai..
“Inayini momy?”
“Lafiya kalau dota yakuke yasu mamanki?”
“Suna lafiya Alhamdulillah.”
“Masha Allah haka akeso.”
“Momy ansaka kuma ranar su NU’AIMA ubangiji ya sanya Alkhairi ya basu zamn laafiya yasa zamu gani.”
“Ameen ya Allah yarinya ta,kema da zaki fito da miji tare zan had’aku na muku auren ai duk lokaci d’aya.”
Dariya tayi cike da jin dad’in abinda momy tace ta sunkuyar da kanta k’asa....
“Kinga rabin ran taki tana sama tana danne danne waya amma bari na kira miki ita.”
“Tam nagode momy.”
“Ki daina min godiya ki d’auke ni tamkar uwa agareki.”
“Insha Allah momy.”
Wayar ta d’auak ta kira NU’AIMA ta fad’a mata rabin ran ta na jiranta a k’asa fa...

Sakkowa tayi jiki a salub’e NI’IMATULLAH tayi mamakin ganin ta a Haka saboda sam bata saba ganin NU’AIMA haka ba dan kuwa idan akace tazo gidan nan nasu tsalle tsallen da NU’AIMA take sha ke kyace k’aramar yarinya ce.
Kafin NU’AIMA tayi magana momy ta kalli NU’AIMA sanan tace”lafiya momyna what’s wrong with u,are u ok.”
“Yes momy i’m ok.”
“Amma kamar kinyi kuka.” Murmushi ta k’ak’aro sanan tace”eh mana momy kuka nayi fa wani agogona ne danasiya a cyprus na nemesa na rasa sai kuma daga baya na gansa da har na fara kuka.”
“Allah ya shirya min ke ta fad’a.
Direct kusa da NI’IMATULLAH tazo ta zauna tana fad’in sannu da zuwa rabin raina.”
“Yawwa rabin raina sannu.”
“Uhm taso mu fita garding. Ta fad’a tana ruk’o hannun NI’IMATULLAH...

Tafiya sukayi a farfajiyar gidan su suka isa garding d’in daya yalwatu da shuke shuke masu ban sha’awa filawowi kala kala da furranni ga bishiyu na marmari abin gwanin ban sha’awa...

Zama sukayi akan kujerun da suke a cikin labun sanan NU’AIMAta kalli NI’IMATULLAH tace”yakike fabin ran.”
“Lafiya ta kalau amma ke kam baki da lafiya kuma kinyiwa mama k’arya maike damunki mai yasaki kuka.”
Cikin muryar ban tausayi tace”NI’IMATULLAH ina cikin damuwa sosai kawai k’arfin hali nake banaso mama ta fahimci abin da ke damuna duk da ita uwace mai fahimta nasan zata iya fahimata amma banason ta fahimci matsalar danake fama da ita saboda banason damuwar ta.”
“Subahanallah mai yake faruwa dake ne har haka.”
“Uhm NI’IMATULLAH ina cikin damuwa sosai kinga tunda aka fara maganar auren nan Salman ya canza baya kirana a waya ni zan kirasa sai ya kawo min uzuri yana kaza yana kaza sanan abu kad’an sai ya ringa min fad’a kin sani i cant take it any more ni banason fad’a shiyasa ma Allah bai halliceni da son shiga cikin mutane ba saboda ni bani son fad’a ko kad’an zuciyata tana min zafi sosai da sosai ina tunanin ko na masa wani abu ne yake min haka amma na kasa ganewa.”
Wani dogon numfashi taja sanan tace”amma rabin ran kin tabbata biki masa wani laifin ba.”
“Wallahi rabin ran babu laifin dana masa kinsan ina sosa sosai kinsan bazan iya yi masa laifin dazaiji haushi har haka ba sanan har yayi zuciya,bnsani ba ko dan yaga inasonshi shiyasa yake min haka.”
“Kiyi hakuri bahaka bane kin fad’a min cewa Salman mutumin kirki ne ko so bana tunanin cewa zaiyi miki wani abu kodan ya b’ata miki ki masa uzuri a koda yaushe ki zamo mai yiwa mutum uzuri kinji,sanan kirasa kinji zan masa magana.”
Ba musu ta ciri wayar ta tafara danna kiransa amma har ta katse bai d’aga ba.
“Kin gani rabin raina na kirasa bai d’aga ba kuma idan za’a kwana bazai kirani ba kuma bamu saba haka dashi ba,kin ga dole zanyi zargin ko auren ne bayaso dani.”
“Shittttt karki sake fad’ar haka dabaya sonki da bazai zo gidan ku ya b’ata lokacin sa abanza wajen kiranki a waya da zuwa inda kike da kuma kaiki inda kike buk’ata.”
“Amma rabin raina mazan yanzu ne I’m scared wallahi ina jin tsoro.”
“Calm down everything will be right insha Allah karki damu yanzu bani number tasa na kirasa.”
Karan to mata number tayi,thesame number sa datayi blocking sauri tayi ta bud’e dankar NU’AIMA ta gane sanan ta danna kira tasata a handsfree,aikuwa ringing biyu ya d’aga tare da cewa hello ba.... bata bari ya k’arasa ba tayi maza tace”Assalamu alaikum,sunana NI’IMATULLAH nice aminiyar NU’AIMA daman NU’AIMA ke kiranka baka d’aga shine nace bari na gwada a tana yanzu muna tare da ita gata nan.”
Tana bata wayar tayi mata da alama tayi magana gaisheshi ta farayi yana amsawa yana taso ta bawa NI’IMATULLAH wayar wai su gaisa.bayan tabawa NI’IMATULLAH wayar ne tace masa”yawwa ango daman kai muke nema idan bazaka samu damuwa ba yaushe zakazo muyu maganar yarda zamu shirya bikin da kuma tsare tsaren da zamuyi.”
“Ah aha ai babu damuwa yanzu ma sai intawo babu matsala.”
“Ahh ayi haka kada mu tsareka ko kana aiki.”
“Aa babu damuwa yanzu zanzo insha Allah.”
Haka sukayi sallama cike da mamaki NU’AIMA ta juya ta kalle ta tare da fad’in “rabin raina kinji wani ikon Allah kinga yanda yake ta rawar jiki saboda kin masa magana ikon Allah kenan.”
“Babu wani ikon Allah daman Na fad’a miki may be ko aiyukane suka masa yawa amma ai mutumin kirki ne wanda zaki aura ki kwarara gwiwarki banason ki ringa karya zuciyar ki a wani lissfi na daban kisa a ranki cewa Salman naki ne kuma ke tasa ce da yardar Allah har abada koda kuwa kinga wani suyi to karki manta da Allah kikai kukan ki wajen Allah insha Allah komai zai tafi kamar yadda yakamata.”
“Nagode nagode sosai wallahi zuciyata tayi mutuk’ar yin sanyi sosai da dad’i naji dadin kasancewar ki a tare dani fatana ubangiji yabar mana zumuncin mu har zuwa k’arshen rayuwar mu.”
“Ameen ya Allah ta amsa.”
Da yake gidan su Salman da NU’AIMA babu nisa hakan yasa nan da nan yazo parking yayi sanan ya sakko daga motar,kiran NU’AIMA yayi yace mata ya iso suna ina ta fad’a masa sanan ya nufi wajen.
Yana k’arasawa yaji tamkar an sashi a aljanna saboda NI’IMATULLAH dake wajen sai wani kallonta yake ita kuma basarwa tayi tare da fad’in “sannu da zuwa angon NU’AIMA.” Murmushi yayi sanan yace”yawwa amarya.”
Zaro ido tayi tace amarya kuma?”
“Eh mana amarya,NU’AIMA zata zama amarya ai kinga kema amaryace.”
NU’AIMA ce tace”wanan haka yake luv ina yini sannu da zuwa fatan kazo lafiya.”
“Lafiya kalau luv Alhamdullila yasu momy.”
“Kalau suke.”
Zama yayi suka fara hira dai sama sama Sai NI’IMATULLAH tace”rabin ran kinbar bak’o babu abin sha.”
“Wayyo Allah na sha’afa kinga koke ma haka na barki wayyi duk da cewa kedai ba bakuwa bace.”
“Hhhh fad’a mata dai NI’IMATULLAH.”
Sam batason yana kiran sunan ta har wani k’arawa yake da wata lankwasarsa ta munafinci...
Tashin NU’AIMA tayi ta tafi kawo musu drinks inda NI’IMATULLAH ta maida kallon ta wajen paris tare da cewa” aduk sanda zamu had’u arziki baya had’amu sanan baya rabamu inaso yaudai d’aya kabari mu had’u cikin arziki sanan mu rabu cikin sa.”kallon ta kawai yake yana murmushi yanaji kamar ya had’iyeta...
“Inajin ki madam.”
“Ya kamata yanzu ka tsaya kasan inda yake maka ciwo NU’AIMA tana da hakuri sosai idan ka sameta a matsayin mata kayi sa’a duniya kai har ma da kahira saboda zata iya yi maka silar shiga Aljanna ma,dan haka ina mai maka nasiha dan Allah ka kula da ita kamar yadda kake da koma yafi da kuna shirin aure,shi kuma aure ba abu bane na wasa dan Allah paris ka ajje duk wasu shaye shaye da abubuwanka a gefe saboda Allah ya baka mata mai kyu ga kyan hali ga hakuri....”
“Wai mai yasa kike ta wanan lissafin ne kan NU’AIMA ai a kanki yakamata ki lissafa wanan abubuwan duk wasu qualities da kike fad’a ai kin fita dan haka ni...”
“Ni ban fita ba ai kasan a inda muka tab’a had’uwa dakai dan haka karka kirawoni a matsyin wata mai qualities like that ka manta kalaman ka agarenj ne yar bariki yar shayeshaye duk da cewa ni ban tab’a shan wani abu dan ya sani maye ba amma ganin da ka min a bariki ka fad’i komai a kaina so ka ajje wanan maganar a gefe.”
“Sooo kingani ke yar bariki ni d’an shaye shaye kin gani mumua fi dacewa da juna amma ba NU’AIMA ba......
K’arar plate d’in data d’aukko drinks d’in sukaji a k’asa sun juyawa sukayi ido hud’u da ita.......

SHAFI NA ASHIRIN DA BAKWAI 27


Da sauri ta tashi ta kallesa ta murza idonta sanan ta sake kallonaa ta murza idonta wai maike faruwa ne ya ta ganta anan kuma..
“Sannu NI’IMATULLAH ya kan naki,fatan ya daina ciwo.”
Ohhhhh sai da yace mata ya kanta sanan ta tsaya ta d’anyi tunani a lokacin ne ta tuna cewa ashefa bata da lafiya ta tuna da duk abubuwan da suka faru jiya,aikuwa jikin hanzari ta tashi daga kan gadon tare da cewa”ina NU’AIMA ta farka ya jikin nata. Tana k’ok’arin sa takalmi ya tsayar da ita tare da mik’a mata hijab d’inta ta saka sanan suka fita tare dashi asuba ce dan haka ya nuna mata d’akin da NU’AIMA take ba’a shiga hakan yasa ta lek’ata ta window tana kwance dai kamar yadda suka barta jiya. Wani hawaye ne ya gangarowa NI’IMATULLAH ta koma ta zauna akan seat d’in da yake kusa da room d’in da NU’AIMA take...
Bayan yayi sallah yadawo ya tadda ita a zaune a wajen yace mata tatashi taje tayi sallah ya nuna mata masallacin mata inda ba musu taje tayi sanan ta dawo bacci yana d’aukarta saka makon allurar da aka mata a haka ta zauna a seat d’in har barci ya kwasheta,yayi yayi su tafi gida amma tak’i tace bazata iya barin NU’AIMA anan ba..
Gashi har tayi bacci yana so yaje gida yayi wanka sai ya dawo saboda jikin sa duk yayi tsami.
Juye juye ya fara ko zaiga NA’EM amma bai gansa ba,sai ransa kawai ya bashi yana reception tashi yayi ya nufi reception d’in aikuwa ya hango NA’EM zaune saman seat yana ta danna waya.
K’arasowa yayi inda NA’EM d’in ya d’go da kansa tare dacewa”Paris kaine da safiyar nan haka?”
Waje ya samu ya zauna sanan yace” uhm ai anan na kwana fa,jiya bayan ka tafi gida ni kuma na fita restaurant dancin abinci na ragewa NI’IMATULLAH hanya dan nakaita gida amma ta ringa kuka bazata bar NU’AIMA ta tafi gida ba,sai kuma tafara min ihu wai kanta zai rabe haka cikin gaggawa muka dawo asibitin a lokacin banga momy ba dan haka likita ya bata gado,yanzu haka maganar danake maka tana k’ofar room d’in da NU’AIMA take tace bazata iya tafiya tabarta ba kuma allurar barcin da aka mata bata saketa ba dan haka gashi har tayi bacci.”
“Ohhh Allah gaskiya wanan yarinya tana kaunar Aima sosai kaga fa wani ikon Allah ni bantab’a ganin k’awance kalar nasu ba tabbas wanan kawaye ne  abin so Allah yabar musu zumuncin su.”
Ameen Paris ya fad’a tarre d tunanin cewa lallai idan NU’AIMA ta dawo hayyacinta zata fad’awa kowa abin da ya faru sanan zata tsanesa amma ba laifina bane NU’AIMA Ki fahimceni wallahi Allah ne ya jarrabeni da son aminiyar ki bani da yanda zanyi na kaucewa k’addara ta.
“Ya naga kayi shiru lafiya kuwa paris.”
“Uhm lafiya kalau wallahi kawai damuwa ce tamin yawa ina tausayin halin da matata take ciki ga bikin mu ya gabato Wayyo Allah.”
“Babu komai insha Allah ubangiji zai bata lafiya zataji sauk’i da yardar Allah kuma za’ayi komai kamar yadda aka tsara babu abin da za’a fasa insha Allah,kawai kayita addu’a Allah ya bata lafiya yasa kaffarece.”
“Ameen ya Allah..”
Misalin k’arfe takwas su momy suka k’araso asibitin tare dasu INAYATULLAH da Daddy tayi mamaki sosai ganin ashe NI’IMATULLAH ba gida ta tafi ba sanan da NA’EM ya fad’a mata yadda sukayi da paris ta tausaya mata sosai sanan ta jinjinawa paris dan haka tace”NI’IMATULLAH inaso kibi paris ya kaiki gida kinji,kinga kema baki da lafiya inaso yanzu ku tafi kije ki huta insha Allah zata samu sauk’i banaso kiyi min musu kinji yar albarka.”
“Shikkenan momy Allah ya bata lafiya mai daurewa yasa kaffara ne.”
Ameen suka amsa inda ta shiga motar paris shine ya shiga suka tafi...
Hanyar unguwar su NI’IMATULLAH ya dosa babu wanda yace da wani uffan har ya kaita k’ofar gidan su,sauka tayi tare da ce masa”nagode.” Cikin sanyi murya,shima cikin mutuwar jiki yace naga malam ya fito bara naje na gaisar dashi.”
Batare da tace masa komai ba ta fita shima ya fito,shigewa gida yayi shi kuma malam ya daskare a wajen saboda abin al’ajabin da yagani.
Cike da mamaki yake kallon paris da ya k’araso gabansa tsugunnawa yayi ya gaida sa sanan ya fad’a masa koshi wanene sanan ya fad’a masa duk abubuwan da suka faru kar aga ta kwana kuma daman tace ta kira waya ta fad’a.”
Hakane malam ya amsa sanan yayi musu addu’a Allah ya k’ara sauk’i.

Kud’i ya ciro a aljihunsa ya mik’awa ajjewa malam yace a rabawa d’alubai cike da mutuntaka malam ya amsa ya gode shi kuma ya tashi ya shiga mota ya juya...

Bayan ta shiga gida direct d’akin babah ta shiga ta zauna ta zuba tagumi. Kallo d’aya babah tayi mata taga tayi rama sosai a daren d’aya,nan babah take tambayar ya jikin NU’AIMA.
“Babah har yanzu babu sauki bata frfad’o ba kuma likit yace da safe zata farfad’o amma har yanzu bata hayyacin ta Wayyo Allah ta fad’a tare da fad’in a zuciyar ta duk abin da ya faru da NU’AIMA dani da Paris ne mukaja mata innahlillahi wa inna’ilahi raju’un.”
Babah ce ta dafata tace”karki damu NI’IMATULLAH duk tsanani yana tare da sauk’i insha Allah zata samu sauk’i da yardar Allah yanzu bari na dafa miki ruwan zafi kiyi wanka sai ki kwanta ki hut naga duk a gajiye kike....



Bayan ya koma gida ne yayi wanka inda Momyn take tambayar ina zashi nan yake fad’a mata duk abin da ke faruwa da yake daddyn nasa baya nan yau kwanan sa biyar a dubai yasa basu sani ba,baifiya son shiga sabgar matan gidan ba Amma kuma ya dauki momy tamkar babarsa shiyasa yake girmamata....
Aikuwa suma shiryawa sukayi tare da kannensa sukace ya kaisu dan su dubata. Babu musu ya daukesu sukaje asibitin inda suka dubata suk mata addu’ar samu. Sau’ki ta farka amma kuma likita ya mata allura ta tafi wani baccin..
Paris ya kira Daddynsa ya fad’a masa dan haka yace a gobe insha Allah zai tawo dan kuwa hankalin sa ya tashi shima sam baiji dad’in wanan labarin ba....


Bayan ya maida su momy gida direct gidan su NI’IMATULLAH ya wuce..
Bacci take sosai saboda itama bawai lafiyar cikakkiya garetaba dan haka har sai la’asar sanan ta farka shiryawa tayi tace su Babah su shirya zata kaisu su duba NU’AIMA ana haka wani yaro ya shigo yace”wai an aikoni,wani bak’one yace dan Allah idan NI’IMATULLAH zata iya fitowa ta d’an fito yazo ya dubata sunan s Paris.”
“Dam gabanta ya fad’i,maiyasa Paris yake hake ne,tabbas harga zuciyar NI’IMATULLAH tun ajiya datuna wani har gitsatsen labari da yake son bata sai taji ta tausaya masa sai taji duk tausayin sa ya shiga zuciyar ta dan haka da sauri tacewa Babah bari naje zan dawo yanzu.”
Sulifas d’inta tasa ta zura hijab ta fita,yana cikin mota dan haka motar ta shiga ta zauna sanan ta juya ta kallesa tace”inayini paris ya kuma jikin NU’AIMA?”
“Lafiya Kalau Alhmdlh,and ya jikin naki?”
“Da sauk’i naji sauk’i.”
“Allah ya k’ara sauki,kan naki ya....
Kafin ya k’arasa tace”am Paris ta farfad’o kuwa,idan wani abu ya faru da ita kasani Paris mune sila.”
“Eh ta farfad’o kuma da sauk’i sosai,insha Allah babu abin da zai faru Sai Alkhair ki daina fad’in haka.”
Wani kyakkyawan numfashi taja sanan tayi relax a kujerar tace”kasan me Paris so nake ka bani labarin da ka fara bani daidai yanda zan fahimta.”
Wani Murmushi yayi sanan yace”wane labarin fa.”
“Ohhh ka manta,to labarin ka na jiya inason naji k’arshensa.”
A take ya canza fuska kalar wanan annurin dake kan fuskarsa gaba d’aya ya tafi idonsa ya sauya launi,ya d’an sunkuyar dakai na mintuna da kamar basai magana ba sai kuma ya tuna wani abu yafara magana”tunda kece kika nemi kiji shikkenan zakiji.
Sunana SALMAN MUHAMMAD inda mutane suke kirana da Paris,nataso a tare da mahaifina da mahaifiyata a k’asar nigeria,aiki ya maida Abbana da iyalansa k’asar dubai anan ne kuma y had’u da wni Aminin sa wanda tun suna nigeria suke tare saboda k’arfin amintar suma yasa suke basu da nisa duk da cewa shi wancen d’in bashi da rufin Asiri kamar yadda Babana yake da rufin asiri nataso cikin soyayyar su sosai da sosai kwasam bayan na girma na mallaki hankali,bayan babana ya had’u da aminin sa a dubai sun shirya dawowa gida nigeria saboda suga yan uwa sanan kuma baban ya ziyarci Iyalan aminsa,kamar wasa haka aka shirya mana tafiya danida mamata muka dawo nigeria ranar dabazan manta da ita bak’ar rana a wajena itace ranar da muka ziyarci iyalan Daddyn dubai ranar ce da komai da komai ya tonu amma kuma a ranar nayi rashi biyu nayi rashin mamata sanan nayi rashin mahaifiya ta.”
Sai yayi shiru yana jan wani dogon numfashi...”
Calm down Paris nakasa gane wanan labarin naka,kace kunzo ziyara to mai yafaru kuma wace maman naka ka rasa da kuma mahaifiyar ka ban gane ba..”

“NI’IMATULLAH tundaga ranar da muka ziyarci daddyn dubai ranar da matar daddyn dubai ta ganni taga babanaa lokacin wasu makirce makircen data k’ulla duk ya bayyana dan kuwa da kanta ta tona abin da tayi..

Wato tan ita d’in kishiyar mahaifiyatace daddyn dubai yana da rufin asiri amma shi ba mai kud’i bane inda shi kuma baba Muhammad mai kud’ine kuma yafi rayuwa a k’asar waje. Kishiyar mahaifiyata itace uwar gida an auro mahaifiyata ne bayan ita waccen tana da yara hud’u duk mata kuma ajere ajere take haihuwar su tana so Allah ya bata na miji tun a haihuwar fari amma Allah bai bataba tashiga ta fita tashiga ta fita amma duk haihuwa namiji take haifa,a kwana a tashi sai taga mahaifiyata da ciki har na tsawon wata tara wanda sam bata kula dashiba har sai da yakai watannin haihuwa,saboda fitinarta yasa daddyn dubai bai fad’a mata,aikuwa ranar data kula dashi a ranar ta nufi gidan bokanta akan a zubar da cikin nan bokanya nuna cewa cikin nan bazai tab’a zubewa ba amma idan ta haihu akwai yadda za’ai.. 
haka ta dawo gida takasa zaune ta kasa tsaye ranar da mahaifiyata tafara nakuda haka ranar maman matar baba Muhammad tafara nakuda ganin abokinsa ne amini yasa ya kaisu asibiti d’aya akayi sa’a suka haihu lafiya bayan ankaisu d’akin hutu ne suna hutawa kishiyar babata ta salallab’a ta d’aukkoni tadawo dani kusa da mamana sanan ta d’auki waccen macen ta komar da ita kusa da mahaifiyata Allahu akbar ashe ita macen ma Allah yayi mata rasuwa aikuwa hakan yayiwa kishiyar mahaifiyata daidai....
Bayan duk wanan abu data aikata tasan shi babah Muhammad baifiya zama anan ba so dole zamuyi nisa da mahaifiyata kuma babu wanda zai sani ana haaka kuwa tafiya ta tasowa babana inda muka koma america da zama kuma kishiyar mahaifiyata taje tabawa bokanta kud’ad’e cewa bazan tab’a dawowa mu had’u koda daddyna ba,ya mata alk’awarin duk sanda nadawo mukayi ido hud’u tofa da bakin ta zata fad’i abin da tayi. Tasan cewa bazan tab’a dawowa ba dan kuwa bata tunanin ma zasu sake had’uwa dashi dddyn dubai da babana hakan yasa ta amince da hakan,bayan had’uwar mu asiri ya fallasu inda a take mamana ta zube hankalin ta ya tashi cewa niba d’anta bane kuma tana ganin kamar daddyn dubai zai raba mu hakan yasa hawan jini mai k’arfi yakamata kwanan ta biyu a asibiti ta rasu.....


“To amma ina ita mahaifiyar ka kuma sanan yaya Abbah Muhammad ya yarda ya bada kai yakoma shi kad’ai.”
Babana bai badani ba dan kuwa daddyn dubai yace kamar yadda na taso a d’ansa haka har abada zan kasance d’ansa babu canji,sanan na tambayi mahaifina ina mahaifiyata duk sanda nayi masa tambayar nan baya bani amsa idan nace ko ta mutu ne yakam fad’a min shima bai sani ba dan kuwa kishiyar mahaifiyata tayi silar komai dashi bai sani ba.”
Ana haka baba Muhammad ya barni wajen mahaifina dan in masa hutu,wanan barin nawa da yayi ne ya k’untatawa kishiyar mahaifiyata har take ik’irarin wallahi bazata barni ba sai ta lalata min rayuwa pressure da bansa dashiba yafara hawar min kai a hankali nafara shan giya,ashe magani take saka min a abinci dan na kasance haka ranar da daddy yaganta da idonsa a ranar ta bud’i baki kuma take fad’a masa saboda tana ganin bazai iya sakin taba aikuwa a ranar ya saketa tashiga tashin hankali har tasha alwashin sai tadawo kuma dani da momy ne mukayi sanadin korarrta to wallahi zamu gani... daddy yayi mata fata fata sanan momy tace Allah ya fita da yardar Allah babu abin da zasu gani,ana haka kuma Na had’u da NU’AIMA itama yar abokin daddyn dubai ce,ana haka duk kafin na koma gidan babana k’addara ta sake had’ani dake ina jina jikina ke matatace kuma zan aure ku ku biyu,dan ko agaban daddy zan masa wanan bayanin tunda dai ba haramun bane,inason Ki NI’IMATULLAH kuma zan aureku keda NU’AIMA kuma rana d’aya za’ayi auren insha Allah ba fashi......

SHAFI NA ASHIRIN DA BIYAR 25

Da sauri ya k’araso daman tunda yayi parking d’in motar sa yake hango abin da yake faruwa,yana k’arasowa ya farayiwa NI’IMATULLAH magan duk da cewa baisan ko wacece ba kuma baisan sunan taba,baiwar Allah baiwar Allah kawai yake fad’a amma ina sam bata cikin hayyacinta,abinka da babban gida duk mutanen gidan basusan abin da ake ba,dan momy tana d’akin ta Ita kuma inaya ma bata nan,mai gadi ne shima ya tawo da gudu yana kallon inda NU’AIMA tayi kwance tayi plate,NA’IM hankalinsa ne ya rabu gida biyu wajen wa zaije wajen NU’AIMA dake kwance daya hango fad’uwarta koko wajen paris?”
Ganin paris yana alamun neman taimako sosai dan yafara fita a hayyacinsa yasashi sauri k’arasawa tabbas wanan lamarin ba wanan yarinyar bace ke yi,ya fahimci duk abin da ke faruwa aikuwa da sauri ya d’aura hannunsa bisa kanta ya rufe idonsa  sosai yafara karanto wasu addu’o’i.”
Ai data saki paris yayi taga taga yayi baya da k’arfi ya fad’i a k’asa sai tafara rawa jikin ta yana rawa yana kakkarwa,tana ta wani numfashi sama sama can sai ta saki k’ara ta zube a k’asa duk da k’arar datayi wanan bai hana NA’IM cigaba da abin da yake ba wani gurnani tafara yi tamkar wata zakanya...


Momy ce ta jiyo wanan k’ara sai ta lek’o ta window na sama aikuwa ta hango duk abubuwan da suke faruwa da sauri jikin ta na rawa take sakkow a kuma daidai wanan lokacin INAYATULLAH tadawo daga inda taje....


Paris ne ya tashi dakyar ya koma kan NU’AIMA yana girgizaata amma ina ko motsi batayi,ya kallo d’ayan b’angaren daake gunurzu da NI’IMATULLAH daman shi yasan za’a rini tabbas yasan za’ai haka wanan yarinyari yasan daman tana da matsala daga an mata maganar aure saita fita hayyacin ta tane mi ta kashe mutum,haka fa sukayi da alhaji d’an gaske shima Allah ne ya cece sa mtwssss...

Tashi yayi ya d’aukko mota a daidai nan momy ta sakko tana tambayar lafiya mai yake faruwa naga gidan gaba d’aya ya rikice ne?”
“Wallahi momy babu lafiya yanzu dai babu lokacin bayani NU’AIMA zan kai asibiti ku shigo mota INAYATULLAH mu tafi.”
Ita kuma NU’AIMAN mai yasameta,a sanina dai bata da iskokai bansani ba ko a makarantar kwanan nan ta kwasosu.”
“No ba aljanu bane,ta tsorata da abin da yake faruwa shine ta suma.”
Cikin hanzari kuwa tace inaytullah tahau mota su tafi insha Allah zata farfad’o bai kamata kuma su tafi su duka abar NI’IMATULLAH a wanan halin ba,tunda gaki ga paris nasan zaku kula da ita.”
“Tam shikkenan momy.” INAYATULLAH ta fad’a tana shirin yin kuka. Suna shiga ya figu motar kamar zai tashi sama saura k’iris ma ya buge mai gadin ya wuce....

Momy kuma ta koma b’angaren su NA’IM.. ta kamo NI’IMATULLAH inda take ta ihu can yacewa momy ta taimako masa da cup da ruwa,aikuwa nan da nan ta tashi ta daukko masa dan ta manta da wasu masu aiki ma a gidan saboda rud’ewa. Amsa yayi yafara karanto ayoyin Allah da kuma wasu addu’o’i da mussaman aka koya musu su lokacin da suna madina.




Wata katuwar muryace ta fara magana”kaiiiiii yaro kayi ganganci kayi mutuk’ar ganganci mai yasa zaka shiga harkar daba taka ba,kanaso ka rasa rayuwar kane kana karmin yaron ka dakai,to idan kana son kanka da arziki ka wuce kabarmu karka sake zuba mana wanan wutar idan kuwa kayi wasa. To tabbas zata tashi dakai.”
“Astagfutullah,kai wanene kuma sanan mai yasa a shigo jikin wanan baiwar Allah maine yasa ka shigo.”
“Nifa na dad’e anan kuma matata ce matatace dan haka ka fita daga sabgar mu Na fad’a maka.”

Tundaga haka NA’IM da ya sake tambayar sa bai sake magana ba haka kuwa yaci gaba da zuba mata ruwan addu’ar har ta kwanta tayi lakwab a cinyar momy.
“Momy tayi bacci ki kaita ta huta.”
Momy ce ta d’auketa takaita d’akin su NU’AIMA ta kwantar da ita akan gado sanan ta fito palor tace dashi”mai yake faruwa ne Na’im hankalina ya kasa kwanciya.”
“Momy wanan yarinyar fa a ina take kuma mai tazo yi nan gidan,sanan mai ya had’ata da paris har naga ta shak’e masa wuya,badan Allah ya aikoni yau nadawo da wuri ba da bansan mai za’ai ba.”
“Hmmm wanan d’in k’awar NU’AIMA ce wacce kullum take maka zancen ta.”
“NI’IMATULLAH right.”
“Eh haka ne,ashe baka manta sunan taba?”
“A’a momy yanzu dai ya fad’o min,gaskiya momy nayi mutuk’ar tausaya mata ganin halin data ke ciki yarinya k’arama amma ba wasu mugayen aljanune a jikin ta kina ji ma yana cewa matarsa ce tabbas momy fitar dasu ba abu bane mai sauk’i.”
“Amma NA’IM baka da taimakon da zaka mata,wanan d’in k’awar y’atace tamkar y’a nake d’aukarta ganin yadda suna shak’u da NU’AIMA dan haka kaima ina so ka d’auke ta tamkar k’anwa kaji a ranka kamar NU’AIMA ce take cikin wanan halin zaka nema mata taimako.”
“Insha Allah mom saidai akwai wani abokina da mukayi karatu tare zan masa magana amma ba’anan garin yake ba yana maiduguri amma insha Allah ko zuwa bayan bikin su NU’AIMA ne komai zai warware Allah ya bata lafiya.”
“Ameen ya Allah Ameen,yawwa kira min INAYATULLAH naji yanda sukayi a asibiti.”
D’aukko wayar sa yayi ya danna kiran INAYATULLAH tana d’agawa da kuka a bakinta tace”hello bro ka fad’awa momy cewa ciwon NU’AIMA mane ya tashi yanzu nan kikita yake sanar samu dan hakaa sun shgar da ita I.C.U.”
Innahlillahi wa’inna ilahi raju’un gamu nan zuwa.”
Momyce ta kalle sa tace mai kuma ya faru?”
“Ciwon NU’AIMA ne ya tashi.”
“Innahlillahi wa innailahi raju’un,ya ubangiji kana ji kana gani ubangiji ka dafawa wanan yarinya ubangiji ka bata lafiya ka shiga lamarinta,bara na daukko mayafi na mu tafi sanan ka kira daddyn ku kafad’a masa.”
Sama ta haura ta d’aukko mayafin tana sakkowa a daidai lokacin NI’IMATULLAH ta juya,ta sake juyawa sai ta farka ganin ba d’akin ta bane kuma ita kad’ai a d’akin gaskiya ma bata gane ko ina bane si kawi ta fito tana dube dube,taga momy ta sauka zata fita a palorn da sauri ta sakko tana sakkowa tana tunawa da abin da ya faru tace”momyyy.”
“Juyowa momy tayi sanan tace”kin tashi NI’IMATULLAH.”
“Eh nastashi ina NU’AIMA?”
“I’m sorry NU’AIMA tana asibiti ciwon ta ya tashi.”
“Ciwo kuma,wane kalar ciwo ne da ita.”
“Shigo mota babu lokaci,”
Bayan sun shiga motar ne ta kalli momy hankali a tashe tace”momy dan Allah wane kalar ciwo ne da NU’AIMA?”
“NU’AIMA tana fama da ciwon zuciya tun batafi 12 years ba hakan ya bayyana,haka dai muke treating d’in ta bama son abin da zai d’aga mata hankali ko damuwarta.”
“Innahlillahi wa innailahi raju’un,ciwon zuciya kumwa wayyo Allah na,aikuwa sai ta fashe da kuka sosai momy ce take ta lallashin ta,shidai yana tuk’i hankalin sa gaba d’aya Yana asibiti wajen yar uwarsa...

 Basu jima ba saigasu a asibitin saboda babu nisa da gidan nasu,shiga ciki sukayi inda suka nufi b’angaren da ake kaita take ganin likita,likitan suka gani ya kwatanta musu inda suke... suna zuwa sukaga su paris da INAYATULLAH sunyi carko carko a tsaye ita kuma tana ciki an saka mata oxygen tana kwance a gado ba’a bawa kowa damar shiga ba..
Momy ta zauna akan kujera tayi tagumi tana karantawa NU’AIMA addu’o’in samun sauk’i,itama NI’IMATULLAH zama tayi akan kujera cikin mutuwar jiki da rasa yak’ini tama rasa yanda zatayi da rayuwar ta...

Suna nan haka rai babu dad’i har ishsha’i tayi inda suka nufi massalacin dake asibitin dan yin sallah jikin paris gaba d’aya ya mutu yama rasa mai yakamata yayi yasani idan NU’AIMA ta mutu to babu makawo shine ya kasheta ya rasa yaya zaiyi ma da rayuwar sa ne... shida NA’IM sukayi sallah inda  daddy ya k’araso kenan dan kuwa baya garin. Tambayar NA’IM ya fara yi kamar haka”NA’iM wane irin ganganci ne da rashin tunani irin naku ya jawo haaka?”
Gaban paris sai bugawa yake kamar ana buga ganga saboda tsoro kar NA’IM yace wani abu..
NA’IM yace”wallahi daddy bansan abin da ya faru ba nadawo daga wajen aiki ne kawai ina shigowa gida ita kuma zata fito a daidai k’ofar palor ta zame ta fad’i,daga nan ta suma ma,da muka kawo ta kuma likita ya gwada ta yace ciwon tane y tashi.”

“Sakacin banza da wofi,wallahi kasani NA’IM, PARIS DA MOMY naji wani abu tsab’anin haka wallahi sai ranku ya b’aci Na fad’a muku.”


Yana fad’ar haka ya koma reception ya zauna....

SHAFI NA ASHIRIN DA SHIDA 26

Momy ce ta kalli NI’IMATULLAH tace da ita”daughter ya kamata ace kin koma gida kinga dare yayi hankalin mamanki zai tashi.”
“A’a momy wallahi bazan iya tafiya inbar NU’AIMA a cikin halin da take ciki ba momy zan tsaya har sai ta farka.” Tunawa tayi da k’aramar wayar data siyaawa babah jummai ta d’aukko tafara daddanawa ta kirata,fad’a mata cewa NU’AIMA batada lafiya yanzu ma suna asibiti gashi har yanzu bata farfad’o ba dan haka tana ganin sai lokacin da su momy zasu tafi sanan itama sai ta dawo gidan gashi kanta sai ciwo sosai yake kamar zai fashe...
Babah jummai taji tausayin NU’AIMA sosai tayi mata addu’ar samun sauk’i sanan tace shikkenan babu damuwa zata fad’awa malam.”


Komawa gefe tayi ta zauna shima paris baya zaune baya tsaye kawai baisan lokacin da ya tashi a firgice ba yayi hanyar fita gudu gudu babu wanda ya kula dashi a lokacin Sai NI’IMATULLAH dan kuwa shi NA’EM ya koma gida ya d’aukko musu abubuwan buk’ata duk da cewa su momy zasu koma gida amma shine zai kwana a asibiti kowa dai jiki babu dad’i....

Ganin tabbas paris ba’a hayyacinsa ya fita ba hakan yasa tayi gaggawar binsa,duk batare da momy ta ankara ba mota ya shiga ya runga lalube koda zaiga kwalbar giya amma bai gani ba ai kuwa sai ya fisgi motar daidai nan NI’IMATULLAH ta k’araso garesa tare da matsawa gaba saura k’iris ya take ta da mota yaja birki cikin tashin hankali ya fito ya janyota da k’arfi ya saka ta a motar tare da rufe murfin baice da ita komai ba yaja mota yabar asibitin.
Kallon sa kawai takeyi amma ya d’auke kansa yama k’i bari su kalli juna ita kuma so take ta tambaye sa maike faruwa mai yake damunsa kuma ina zashi haka ne cikin fushi datashin hankali...
Tsayawa yayi daidai wani shago ya fito ya kulle motar sanan yaje ya mik’a kud’i kamar yadda aka saba haka aka d’aukko aka basa cewa yayi biyu yake buk’ata suka k’ara masa glass cup ya d’auka ya fara zubawa yana sha a take ya shanye wanan d’ayar sanan ya tawo da d’ayar ya bud’e yana sha...

Zaune take a cikin motar tunani kala kala ya shiga zuciyar ta wai maike faruwa ne ina kuma paris yayi,sanan mai ya tafi yine yabarta a mota kuma ya rufe motar batare da yamata wani k’arin bayani ba wai mai yake faruwa dashi. Tana cikin wanan tunanin ta hangosa ya nufo motar yana ta watan gaririya.. yana zuwa ya bud’e motar dakyar ya shiga wani bak’in ciki datakaici ne ya tokareta tazo zata maresa kawai sai ta fasa sai ta rik’o rigarsa tana masa magana kamar haka”wai dan Allah paris kai mutum ne kuwa anya kai mutum ne,sam d’abi’unka basa kama dana mutane,gaka nan kowa yagan ka sai yace kai mutumin kirki ne amma sam bahaka bane,gaskiya paris nataya aminiyata kuka sosai da sosai irin mijin da Allah ya bata kenan zanso ace wanan auran ya lalace dan sam wallahi buku dace ba sam baka dace da NU’AIMA nitsartsiyar mace kamila,amma kai fa?mashayi....
Bata k’arasa ba shima ya cukumeta yana fad’ar da d’i kik’ara  NI’IMATULLAH nid’in mashayi ne sam ban dace da NU’AIMA ba,bandacw da ko wane irin mutumin kirki ba nid’in bani da tarbiyya mashayi ne amma bari kiji abin da biki sani ba NI’IMATULLAH kinsan wanda yayi sanadiyyar fad’awa ta wanan yana yin ba kowa bane illah kishiyar uwa.”
Gababtane yayi mummunanr fad’uwa tace”ban gane ba Paris mai kake nufi.”
Dafe kai tayi sosai sakamakon wanan kishiyar uwar da Yafad’a daman kanta ciwo yake mata kamar zai fashe tace”mai yasa kace haka paris ita tadawo dakai haka ko kuwa ita tasa kake shaye shaye ban gane ba tilas tayi maka ko dole nakasa fahimta.”
“Ta cuceni ta rabani da iyayena a lokcin da nafi buk’atar su saida lokaci ya kure komai ya lalace na gano mahaifina har nake zaune a gidan sa a yanzu.”
“Nakasa gane labarin ka bana ma wani fahimta dan Allah ka maidani asibiti kaina ciwo yake kamar zai fashe.”
Hankalin sane ya take sosai yaja da baya ya sauke kukerar gaba inda ya kwantar da ita gaba d’aya jikin ta ya d’auki zafi kamar garwashi.”
“Innahlillahi NI’IMATULLAH kamata yayi kema kiga doctor,da nayi tunanin nakaiki gida amma yanzu dai kam bari nafara kaiki kiga Dr.”
A hankali idon ta yake lumshewa har ya rufe gaba d’aya ta daina ganin komai..
Duk da cewa a shaye yake amma hakan bai hanasa saurin jan mota inda ya nufi hanyar asibiti gashi dare yayi,gashi idon sa sai lumshewa yake kamar dai bazai iya k’arasawa ba dakyar ya k’arasa,akwai abokinsa dake aiki a asibitin dan haka kiransa yayi aa waya duk da cewa wayar ma tana ta masa biyu biyu uku uku haka yayi sa’a ya kirasa amma cikin rashin sa’a yake sanar dashi cewa wallahi ya koma gida amma bari ya kira abokin aikin sa babu damuwa...


Zuwa wanan lokaci momy ta d’auka NI’IMATULLAH ta tafi gida dan bata gantaba haka shima NA’EM ya dauka paris ya tafi gida dan bai gansa ba,dan haka su momy sun tafi gida NA’EM ne dai ya zauna saboda shine zai kwana daddy ma ya koma gida saboda ba’a buk’atar mutane dayawa mutum d’aya ake buk’ata.


A haka mayan nasa yake ta jansa,haka ya fito da ita idonsa duk dai yana raba masa hanya amma cikin sa’a likitan ya hango su, da sauri yazo inda suke sanan yace”Hiii I’m dr Ameer,Dr ne yamin kwatance ku kaine paris.”
Dakyar paris ya d’aga kansa yace”yeah I’m sanan ya nuna NI’IMATULLAH yace she need first help plsss dr.”
Kallom sa yayi sosai sanan yace” and u too,kaima kana buk’ata.”
“Nace maka ina buk’ata Na fad’a maka kayi gaggawar bata agajin gaggawa ni bana buk’atar wani taimako kaji ko.”
Yafad’a yana daka masa tsawa...
Da sauri Dr yace ya d’aukko masa ita haka suka kamata aka kaita wani room inda dr ya auna ta yaga stress ne na ciwan kai sosai sai yayi mata allurai harda na barci... 
paris zama yayi akan wata kujera a gefen gadon ya kifa kansa a haka kuma bacci yayi awan gaba dashi shima...

Tunda baba jummai taga NI’IMATULLAH tasan tabbas jikin NU’AIMA ne ya tsanan ta insha Allah suma da safe zasuje su dubata....


Misalin k’arfe uku na dare NU’AIMA tafara motsawa aikuwa da sauri NA’EM ya tafi ya kira Dr. nan dr yazo ya sake duddubata sanan ya sake mata allura yace insha Allah zuwa safiya zata farka da yardar Allah.”


Haka NA’EM ya dawo ya zauna a kujerar yana ta tunanin abin da yasa ciwon NU’AIMA ya tashi ga kuma abin da ya faru d’azu shikam dabai saba son hayaniya ko tashin hankali ba sam abun bai masa dad’i ba,abubuwan duniya duk sun tsaya masa ya sake dubar k’anwar tasa Allah sarki abin tausayi kamar ba uta bace mai shegen surutun nan ba tanayi suna mata fad’a akan ta fiya magana amma saigashi yanzu tana kwance rai a hannun Allah bata iya ko motsa hannunta Allah sarki rayuwa kenan...
Ya fad’a yana tashi ya shiga toilet ya d’auro alwala yazo ya fara nafula yana rok’on Allah akan yabawa k’anwarsa mafi soyuwa agaresa lafiya...




Yanzu ashe zaki iya bari a rabamu ashe zaki iya bari wasu suyi tasiri agaremu a soyayyar mu dan suga sun raba mu.”
“A’a SAHEED kasani bazan tab’a iya bari a rabani dakai ba,duk wanda yace zai rabamu Allah saidai naga bayan sa amma baza mu tab’a rabuwa ba tunda muka had’u mun had’u kenan babu rabuwa.”
“Tun da nake bantab’a ganin bil adam d’in da nake tsorata da firgita idan nagansaba kamar NA’EM na tsane sa banason ganin sa na tsane sa wallahi,bani da yanda zanyi dashi amma inaso kema karki sake kuma sake had’uwa saboda bamai sanki bane kuma yanaso ya rabaki da mai sanki suna shirya wani shiri karki yarda da wani mai magani Na fad’a miki.”
“Naji SAHEED kasan bazan iya rayuwa babu kaiba maine yasa kake fad’ar haka.” Shi SAHEED yasan cewa duk sanda tagansa tana fad’in bazata iya rayuwa idan babu shiba amma kuma duk sanda ya kasance ya nisanceta bazata tab’a tuna cewa sunyi wata rayuwa ba har sunyi soyayya ba sam bazata iya tunawa ba shiyasa yake so ya jajir ce akan karta sake ta yarda a rabasu kar ta yarda..
“Amma SAHEED wai ya maganar auren namu kuwa mai zai hana yanzu mune a d’aura mana aure.”
“A halin da ake ciki bazan iya b’oye miki baza mu iya aure ba saboda wasu abubuwa da suka bijiro amma tabbas idan kika nisnci wanan bil adam d’in to tabbas babu abin da zai hana mu aure.”
“Shikkenan SAHEED duk yadda kace haka za’ai,danni idan ban aure kaba babu wanda zan aura har namutu.”
“K’arya kike yarinya k’aryar ki tasha k’arya ai tuntuni kin riga da kinyi aure kuma kinyi nafarko kinyi nak’arahe.”
“Wayyo SAHEED ka koresa zai kasheni idan yana magana kaina yana mim ciwo sosai kamar zai fashe ka koresa bana son ganin sa Wayyo SAHEED SAHEED...
A halin da ake ciki SAHEED bazai sake tunkarar ifiritun nan ba kuma shima bazai tun karesaba dan kuwa akwai badak’ala sosai a gabzawar tasu......

Tana fad’ar SAHEED SAHEED tana dafe kai ta farka tana fad’in kaina zai fashe SAHEED kaina zai fashe Wayyo Allah na.”

Firgigit paris yayi ya farka yaganta yana dafe kai tana kiran SAHEED tabbas a yanzu ya gane SAHEED shine shaid’anin aljanin da yake tare da ita kuma yake so ya kassara mata rayuwa...

dafe kannata yayi yana karan to mata wasu addu’o’i da ya koya a wajen malaminsu,a hankali take sauke numfashi har tadawo hayyacin ta dakyar take bud’e ido a hankali ta bud’e sa tangararo akan paris....

SHAFI NA ASHIRIN DA TAKWAS 28

“Amma wanan wace irin magana kake yi Paris,kasani dani dakai babu aure har abada koda da soyayya tofa babu aure bazan tab’a auren mijin k’awata ba koda kuwa zan mutu banyi aure ba kuma ina mai jan kunnen ka da gargad’i agareka kada ka sake ko da wasa ka fad’awa wani wanan maganar,idan kuwa kayi kuskuren sanar da wani wanan maganar wallahi sai kayi danasanin sanina da kayi Na fad’a maka. Tana gama fad’ar haka ta fita a motar ta koma gida tace Babah ta fito su tafi,babu musu Babah ta fito ta shirya inda NI’IMATULLAH ta d’aukko motar ta suka shiga suka nufi asibitin..

Suna zuwa a harabar asibitin tayi parking d’in motar sanan suka fita suka nufi room d’in da NU’AIMA take,bayan sunje taga bataga alamar su momy ba ta le’ka ta window taga babu NU’AIMA sai ta ciro wayar ta tafara kiran INAYATULLAH.
Direct tayi mata kwatancen inda aka komar dasu,aikuwa kamar yadda ta kwatanta mata kamar haka suka nufi room d’in su momy duk suna zazzaune akan kujera inda ita kuma NU’AIMA tana kwance a kan gadon suna ta fira tana kallon kowa da kowa amma batayi magana ba ganin NI’IMATULLAH ne yace NU’AIMA yin murmushi sanan NI’IMATULLAH tafara gaisar dasu momy sanan ta matsa kusa da ita..
Babah ce tace”ina yini hajiya ina gajiya,ya kuma jikin wanan y’a.”
“Lafiya Kalau Alhmdlh mama,gashi nan jiki da sauk’i sosai Alhmdlh.”
“To ubangiji ya k’ara sauk’i yasa kaffarane..
Haka sukayi shiru tunda ba sabawa aukayi da juna ba...

NU’AIMA ce ta kama hannun NI’IMATULLAH ta rik’e gam. NI’IMATULLAH ta kalleta tare da cewa”ya jikin Ki NINU ina fatan yna miki sauk’i sosai.”
“Da sauk’i sosai rabin raina.”
“Allah ubangiji yabaki lafiya mai d’aurewa ya k’ara sauk’i yasa kaffarace.”
Lumshe ido tayi sanan ta bud’e tare da fad’in”ameen NINU an bani labarin yadda jiya kikak’i tafiya konan dacan duk da kema d’in ba lafiya garekiba Allah ya bar mana zumuncin mu nagode da.... kafin tak’arasa tasa hannu ta rufe mata baki tare da fad’in “kiyi shiri NINU kinsani ba’a buk’atar mara lafiya ya ringa magana sosai kamar haka.”
“Uhm rabin raina kenan ai naji sauk’i.”
Tana fad’ar haka daidai lokacin da Paris yayi sallama ya shigo,gaisar da su momy yayi inda momy tacewa Babah su koma waje dan sufi gaisawa dakyu haka suka fita sukabar d’akin inda NU’AIMA ta juya bayan ta daga yanda take ya kom bayan ta kawai Paris yakeiya gani..
Hawaye ne yake bin kumatunta yan wanke mat fuska...
Sallama yayi amma babu wanda ya amsa masa,saboda bakin NI’IMATULLAH yayi nauyi takasa magana balle kuma NU’AIMA datake kuka sosai...
“Innahlillahi maike faruwa NU’AIMA mai yasameki dan Allah kibar kukan nan kinsan yana yin condition da kike ciki karki kuka plssss.”
Share hawayen ta tayi duk da cewa wani yana bin wani sanan tace”maine ya kawo ka nan Salman,nasan cewa baka sona amma kuma nake k’aryata zuciya ta,amma tundaga lokacin da naji da kunne na na gasgata abin da zuciya take fad’amin,wallahi Salman na yarda ka auri NI’IMATULLAH indai hakan zaisa kuyi farin ciki....
“NI’IMATULLAH ce ta katseta da,karki k’ara fad’ar haka aima ni babu wata soyayya tsakanina da Paris ki sani,kuma ni bazan tab’a auren saba wallahi har abada.”
“Idan ke bakya sanshi,aishi yana sonki yanzu bazaki iyayi danni ba.”
“Dan Allah bana son wanan maganar Aima ki daina fad’ar haka kada kibari wani yaji na rok’eki.”
Salman ne yace”NU’AIMA baki da lafiya dan Allah kibar wanan maganar sanan ni bantab’a k’inki ko sau d’aya a rayuwata ba ina sonki sosai da sosai hasali kece wacce nafara so duk cikin y’an mata dan haka ina mai tabbatar miki da nid’in masoyin kine sanan ina mai baki hak’urin abin da ya faru.”
“Mai yasa zaka bani hakuri Salman Abin da yakamata ne kakeyi ni banga laifin kaba.”
“Ki daina maganar nan dan Allah.”
NI’IMATULLAH ce tace”wallahi Aima indai har bazaki shiru da wanan maganar ba to zan tafi kuma zan tabbatar miki da idan natafi bazaki k’ara ganina ba har abada dan haka indai kinason ki daina ganina kici gaba da fad’ar duk abin da kikaga dama.”
K’ara rik’e hannunta tayi sosai sanan tace”babu inda zaki muna tare har abada... Tafad’a hawaye yana zubadaga idonta sosai suka rarrasheta inda NI’IMATULLAH take ta goge mata hawayen nata da tissue...
Paris dabai fiye san damuwa tayi masayawa ba dan yanzu sai ya fita a hayyacinsa yasa yayi musu sallama ya tafi..

Juyowa NU’AIMA tayi ta kalli NI’IMATULLAH tare da cewa”dagaske Salman yana sona Rabin raina kina ganin idan na auresa banyi kuskureba.”
“Tabbas hakane Aima yana sonki sosai da sosai,idan kika auresa bazaki tab’a kuskure ba,kuma insha Allah zai kula dake sosai da sosai nasan da haka.”
“Tam shikkenan Rabin raina indai zai kula dani har haka to ina so kimin wata alfarma guda d’aya.”
“Ina jinki wace irin Alfarma ce wanan,ki nemi duk abin da kike nema a wajena babu abin da bazan miki ba NU’AIMA indai har ina da dama.”
“Kina da dama sosai.”
“Wanan wace irin dama ce.”
“Uhm inaso ki auri paris indai har zai kula dani kamar haka to na tabba zai linka kulawar da zai min wajen yi miki ita.”
Wani gaban NI’IMATULLAH ne yayi mummunar fad’uwa ta dafesa tace”Na fad’a miki kada ki sake min kalar wanan zance,wallahi NU’AIMA inda zaki ringa min wanan kalar zancen zaki nemeni ki rasa kuma bazaki sake ganinaba inafad’a miki wanan maganar da gaske bada wasa ba.”
Ajiyar zuciya tayi sanan ta rufe ido batare data sake cewa komai ba..
Blanket d’in ta jawo ta rufeta dashi sanan tace da ita”ki kwanta kiyi bacci mai dad’i kinji sai na dawo zankai baba gida.”
Murmushi tayi mata sanan ta fita suka sake gaisawa da momy sanan tayi mata sallama momy ce tacewa NI’IMATULLAH ta zauna a gida fa basai tadawo ba tunda gashi har magrif tayi,NI’IMATULLAH badan taso ba tacewa momy ta shikkenan bazata dawo ba insha Allah sai gobe haka suka yi sallama suka tafi..


Bayan ya koma gida ne yayi wanka ya shirya tsaf abbansa ya kirasa ya fad’a masa ya shirya nan da awa d’aya yaje ya d’aukkosa a filin jirgi,yayi mamaki sosai yace”wai daddy yau ka tawo kenan na d’auka ai sai gobe.”
“A’a son fasa wa nayi kaganni tare da Abbanka dan ya biyoni dan yaji ya ake ciki a game da shirye shiryen auren naka.”
“Uhm tam shikkenan daddy sai kun dawo ubangiji ya kawo ku lafiya.”
Bayan ya shirya tsaf ya fad’awa momy ya tafi ya tawo da daddy nan Zaituna tace ita sai ta bishi da har yace ta zauna bazai je da ita ba sai kuma yace ta tawo su tafi...
Ba abu bane mai sauk’i ace ka manta da wanda ka d’auka a matsayin mahaifin ka dan haka ganin Abban nasa dayayi yayi mutuk’ar dad’ad’a masa,wata kewarsa ce ta daban take shigarsa dan haka yayi huging nasa sosai tare da cewa”i miss u so much my lovely dad.”
“Missing u too my son.”
Sannu da zuwa yayiwa daddyn dubai.
Daddyn dubai yace”lallai naga yan ubanci shine ni baza’ai hugging d’in nawaba sai ace min wani welcome.”
“Ohhh sorry daddy come close.”
Dariya sukayi sanan suka shiga mota yana wa zaituna magana itama tana ta cewa tayi kewar sa,a mota ne Abba yake cewa”son ya jikin amaryar taka,naji labarin she’s not feeling fine,har yakai ta kwanta asibiti.”
“Eh wallahi Abba tana fama da jiki sosai.”
“Ohhh God ubangiji ya bata lafiya.”
“Ameen daddyn dubai Yafad’a sanan yace”wallahi shiyasa duk jikina yayi sanyi naji dole inaso naje na dubata dan haka insha Allah gobe zamuje ka kaimu hospital d’in.”
Shikkenan daddy Allah ya kaimu..

Haka suka je gida ana ta murnar dawowar su..


Yaukam tunda ta kwanta bacci gabanta yake ta fad’uwa ta rasa dalili amma sai ta alak’anta hakan akan maganganun da sukayi da Aima,amma har gari ya waye bat dainajin fad’uwar gaba ba dan haka saitafara karanta,la’ilaha’illah anta subahanaka inni kuntu minazzalumin..
Acikin zuciyar ta tana addu’a Allah dai yasa ba wani abu bane zai samu NU’AIMA kar taje ta mutu fa wayyo....

Aikuwa ta kasa zaune ta kasa tsaye aikuwa tun safen ta shirya dan zuwa asibitin.....




Abla ce.✍🏻
NI’IMATULLAH 



BY HAAJARA ABLA

SHAFI NA ASHIRIN DA TARA 29

Babh ce ta kalle ta tare da fad’in”wai NI’IMATULLAH lafiya kuwa,naga gabadaya bakya hayyacin ki,gashi naga kina ta sauri sauri.”
“Wallahi babah yau kawai na tashi gabana yana ta fad’uwa shine nake sauri naje nagani NU’AIMA Allah yasa ba wani abu bane ya sameta.”
“Insha Allah ba wani abu bane ya faru da ita da yardar Allah,ki ta addu’a kinji,Allah ya kaiki lafiya.”
Haka ta fito jiki duk ya mutu wani kala take ji,tana ta addu’a dai Allah yasa ba wani abu bane ya samu Aimanta.
Misalin k’arfe sha biyu na safe ta isa asibitin,direct d’akin da NU’AIMA take ta nufa inda ta tarar dasu momy harda su daddy gaba d’aya sai sun hallara. Shiga tayi kanta a k’asa ta fara gaida mommy da Daddy sanan ta gaida NA’EM da wanan shine karo na biyu data gansa kawai sai taji gaban ta ya sake fad’uwa yana da kwarjini sosai dan Allah yayi masa,shiyasa ko had’a ido bata iya yi dashi...
“My daughter ga waje ki zauna momy ta nuna mata seat,inda tace tam ta zauna ta sunkuyar da kanta a k’asa inda momy take cewa”ai da sauk’i sosai jikin NU’AIMA saboda likita muke jira ma zai sallame mu ita kuma tana bacci ma.”
“Ayya ubangiji ya k’ara mata sauk’i yabata lafiya mai d’aurewa.”
Idan ta d’ago ido duk da ba NA’EM zata kalla ba amma sai gabanta ya fad’i kawai sai ta tuna da maganganun SAHEED cewa ta nisanci NA’EM kada ta sake su k’ara had’uwa,yanzu yaya zatayi tabbas data san yana nan da bazata k’araso ba,amma babu mafita,tayi tunane tunane amma duk ba mafita kawai sai tacewa momy”am momy zan d’an fita.”
“Ok babu damuwa yarinya ta sai kin dawo.”
Fita tayi inda ya bita da kallo saida ta fice sanan ya dawo da kallon sa kan NU’AIMA wacce tafarka yanzu.”
Tashi yayi ya matsa kusa da ita yana shafa kanta sanan yace”little momy ya jikin naki.” Murmushi tayi tankalle sa tare da fad’in”fine big bro.”
“Masha Allah.. su Daddy ne suke tambayar ko wani wajen yana mata ciwo ta fad’a musu a’a babu inda yake mata ciwo kan nata ma da yake ciwo da yanzu ya daina.”
Daddy ne yace da NA’EM yaje ya kirawo Dr.yazo ya dubata.
Babu musu ya nufi office d’in Dr. ya kirasa inda yace gasa nan zuwa nan da 2mints. Dawowa yayi yana gab da zai shiga d’akin ya hangota a k’ark’ashin wata bishiya look like kamar magana take amma shi idan zai iya dubawa dakyu gaskiya baiga mutum a wajen ba hasali ma mutane d’aya d’aya ne a wajen,k’ara matsowa yayi dan idon sa ya gane masa abin da yake gani kuma ya kasa kunne dan yaji tana ta magana.”



NI’IMATULLAH inajin zafi sosai da sosai ina jin zafi yana tunkaro ni kawai mubar asibitin nan bana son zuwa nan.”
“Amma SAHEED kabari naje nayiwa NU’AIMA sallama kana ganin ko ganina fa batayi ba sakamakon tana bacci na shiga plssss kabari nayi mata sallama sai mu tafi.”
“Amma fa nasan zaki iya samun SHEIK a ciki,SHEIK shine sunan da y’an gidan au NA’EM suke kiransa dashi... kafin ta samu damar yin magana kawai ta nemi SAHEED ta rasa aikuwa ta fara juye juye tana fad’in SAHEED SAHEED ina kayi... cak ta tsaya ganinsa tsaye a kanta tare ta cewa” what are u looking for.”
“Uhm am daman daman.”
Shi ba mainsan surutu bane amma duk da haka sai ya tsinci kansa da cewa”yakamata kibar nan wajen kamar babu security kuma wajen yayi duhu bai kamata ki ringa tsayawa a irin wanan wajen ba.”
“Shikkenan yaya nagode.”
Ajiyar zuciya sakamakon jin wanan muryartata very silent nd so sweet,a hankali yace NU’AIMA ta farka kuma ing yanzu likita zai bamu sallama zamu wuce gida.”
Masha Allah Tafad’a tana tafiya dan zuwa taga k’awartata.
Kamar yadda NA’EM ya fad’a haka likita yazo ya rubuta musu takardar sallaam sanan ya bata magunguna sanan ya fad’a musu dan Allah su kula da lafiyar ta sosai dan ba’a son abin da zai b’ata mata rai ko ya yake...
Tare da NI’IMATULLAH suka dawo gidan dan kuwa yamma batayi ba shiyasa tace zata rakasu daga nan sai tadawo gida...

Bayan sun dawo gida sun zauna a palor gabad’aya har Daddy,NI’IMATULLAH duk a takure take tana son tafiya su kuwa ko a jikin su saboda sun riga sun saba suna rayuwa tamkar turawa abinka da yan boko kuma ga ilimin addini da tarbiyya hakan yasa suke abu abin sha’awa da birgewa...
NI’IMATULLAH tana zaune a cikin su cike da kewa ta mahaifinta da mahaifiyar ta,cewa NU’AIMA tayi ana son zata shiga toilet aikuwa ta rakata,tana shiga bathroom d’in ta fashe dakuka,saboda tayi kewar mahaifanta sosai da sosai tayi kewar maman ta sosai da sossi tana rok’o kuma bazata fasa rok’on ubangiji ya dawo mata da mahaifiyar taba da mahaifinta kuka take sosai...
Ina kewarki mamana,ina kewarka abbana nakasa jure rashin ku agareni bazan iya d’auka ba yayi min nauyi da yawa,dani da k’anne na muna buk’atar ku yaranku suna buk’atar ku a kusa dasu,nakasa mantawa innahlillahi wa inna ilahi raju’un wajen shekara shida kenan kin min nisa and u Abbana wajen saving years nayi kewarka sosai da sosai ubangiji yasa kuna cikin kwanciyar hankali a duk inda kuke,ubangiji yasa kuna cikin k’oshin lafiya....

Alwala ta dauara sanan ta fito ta tada sallah tana ta sallah da rok’an Allah da ya bayyana mata iyayenta...


Kiran Paris ne ya shigo wayar sa,dubawa yayi sanan ya d’auka”hello bro yakake ne.”
“Normal,am mun shigo asibiti tare dasu Daddy da Abbana amma naga babu kowa a d’akin ko an canza muku d’aki ne?”
“Ohhh God bata kiraka ta fad’a maka ba,ai an sallame mu bamu dad’e da koma gida ba,yaushe Daddy ya dawo kuma.”
“Ayya jiya ya dawo,shikkenan badamuwa bari kawai mu wuce.”
“Aa maine haka bazaku k’araso gida ba,kaji ka kamar wasu bak’i daman shima Daddy yana nan kaga sai kuyi masa bazata.”
“Hakane fa bro,to shikkenan sai mun k’araso.”
“Ok Allah ya kawo ku lafiya.”
Sauke wayar yayi sanan ya fad’awa Daddy yadda sukayi da Sheik,Daddyn dubai kansa ne yake sarawa da har zaice kawai su tafi gida sai kawai yace suje amma baza su dad’e ba....

Haka kuwa akayi suka shiga mota yaja su ya kawosu har gidan surukan nasa kuma gidan Abokin sa,bayan sunyi parking a parking space ne suka fito inda ya sake yiwa NA’EM waya yace masa gashi nan bari ya fito....
Ko sauke wayar baiyi ba Sheik ya k’araso cikin ladabi da d’a’a ya gaida du Daddyn dubai da Abba Muhammad sanan yayi musu iso zuwa palorn Daddyn nasu.zama sukayi yasa masu aiki suka kawo musu abin tab’awa sanan ya koma yacewa Daddyn sa yayi bak’in. Daddy yayi mamaki sosai dan kuwa baisan Daddyn dubai ya dawo ba dan haka yaji dad’in ganin su sosai a tare dashi harda Abba Muhammad sosai suka zauna suna ta firar yaushe gamo dan kuwa tunda aka sa rana basu sake had’uwa ba..
Daddy dubai yace”ya mai jiki kuwa gashi yar tawa batada lafiya ga aure saura sati biyu.”
“To ya za’ai daddy haka Allah ya tsara sanan nidai d’ana bazai jirana babu fashi za’a d’aura auren daga baya idan yar taka ta warware sai ayi biki.”
Daddyn dubai yace”aa daddy saboda sankai dai,da dai an barmin y’ata ta warware sosai tukun.”
“Hhhhh Abba sa baki a maganar nan yarinyar safa taji sauk’i sosai zansa akirawota ya ganta.”
“Ahhhh daddy kada ka wahalar min da yarinya da kaina zan shiga na ganta sai mu gaisa da momynta.”
“Wanan haka yake,duk abin da uban y’a yace haka za’ai saboda haka daddyn dubai anan dai na goyi bayan ka,amma fa kar Daddyn Sheik yace nayi sonkai.”
Dariya gaba d’aya sukayi inda suka cigaba da firar su...



Bayan ta idar da salla ne take cewa NU’AIMA zata wuce gida.
NU’AIMA ce ta kalle ta sanan tace”amma what’s wrong naga duk kamar jikin ki yayi wani kala.”
“Uhm babu komai Aima duk damuwa ta akan rashin lafiyar kice amma yanzu naga kinji sauk’i sosai nagode Allah.”
“Nagode nagode sosai NI’IMATULLAH samun k’awa aminiya kamarki a wanan zamanin tabbas sai an tona nagode Sosai ubangiji ya biya miki buk’atunki Na Alkhairi.”
“Ameen ya Allah Ameen.” Babbar buk’atata a yanzu bai wuce had’uwa da iyayenaba ina jin kewar su sosai,sanan ina jin kamar wani abu da nake da alak’a dashi a kusa dani inaji a jikina kamar wani jinin yaa yawo yna giftawa tanan.....

“NINU lafiya naga kin tsaya kinyi shiru.”
Uhm bubu komai ni zan wuce.”
“Ok bari na rakaki.”
“Aa wallahi keda bakida lafiya barni na tafi mitana tana farfajiyar gidan nan fa.”
Dan Allah kibari na rakaki kinga na kwana biyu fa a kwance.”
Tam shikkenan tunda kin takura.”
Suna k’ara fitowa gabanta yana tsanan ta bugawa tana ji kamar zata rufe ido ta bud’e tayi arba da muhimmin abu mai muhimmanci and so close to her,tabbas tana jin abun amma ta rasa menene. Tana cikin tunanin taji muryar dabazata tab’a mantawa da itaba wacce ko amafarki tajita sai ta farka a razane ta d’ago da kanta zuwa inda taji sautin muryar ya daki dodon kunnenta inda sautin muryar yayi barazanar raba zuciyar ta da k’irjinta.......





ASTAGFURULLAH WA ATUBI’ILAIK.✍🏻
