SHAFI NA TALATIN DA BIYU 32

Yana ta safa da marwa a k’ofar bakin d’akin haihuwar gabad’aya ya rasa abin yi yana jin wani abu ya tokaresa a mak’ogaron sa kuma ya kasa fad’a masa,innailahi kawai yake ja saboda yaha irin tashin hankali da Rahma ke ciki anan haka wani likita yazo yace ai wanan saidai ayi mata aiki dan tana buk’atar aiki da gaggawa,sa hannun iyayen ta ko kuma mijin ta ake buk’ata..
Alhaji umar ne yabi likitan office d’insa yasa hannu domin kuwa iyayenta ba a kusa suke ba saboda su yan niger ne kuma a can cikin wani garin buzaye suke arlit,dan haka ba’a kusa suke ba,haka dai Yasa hannun aikuw cikin nasara akayi mata aiki aka ciro mata babu boy shine Abbah,a wanan haihuwar tata ce a kuma wanan lokacin sai yaji kamar an zare masa wani miki da yake damunsa ancire masa komai a ransa haka ya saki jiki ya fidda kud’in ya siyo mata abubuwa kala kala naci dana buk’ata a matsayin ta na mai jego sanan ya janyo NI’IMATULLAH a jikinsa,daman sometimes yana haka sai kuma lokaci d’aya ya dawo gidan jiya...

A ranar da Rahma ke asibiti tana nak’uda Atine tasan komai kishiyar ta,akwai wani mutum mutumi datake azabtar da Rahma dashi,shi ta d’aukko abu tamkar y’ar tsana haka ta ringa wasa dashi tana komar mata da d’a k’irji hardai daga baya da rok’an Allah sukayi nasara akan ta dukda basu sanmaike faruwa,Rahma tana da ilimin addini sosai kuma tana da tarbiyya sosai hakan yasa tun NI’IMATULLAH bata da wayo take karantar da ita addini tare da tarbiyya tun NI’IMATULLAH nada shekara goma ta haddace al’qur’ani mai girama haka wasu daga cikin hadisai ta aamu tarbiyya a wajen mahaifiyar ta daidai gwargwado,bawai iya karatun addinin ka zai kareka daga fad’awa sharrin masu shirka ba tabbas asiri gaskiyane kuma tunta yaci fiyayyen halitta babu wanda bazai kama ba,saidai idan Allah ya kub’utar dakai to ka kubuta idan kuma ya kasance yazo maka a zanen k’adaddarar ka to babu yanda zakayi ubangiji shi zai warware maka kuma duk wanda ya cutar dakai ubangiji bazai barsa ba...


Yau kwanan su biyar kenaan a asibitin saboda tasha mutuk’ar wahala ga kuna aiki shiyasa ba’a sallame suba ance harsai nan da sati biyu...

Zaune yake kusa da ita a gefen gadon ya rik’e yaron a hannu sanan NI’IMATULLAH na rungume a jikin sa,babu wanda yazo asibitin daga matarsa har y’ay’anta saidai yan uwansa duk sunzo sun dubata tunda a lokacin suna zaune a kaduna daman shi babansa asalin d’an kaduna ne...

“Dan Allah Rahma ki k’ara hak’uri dni nasankina yi sosai da sosai samun irin ki sai an tona sanan ki yafe min wallahi duk wasu abubuwan da nake miki bayin kaina bane Wallahi bansan ma inayi ba.”
“Babu komai Daddyn NI’IMATULLAH insha Allahu watarana sai labari zaizo ya wuce kamar ba’ayiba.”
“Ina jin kunyar ki sosai idan na duba naga abin da ke faruwa nakasance nakasa yi miki adalci,naje na d’aukkoki daga k’asarku daga garin ku daga unguwar ku daga dangin ki,na kawo ki nan baki da kowa sai Allah babu wanda kika sani amma nazo ina azabtar dake,wallahi muddin biki yafe minba Rahma sai Allah ya kamani.”
“Nikam wallahi na yafe maka Daddy na yafe maka duniya da lahira,insha Allah itama Atine zata bar abin da takeyi watarana insha Allah itama zata shiryu.”
“Bana tunanin haka Rahma,saboda atine tayi nisa kuma duk wanda yayi nisa baya jin kira,saidai ubangiji ya dawo da ita hayyacin ta idan mai shiryuwa ce Allah ka shiryeta idan kuma bazata shiryuba Allah kai kasan yanda zakai da ita.”
Ameen ya Allah suka amsa a tare sanan suka saka wata firar suka ci gaba....



Ranar da sati d’aya ya zagayo a ranar aka rad’awa yaro sunan Abban Daddyn dubai wato bana umar kenan yaro yaci sunan Abubukar shine suke kiransa da Abba,bak’aramin bak’in ciki tayi ba a lokacin datayi toxali da jaririn kishiyar tata,daman na miji ta haifa ni ban tab’a ganin mayya ba irin wanan matar ta rantse cewa sai ta haifi magaji a gidan nan sai ta haifi magajin dukiyar nan to wallahi duk tayi ta gama da ita da arzik’i sunyi hannun riga wallahi,saboda bazan tab’a bari ba indai ina raye har abada,gashi itama yarinyar nan tata naga ta taso da farin jini sosai duk dangi sai sonta ake dan haka nidasu ne.. ganin canzawar da yayi yasa hankali k’ara tashi dan haka ta d’ugunzuma gidan bokan ta...

Basusan yanda sukayi da bokan taba amma haka dai bayan Rahma taji sauk’i ta warke ta dawo gidan taci gaba da bauta kamar yadda ta saba saidai a wanan karan ba sosai take ba dan kuwa mijin nata ya sauya sosai abun ya sake sa dan haka ya tsaya a tsaye dan ganin ya kare darajar matar sa...
A kwana a tashi babu wahala inda Abbah yayi wata biyu da haihuwa anan ne Rahma Allah ya k’ara had’ata da cikin Abbas,kwatakwata bata zata ba dan kuwa bazato babu tsammani hakan ta faru kuma bata tab’a kawowa ciki garetaba ta d’auka yanayin tane ya sauya na idan tana shayarwa tana daina Al’ada kamar yadda tayi a haihuwar NI’IMATULLAH to ta d’auka hakan ne bawai ciki bane...

A wanan yanayin ne ya fahimci komai amma bai fad’awa kowa ba yayi shiru da bakinsa,duk yanayin da take shiga idan ta samu ciki shinta shiga amma saboda tsabar bata shi take ba sam batayi wani tunannin saba,dan kuwa hausawa sunce sarai daci shi yake kawo jin yinwa.....


Ta nemi alfamar akan dan Allahya kaita k’asar su tana so taje ta gano iyayen ta tunda tayi aure bata tab’a zuwa ba sai haihuwar ta tafari,tun daga nan kuma bata sake komawa ba...
Ganin tana da k’aramin ciki yasa yayi mta dabara akan tayi hak’uri akwai wata tafiya datake tunkarar sa yana son zai fara business a dubai dan haka kuma shi yana ganin ma da ita zai tafi amma insha Allah kafin su tafi zai san yanda z’ai ya kaitak’asar tasu...
Abin da yasa ya hanata zuwa a wanan lokacin saboda k’aramin cikin datake tare dashi,dan kuwa zai iya samun matsala saboda tafiya ce ba yar k’arama ba kuma garin yana da nisa sosai sai ta iya samun matsala ga kuma jariri datake dashi...


A kwana a tashi babu wahala a wajen Allah cikin Rahma ya shiga wata takwas cif cif,ta fito b’angaren ta tana kwallawa NI’IMATULLAH kira dan kuwa bata ganta ba tasan ta fita,kuma tasan ba inda zata illa wajen yan uwanta,yanzu Rahma data samu y’anci ita ko sashen Atine batayi dan kuwa haka mai gidan yace dole Atine ta yarda da yar aiki kotak’i ko taso matar sa ta daina mata bauta,wanan shine dalilin dayasa ko shashen nata bata zuwa..
Bata ankaraba tazo fita ta suke akan tiyels d’in sashen su Atine inda yaranta suka zubar damai ita kuma bata kula ba ya sulal da ita ta fad’i... wata k’ara ta saki sakamakon d’an cikita da yayi wani motsi mai k’arfi ya bigi cikin nata... adaidai lokacin Atine tayi arba da wanan cikin Na Rahma salati sosai tasa ita tund take a rayuwa bata tab’a ganin mutum kamar aljana ba sai Rahma,tunda aka aurota duk sanda zata ganta da ciki tofa sai ya tsufa zata gani dan haka tana fitowa ko kallon inda take batayi ba cikin d’imaucewa ta ziri gyalenta dake sak’ale akan igiyar farfajiyar gidan tayi waje...

Bata tsaya a ko ina ba sai gidan wani boka da aka kwatanta mata,tana zuwa zata shiga kenan ya daka mata ihu yace ta dakata,tsaya wa tayi cak babu alamar tsoro ko fargaba,dan kuwa zuciyar ta ta k’ek’ashe babu batun imani ko guda a tare da ita,haka yace”ki shigo da ashar.”babu musu ta shiga tanayi kamar yadda ya fad’a mata,bayan taje su suka san abin da sukayi ta dawo gida...


Dakyar ta tashi daga fad’uwar datayi ga bayan ta ya rik’e ya fara nata wata azaba gad’an cikin ta ya takure yak’i motsi ya dunk’ule a waje d’aya dakyar ta rarrafa ta koma sashen ta dan tabar jariri duk da cewa yanzu yayi wata har goma sha d’aya amma saboda bashi da saurin girma sai kace baifi wata tara ba,tana komawa ta tarar da NI’IMATULLAH ta shigo ta k’ofar kitchen,tana tayiwa Abba wasa yana nema yayi kuka ya rarrfa ya tashi tsaye yana ta gwaranci...

Waje ta samu ta zauna ta jingina da fillow ta d’auki waya ta kira mijin nata tare da sanar dashi abin da ke faruwa.
Cikin damuwa yace yanzu su tawo asibiti,tace”wallahi yaya baza mu iya zuwa asibitin nan ba cikina yana min wani iri sosai banana jin dad’i kawai ina jin wani yanayi na daban na shigata,dan Allah ka kula min da yarana.”
“Bangane ba dan Allah ki daina wanan zance kamar wacce wani abu zai sama dan Allah kibari.”
“Tam shikkenan yaya saika dawo d’in.”
Bayn yadawo ne sukaje asibiti aka bata magunguna tasha,taji dama dama amma still jikin ta yayi sanyi sosaikamar zata rasa wani nata take ji,tayi kewar gida da mahaifiyar ta da mahaifnta da yan uwan ta aure kenan ya nesan tata da gida. A wanan daren tayi kuka sosai duk da cewa ina yarinya amma ina fahimtar komai da komai dake faruwa da mahaifiyata a wanan daren ne kuma ta tashi,tana iwace,iwace. Haka suka kwana ana ririk’eta Daddy yana mata addu’a amma ina sai abu yaci gaba sosai,aikuwa kwana biyu dahaka ta tubere sai hauka tuburan......




ASTAGFURULLAH WA’ATUBI ILAIK.✍🏻
NI’IMATULLAH 



BY HAAJARA ABDUSSALAM ADAM ARZUK’A (ablas’glam)



ANNURI WRITERS ASSOCIATION💫
(Annuri is the best).

MARUBUTA MASU HAZAK’A DA HIKIMA DA AZANCIN MAGANA,ALLAH YA K’ARA D’AUKAKA ANNURI WRITERS,ANNURI WRITERS NA GODIYA DA KUMA JINJINAWA GAREKI YUSRA ABUBAKAR MARUBUCIYAR RUWA BIYU,ALLAH YA K’ARA MIKI BASIRA TARE DA YAN UWA NA CIKIN ANNURI WRITERS,SANAN JINJINA AGAREKI MATAR MAMMAN BABU DARE BA RANA KIN BADA GUDUN MAWARKI UBANGIJI YA K’ARA MIKI FARIN CIKI DA KWANCIYAR HANKALI A CIKIN RAYUWAR AURENKI ALLAH YA RAYA MIKI HANAN D’INKI.ANNURI WRITERS NA JINJINA MUKU.🥰😍😘❤️😍LOVE U ALL.



SHAFI NA TALATIN 30

Da gudu NI’IMATULLAH ta ruga daidai inda Daddyn naya yake ta zube a k’asa sumarmiya,cikin hanzari ya sunkuya yana jijjigata “NI’IMATULLAH,NI’IMATULLAH ki tashi kiyi farin ciki kinga Daddyn ki,ki tashi dan Allah kada ki tafi ki barni kamar yadda mahaifiyar ki ta tafi ta barni.” Abba ne cikin gaggawa ya janyesa sanan yace momy ta d’aukkota ta kawo ta palor,haka kuwa akayi d’aukkota tayi tare da taimakon INAYATULLAH inda suka shiga palor da ita ta ajjeta a kujera 3seater..
Ruwa aka samo aka yayyafa mata a take ta farfad’o,mustsuka idanuwan ta tafarayi sanan tafara k’ok’arin bud’esu dan kuwa sun mata nauyi sosai dakyar Allah ya bata ikon iya bud’esu aikuwa kan nata yana kallon saman P.O.P da aka k’awtashi a palorn,dakyar ta iya samu ta dawo hayyacin ta dan kuwa sosai take jin juyawa,juye juye tafara yi tana kallon kowa inda kowa yayi cirko cirko an rasa mai magana sai Daddyn dubai daya taso ya k’ara matsawa kusa da ita ganin ta farka,Paris kuwa kusan daskarewa yayi,har yanzu kwakwalwarsa tafasa fahimtar dashi abin da ke faruwa mai yake faruwa ne,maine had’in NI’IMATULLAH da mahaifina kuma I can’t believe what’s going on...
“NI’IMATULLAH.”Daddyn dubai ya fad’a yana kallon ta,dakyar ta iya tashi ta matso kusa dashi ta furta”Abbahna kaine dagaske kaine ko kuwa mafarki nake,na saba irin mafarkin nan marin kanta tafarayi Daddyn dubai ne ya kamo hannuta shima jikin sa rawa yake yace”nine NI’IMATULLAH nine tabbas nine a wanan karon ba mafarki kike yi ba,ina umman ki sanan ina Abbana yake,ya akayi da cikin jikin ta ta haihu ko kuma mai ya faru dan Allah kiyi gagawar kaini garesu NI’IMATULLAH,a kullum ina rok’an Allah kar ya kasheni har sai nagan ku keda mahaifiyar ki na rok’eku gafarar abubuwan da suka faru wallahi ba’a sanina komai ya faru ba NI’IMATULLAH kuma ba’a hayyacina ba,dan Allah kiyi gaggawar kaini ga ummanki inaso naganta inaso na nemi yafiyar ta kafin k’asa ta rufe min ido.”
Paris ne ganin kansa yana neman tarwatsewa gashi NI’IMATULLAH takasa magana sai kuka take sosai kamar ranta zai fita, matsowa yayi wajen Daddyn nasa ya dafashi tare da fad’in”wai Daddy maike faruwa ne kun barmu a duhu dan Allah ka daina wanan kukan Daddy ita d’in wanan wacece ita.”
“Wanan da kake ganin ta Paris yar uwar kace uwar ku d’aya uban ku d’aya kaine wanda mahaifiyar ta tafara haifa.”
Cikin hanzari ta mik’e tana kallon paris tana nuna sa d yatsa taya hakan zai faru”Abb..aa...ya..yaa.... gabad’aya hankali ko k’wakwalwa takasa tattaro kalaman da zatayi amfani dasu wajen magana dan gaba d’aya kwakwalwarta ta tsaya cak a wanan lokacin,kukan datake ya k’afe..
“Daddy ban gane ba..ban..gane..abin da kake nufi ba..NI’IMATULLAH..kuma k’anwata I’m even not get it,how could this be,can’t believe.”
Da dadyn su NU’AIMA ne da Abba Muhammad suka fara magana.
Daddyn NU’AIMA.” Assalamu alaikum warahmatullahi wa barakatuhu,hak’ik’a wanan abin al’ajabi ne sosai da sosai dan haka kowa ya kwantar da hankalinsa Paris ka zauna ka nutsu sanan kema NI’IMATULLAH ki zauna ki nutsu sai a tattauna komai yanda ya dace’Daddyn dubai plsss take it easy plsss.”
Abbah muhammad ne yace”Allah kenan,Allah abin godiya wato godiya ta tabbata ga Allah da ya nuna mana wanan rana,hak’ik’a aminina yayi rashin matar shi da y’arsa tare da jaririn yaro dabai fi shekara d’aya ba tare da tsohon ciki,a kullum dasu yake kwana dasu yake tashi ya kasa sanin inda zaiga matar sa da lokaci guda ciwo ya sameta ya tab’a kwakwalwarta har wasu suke kira data haukace a kullum Yana addu’a ubangiji ya kawo ranar da zai had’u dasu ya nemi yafiyar su dan tabbas yana ji a jikin sa cewa suna a raye basu mutu ba,sai gashi abin farin ciki yau Allah ya nuna masa yar’sa kuma farin cikinsa wacce a kullum yake kwana dasu yake tashi dan haka wanan abun babu abin da yakamata muyi illah godiya ga Allah subahanahu wata’ala da ya nuna mana wanan rana.”


Wanan abin ban al’ajabi da mamaki da yaww yake Allah kenan...

“Kimin magana NI’IMATULLAH ki min magana ina matata abar kaunata take sanan ina Abba yake kimin magana dan Allah yarinyata.”
Tashi tayi ta rungume mahaifin nata tana ta kuka mai tsuma zuciya...
“Nayi farin cikin ganin ka abbana nayi farin ciki mara misaltuwa,sanan nayi bak’in ciki amma nayi farin ciki sosai Allah ya amshi addu’a a ta kuma su Abba da k’anin sa Abbas suna can gidan da ake ruk’on mu acan,a kullum ina addu’ar ganin wanan rana daman nasan zaka dawo Abbana.”
“Tabbas NI’IMATULLAH nasan Allah bazai tab’a sani in gushe batare da nadawo garekuba shekaru watanni da kuma awanni sun wuce sun shud’e duk da burin ubangiji ya cika min burina ya barni koda kwanan k’arshene a duniya tare da ya nuna min ku,naakasa daina jin zafin abubuwan da suka faru taya zan kasance a kwanciyar hankali matata mafi soyuwa a gareni ta samu ciwan kwakwalwa a rana d’aya ta b’ace ita da yarinya wacce batafi shekara sha biyu ba da yaro d’an shekara d’aya da tsohon ciki,bayan duk kalar azabar datasha a wajen sakina taya zan iya kasancewa cikin jin dad’in rayuwa na rayu na manta da farin ciki,na tuna da farin ciki ne a lokacin da babban d’ana ya bayyana kuma shine yayan ki tabba Allah maji rok’an bawan sane tabbas,NI’IMATULLAH kiyi maza kiyi gaggawa ki kaini naga matata sanan naga yarana na rok’eta yafiya bansani ba ko zata iya yafe min....”


Kalaman Abbana nata ya k’ara sata a cikin matsanancin damuwa da kuka,ina ma a tau mahaifiyar ta tabayyana,ina na ace suna fita su had’u da ita,ina ma ace suna zuwa gidan malam su tarar ta dawo....

Momyce tace” ALHAMDULILLAHI RABBIL ALAMIN,daman shi Allah haka yake yakan tsarawa mutum rayuwa a duk inda yaso yakan tsarawa bayinsa k’adddara kala kala a rayuwa tabbas sakamakon wad’an da sukayi hak’uri suka rungumi k’addarar su to tabbas wad’anan saka makonsu a wajen Allah na mussamman ne,NI’IMATULLAH ki godewa Allah sann kaima Daddy ka godewa Allah da ya nuna muku junan ku cikin kwashin lafiya,Allah ya kiyaye gaba sanan Allah ya baku hakurin duk abubuwan da suka faru a baya..”
Sosai paris yake kuka kamar ba na miki ba yaudai kamar ba paris d’in da aka sani da ba,kuka yake cikin karayar zuciya tabbas sai yau ya tabbatar da wanan duniya ba komai bace,shi kansa yaga lissafin k’addara a rayuwar sa,Allah sarki NI’IMATULLAH ashe yar’uwar sace,shiyasa yake k’aunatar sosai yake jinta sosai a ransa,tashi yayi ya kamota ya rumeta itama rungumesa tayi tana kuka tana jin wani irin feeling yana sauka yana ratsa sassaa da n’argon na jikinta,ayau taji kamar a sauke mata wani b’agare na nauyi a jikinta,yau itace a kusa da mahaifinta sanan itace a kwance a k’irjin blood brother nata,takasa tantance irin tsantsar farin cikin datake ciki,ashe dai itama zata ga wanan rana...

NU’AIMA banda kuka babu abinda take tausayinsu gabad’aya ya cika mata zuciya Allah sarki,NI’IMATULLAH Allah sarki paris d’inta shiyasa yaji yana k’aunarta yaji yana sonta ashe yar uwar sace kuma jinin sa ce Allah sarki... Tafad’a tana ta kuka....


Daddyn su NU’AIMA ne yace yanzu su shirya suje can gidan malam d’in...
Da saurin Daddyn dubai yace”ehhh NI’IMATULLAH ki kaini can,ki kaini naganta....

Abbah ne yace to ku taso mu tafi gaba d’ayan ku.
Aikuwa ba musu su NA’EM duk aka taso aka fita farfajiyar gidan sanan uka kira driver shi kuma NA’EM yace zaija su Daddy....


Gaban NI’IMATULLAH da NU’AIMA sai fad’uwa yake itama momy kanta datasan cewa Maman NI’IMATULLAH bata nan ta shiga firgici ta yarda Daddyn dubai zai fuskanci cewa matar tasa bata nan ta sake b’ata......





SUBAHANALLAHI WABI HAMDIHI SUBAHANALLAHIL AZEEM.✍🏻
NI’IMATULLAH 




BY Haajara abla


ANNURI WRITERS ASSOCIATION 💫
(annuri is the best).



SHAFI NA TALATIN DA D’AYA 31

Tafiya suke a cikin motar kamar Daddyn dubai yasan bazaije yaga masoyiyarsa abar k’aunar saba,duk jikin sa ya mutu yayi shiru,shiba kuka ba shiba tagumi ba,ya rasa a wane hali ma yake ciki amma ko ba komai yasan yana cikin matsanan cin farin ciki sakamakon bayyanar ababan k’auanar sa wanda aduk bugawar numfashin sa yana bugawa ne tare da sunan su,kamar yadda itama a nata b’angaren tayi shiru banda kukan zuciya da bugawar zuciyar babu abin da ke faruwa da ita,duk da tasan tana farin ciki amma hakan bai hana bawa damuwa samun damar dira a wanan lokacin ba,saboda tana damuwa da b’atan mahaifiyarta a halin rashin lafiyar datake ciki.
Ko kwatancen gidan NI’IMATULLAH bata iya basuba,saboda bakin ta yayi nauyi sosai to da yake motar su Daddy ce agaba inda NA’EM yake ja,haka PARIS ya ringa masa kwatance sun bi har suka k’araso unguwar su NI’IMATULLAH data cika da k’ananan yara da almajirai,unguwa ce ta talakawa dan haka aka zubawa motocin biyu idanu ganin sunyi parking a daidai gidan Baba Malam,fitowa gaba d’aya sukayi malam ne ya taso ganin sun nufosa gakuma NI’IMATULLAH a gaba yasa duk jikin sa yayi sanyi kardai laifi ta aikata musu,amma yasan ita d’in ba ma’abociyar rigima bace ko tashin hankali dan haka ya matsa yana musu barka da zuwa..

Daddyn su NU’AIMA ne da Abba Muhammad suka iya ce masa”dan Allah malam ka gafarce mu,munzo kana tsaka da bawa d’alubai darasi amma idan bazaka samu damuwa ba,mu shiga koda daga tsakar gidan kane ai mana iso idan da dama sai mu shigo.”
“To shikkenan babu damuwa bari na shiga na fito sai ku shigo.”
Haka malam ya shiga yasa aka shimfid’a manya manyan tabarmu guda biyu inda suka cinya tsakar gidan harmaragowa sukayi aka nan nad’e gefen. Komawa yayi yayi musu jagora da kansa zuwa cikin gidan.
Da sallama suka shiga sanan suka zauana akan tabarmar gaba d’ayan su,gaiswa suka farayi wa junan su bayan gaba d’aya an gaisa ne Abba Muhammad yace matan gidan suma suzo dan ayi a gaban su...
Malam ya kirasu suka zauna suka gaisar dasu Daddy sanan kuma Abba Muhammad ya fara magana.”jama’a Assalamu alaikum warahmatullahi wa barakatuhu,bayan gaisuwa hak’ik’a malam yau munzo muku da wani babban al’amari,amma dafarko dai muna godiya wa Allah sanan muna godiya ga abin da kayi mana domin kuwa kai d’in tabbas kacika malami kuma nagari mungode abisa kyutar da Allah ya baka daga sama kuma ka amsa hannu biyu biyu sanan ka rik’esu bisa amana.”
Malam ne yace”babu komai ai shi d’a na kowa ne amma ban gane kuba sam NI’IMATULLAH wad’anan d’in su wane,amma kamar naso nagane wanan d’an saurayin.” Yafad’a yana nuna Paris.
Cikin kuka mai nuna matsanan cin farin ciki NI’IMATULLAH tace”Baba malam wanan d’in Abbanane baba nane mahaifina ne.” Cike da mamaki da d’unbin alhini baba malam da babah jummai suke salati tare da fad’in hak’ik’a godiya ta tabbat ga Allah Subahanallahu wata Allah. Malam ne ya fara magana”wato wanan shine mahaifin Ki NI’IMATULLAH Allah mungode maka Allah mungode maka daka amsa addu’ar mu a lokacin damune tsaka daneman amsrta Allah mungode ma... babah jummai cike da murna itama take wa Allah godiya...
Daddyn dubai ne ya d’ago kansa da runannun idanuwan sa da kukansa suka kasa rik’uwa dake d’auke da farin ciki yace”Malam nagode maka nagode maka Allah ya saka maka da Alkhairi babu abin da zance Sai godiya banda abin da zan saka maka ko na biyaka abin da kaimin,saidai na tayaka da addu’a sanan na rok’i Allah ya saka maka da mafificiyar aljnnar nana firdausi.. murja ma dan abin kunya sai wani janyo NI’IMATULLAH jiki take tana cewa wai ta tayata murnar bayyanar iyayenta itakam banda  shiru datayi tana kallon mahaifinta da d’an uwan ta jinin ta babu abin da take ita kad’ai tasan mai take rayawa a zuciyar ta...

Daddyn su NU’AIMA ne yace”dad’in dad’awa malam y’ata NU’AIMA aminiyar yarka itace zata auri SALMAN wanda yake wan NI’IMATULLAH ne uwar su d’aya uban su d’aya...”cike da mamaki da zunzurutun farin ciki Malam yake kallon NI’IMATULLAH ita kuwa kawai d’aga masa kai tayi alamar hakane wata kwallar farin ciki ta gangaro mata,nana fa aka sake saban babun farin ciki da godiya ga Allah...

Shikam Daddyn dubai sai dube dube yake a wanan d’an k’aramin gidan yanaso yaga ta inda masoyiyar sa zata b’ullo amma bai gani ba,kuma dai baiga alamar akwai wasu d’akuna ba banda wad’anan guda ukun da yagani kuma da alama babu mutum a ciki,bakinsa da yayi masa nauyi ya d’aga tare dafad’in”a gafarce ni malam nasan tana fushi dani bansan a wane irin yanayi take ba yanzu amma dai nasan koma wane kalane tana fushi dani,amma dan Allah malam a bata hakuri a rarrasheta dan Allah ta fito ta ganni tace ta yafemin,ko a wane hali take ciki matata matatace zan rungumeta sanan zan rungumi k’addarar ta,dan Allah kar tayi fushi da mijin ta kuma uban y’ay’an ta...

Tun zuwan su Paris baiyi magana ba sai hawayen da yake sharewa duk da irin jarumtar sa amma a yau a wanan lokacin zuciyar sa ta raurawa sosai,bud’e bakinsa yay cikin sanyi murya yafara magana”nakasa ko nagaza yafewa wacce tayi min haka ta rabani da mahaifiyata a lokacin datake tsaka da son ganin abin da ta haifa abin da ta d’aukesa wata tara a cikin ta,kullum burin ta bai wuce ya fito duniya suyi ido biyu dashi ba amma an rabasu,sanan ta masa sanadiyar maman sa abar alfaharin sa wacce takasance masa uwa mabada mama wacce take kaunarta fiye da rayuwa duk a dalilin sharrin kishiyar uwa ya rasa wad’anan farin ciki,amma da yake Annabi(S.A.W.)yace inna ma’al usri yusra,duk tsanani yana tare da sauk’i sai gashi yau Allah ya kawo masa sauk’i ya had’a sa da farin cikin na had’uwada yan uwan sa jininsa yana burin yana marmarin had’uwa da mahaifiyar sa yana kewarta sosai,ina kewarta Malam kamar yadda na rayu tsawan watanni a cikin ta tana kewar gani na nima haka nake kewar ganin ta a yanzu dan Allah malam kace ta fito muganta...
Kuka ta fashe dashi sosai da sosai kamar  an aiko mata da mutuwa cike da sheshek’a......



Sanyi ake sosai da sosai agarin gashi yau tun safe ta tashi da wankin gaba d’aya yaran gidan kai harma dana kishiyar tata dana mijin,duk da cewa Allah ya rufa masa asiri dan yana jerin masu rufin asiri da za’a iya lissafawa dashi a unguwar yana da gida wan da yaje fegi d’aya ne kuma tsarin mata biyu ne a gidan,anan Rahma ta kasance a b’angare daban inda uwar gidan tama take a b’angare daban tun tasowawata a gidan nake fuskantar rayuwa Allah yabawa mahaifina Alhaji Umar rufin asiri daidai gwargwado saidai mahaifiyata Rahma tana fuskanta matsi da kuma rashin kyutatawa irin Na kishiya duk da cewa kishiyar ta gama da mahaifina sai ya zamana komai mahaifiyatace take yinsa na d’awainiya da gidan da wahalhalun gidan,yana ajje mana abinci suttura da makaranta amma abin haushin fa dole sai wanan kishiyar maman nawa ta yarda sanan zanci abinci zan saka kaya masu d’an dama dama ko kuma zuwa makaranta..
Kallon mamana nayi cikin yanayin sanyi gashi ta yini tana ta turk’ar wanki ga tsohon ciki,tunda ta kyalla ido taga cikin shikkenan take gana mata azaba tayau daban ta gobe daban agaban mijin za’ai komai amma ta tafasa bazai iya cewa ba saboda kuwa an tsaface sa an shanye sa...
“Dan Allah mama kibar wankin nan haka ki kawo nak’arasa miki.”Murmushi tayi mai kama dana yak’e tace”barshi kawai NI’IMA ke yarinya ce bazaki iya wanan wanki ba ai yafi k’arfin ki..” haka nayi nayi tabani wankin na amma tak’i bani harsai da ta kammala bayan magrif sana ta sharce wajen,dakyar take takawa k’afafuwan nan sun kunbura sunyi sumtum abin abin tausayi,mik’eww daga durk’uson datayi na sharce wajen datayi wanki take son yi amma hakan ya gagara k’afa ta rik’e baya ya rik’e bata anakara ba sai jinta tayi a k’asa daga nan bata k’ara sanin inda kanta yaje ba sai farkawa tayi ta ganta a d’akin haihuwaa...

Bud’e idonta tayi dakyar taga abubuwa sun jujjuya mata sunayi mata biyu biyu dakyar ta samu ta bud’e su tarwai ta ganta a saman gado ga kuma wta nurse agabanta,kafin tayi wani tunani taji nurse d’in tace”Alhmdlh tadawo hayyacin ta,ki daure kiyi nishi sosai fush off plss.”
Wani bacci ne yake san k’ara d’aukarta  haka nurse d’in ta ringa tasarta,wani uban ciwo ne ya tsarga mata daga k’afarta zuwa kanta da bayan ta ya dawo k’asan marar ta ya tsaya ya tokare mata,wani salati ta saki d’an cikin nata yana sake hautsunawa yana turo ta sai kuma sam yake yi..

Hankalin ma’ai kaciyar kanta saida ya tashi ganin yadda Rahma ke naku’da tamkar dai baza tai rai ba dan ta fidda tsammani dan kuwa daga d’anytawo ganga ganga sai kuma aga yayi zillo ya koma saman k’irjin ta.....

DAN ALLAH FRIENDS AND SISTERS INA NEMAN BARAR ADDU’AR KU,DAN ALLAH KUSANI A CIKIN ADDU’OINKU SISTER DAN ALLAH😭🤲🏻



SHAFI NA ASHIRIN DA TAKWAS 28

“Amma wanan wace irin magana kake yi Paris,kasani dani dakai babu aure har abada koda da soyayya tofa babu aure bazan tab’a auren mijin k’awata ba koda kuwa zan mutu banyi aure ba kuma ina mai jan kunnen ka da gargad’i agareka kada ka sake ko da wasa ka fad’awa wani wanan maganar,idan kuwa kayi kuskuren sanar da wani wanan maganar wallahi sai kayi danasanin sanina da kayi Na fad’a maka. Tana gama fad’ar haka ta fita a motar ta koma gida tace Babah ta fito su tafi,babu musu Babah ta fito ta shirya inda NI’IMATULLAH ta d’aukko motar ta suka shiga suka nufi asibitin..

Suna zuwa a harabar asibitin tayi parking d’in motar sanan suka fita suka nufi room d’in da NU’AIMA take,bayan sunje taga bataga alamar su momy ba ta le’ka ta window taga babu NU’AIMA sai ta ciro wayar ta tafara kiran INAYATULLAH.
Direct tayi mata kwatancen inda aka komar dasu,aikuwa kamar yadda ta kwatanta mata kamar haka suka nufi room d’in su momy duk suna zazzaune akan kujera inda ita kuma NU’AIMA tana kwance a kan gadon suna ta fira tana kallon kowa da kowa amma batayi magana ba ganin NI’IMATULLAH ne yace NU’AIMA yin murmushi sanan NI’IMATULLAH tafara gaisar dasu momy sanan ta matsa kusa da ita..
Babah ce tace”ina yini hajiya ina gajiya,ya kuma jikin wanan y’a.”
“Lafiya Kalau Alhmdlh mama,gashi nan jiki da sauk’i sosai Alhmdlh.”
“To ubangiji ya k’ara sauk’i yasa kaffarane..
Haka sukayi shiru tunda ba sabawa aukayi da juna ba...

NU’AIMA ce ta kama hannun NI’IMATULLAH ta rik’e gam. NI’IMATULLAH ta kalleta tare da cewa”ya jikin Ki NINU ina fatan yna miki sauk’i sosai.”
“Da sauk’i sosai rabin raina.”
“Allah ubangiji yabaki lafiya mai d’aurewa ya k’ara sauk’i yasa kaffarace.”
Lumshe ido tayi sanan ta bud’e tare da fad’in”ameen NINU an bani labarin yadda jiya kikak’i tafiya konan dacan duk da kema d’in ba lafiya garekiba Allah ya bar mana zumuncin mu nagode da.... kafin tak’arasa tasa hannu ta rufe mata baki tare da fad’in “kiyi shiri NINU kinsani ba’a buk’atar mara lafiya ya ringa magana sosai kamar haka.”
“Uhm rabin raina kenan ai naji sauk’i.”
Tana fad’ar haka daidai lokacin da Paris yayi sallama ya shigo,gaisar da su momy yayi inda momy tacewa Babah su koma waje dan sufi gaisawa dakyu haka suka fita sukabar d’akin inda NU’AIMA ta juya bayan ta daga yanda take ya kom bayan ta kawai Paris yakeiya gani..
Hawaye ne yake bin kumatunta yan wanke mat fuska...
Sallama yayi amma babu wanda ya amsa masa,saboda bakin NI’IMATULLAH yayi nauyi takasa magana balle kuma NU’AIMA datake kuka sosai...
“Innahlillahi maike faruwa NU’AIMA mai yasameki dan Allah kibar kukan nan kinsan yana yin condition da kike ciki karki kuka plssss.”
Share hawayen ta tayi duk da cewa wani yana bin wani sanan tace”maine ya kawo ka nan Salman,nasan cewa baka sona amma kuma nake k’aryata zuciya ta,amma tundaga lokacin da naji da kunne na na gasgata abin da zuciya take fad’amin,wallahi Salman na yarda ka auri NI’IMATULLAH indai hakan zaisa kuyi farin ciki....
“NI’IMATULLAH ce ta katseta da,karki k’ara fad’ar haka aima ni babu wata soyayya tsakanina da Paris ki sani,kuma ni bazan tab’a auren saba wallahi har abada.”
“Idan ke bakya sanshi,aishi yana sonki yanzu bazaki iyayi danni ba.”
“Dan Allah bana son wanan maganar Aima ki daina fad’ar haka kada kibari wani yaji na rok’eki.”
Salman ne yace”NU’AIMA baki da lafiya dan Allah kibar wanan maganar sanan ni bantab’a k’inki ko sau d’aya a rayuwata ba ina sonki sosai da sosai hasali kece wacce nafara so duk cikin y’an mata dan haka ina mai tabbatar miki da nid’in masoyin kine sanan ina mai baki hak’urin abin da ya faru.”
“Mai yasa zaka bani hakuri Salman Abin da yakamata ne kakeyi ni banga laifin kaba.”
“Ki daina maganar nan dan Allah.”
NI’IMATULLAH ce tace”wallahi Aima indai har bazaki shiru da wanan maganar ba to zan tafi kuma zan tabbatar miki da idan natafi bazaki k’ara ganina ba har abada dan haka indai kinason ki daina ganina kici gaba da fad’ar duk abin da kikaga dama.”
K’ara rik’e hannunta tayi sosai sanan tace”babu inda zaki muna tare har abada... Tafad’a hawaye yana zubadaga idonta sosai suka rarrasheta inda NI’IMATULLAH take ta goge mata hawayen nata da tissue...
Paris dabai fiye san damuwa tayi masayawa ba dan yanzu sai ya fita a hayyacinsa yasa yayi musu sallama ya tafi..

Juyowa NU’AIMA tayi ta kalli NI’IMATULLAH tare da cewa”dagaske Salman yana sona Rabin raina kina ganin idan na auresa banyi kuskureba.”
“Tabbas hakane Aima yana sonki sosai da sosai,idan kika auresa bazaki tab’a kuskure ba,kuma insha Allah zai kula dake sosai da sosai nasan da haka.”
“Tam shikkenan Rabin raina indai zai kula dani har haka to ina so kimin wata alfarma guda d’aya.”
“Ina jinki wace irin Alfarma ce wanan,ki nemi duk abin da kike nema a wajena babu abin da bazan miki ba NU’AIMA indai har ina da dama.”
“Kina da dama sosai.”
“Wanan wace irin dama ce.”
“Uhm inaso ki auri paris indai har zai kula dani kamar haka to na tabba zai linka kulawar da zai min wajen yi miki ita.”
Wani gaban NI’IMATULLAH ne yayi mummunar fad’uwa ta dafesa tace”Na fad’a miki kada ki sake min kalar wanan zance,wallahi NU’AIMA inda zaki ringa min wanan kalar zancen zaki nemeni ki rasa kuma bazaki sake ganinaba inafad’a miki wanan maganar da gaske bada wasa ba.”
Ajiyar zuciya tayi sanan ta rufe ido batare data sake cewa komai ba..
Blanket d’in ta jawo ta rufeta dashi sanan tace da ita”ki kwanta kiyi bacci mai dad’i kinji sai na dawo zankai baba gida.”
Murmushi tayi mata sanan ta fita suka sake gaisawa da momy sanan tayi mata sallama momy ce tacewa NI’IMATULLAH ta zauna a gida fa basai tadawo ba tunda gashi har magrif tayi,NI’IMATULLAH badan taso ba tacewa momy ta shikkenan bazata dawo ba insha Allah sai gobe haka suka yi sallama suka tafi..


Bayan ya koma gida ne yayi wanka ya shirya tsaf abbansa ya kirasa ya fad’a masa ya shirya nan da awa d’aya yaje ya d’aukkosa a filin jirgi,yayi mamaki sosai yace”wai daddy yau ka tawo kenan na d’auka ai sai gobe.”
“A’a son fasa wa nayi kaganni tare da Abbanka dan ya biyoni dan yaji ya ake ciki a game da shirye shiryen auren naka.”
“Uhm tam shikkenan daddy sai kun dawo ubangiji ya kawo ku lafiya.”
Bayan ya shirya tsaf ya fad’awa momy ya tafi ya tawo da daddy nan Zaituna tace ita sai ta bishi da har yace ta zauna bazai je da ita ba sai kuma yace ta tawo su tafi...
Ba abu bane mai sauk’i ace ka manta da wanda ka d’auka a matsayin mahaifin ka dan haka ganin Abban nasa dayayi yayi mutuk’ar dad’ad’a masa,wata kewarsa ce ta daban take shigarsa dan haka yayi huging nasa sosai tare da cewa”i miss u so much my lovely dad.”
“Missing u too my son.”
Sannu da zuwa yayiwa daddyn dubai.
Daddyn dubai yace”lallai naga yan ubanci shine ni baza’ai hugging d’in nawaba sai ace min wani welcome.”
“Ohhh sorry daddy come close.”
Dariya sukayi sanan suka shiga mota yana wa zaituna magana itama tana ta cewa tayi kewar sa,a mota ne Abba yake cewa”son ya jikin amaryar taka,naji labarin she’s not feeling fine,har yakai ta kwanta asibiti.”
“Eh wallahi Abba tana fama da jiki sosai.”
“Ohhh God ubangiji ya bata lafiya.”
“Ameen daddyn dubai Yafad’a sanan yace”wallahi shiyasa duk jikina yayi sanyi naji dole inaso naje na dubata dan haka insha Allah gobe zamuje ka kaimu hospital d’in.”
Shikkenan daddy Allah ya kaimu..

Haka suka je gida ana ta murnar dawowar su..


Yaukam tunda ta kwanta bacci gabanta yake ta fad’uwa ta rasa dalili amma sai ta alak’anta hakan akan maganganun da sukayi da Aima,amma har gari ya waye bat dainajin fad’uwar gaba ba dan haka saitafara karanta,la’ilaha’illah anta subahanaka inni kuntu minazzalumin..
Acikin zuciyar ta tana addu’a Allah dai yasa ba wani abu bane zai samu NU’AIMA kar taje ta mutu fa wayyo....

Aikuwa ta kasa zaune ta kasa tsaye aikuwa tun safen ta shirya dan zuwa asibitin.....

SHAFI NA TALATIN DA SHIDA 36

Bayan malam musan sun fita ST fajiyar gidan ne suka fara tattaunawa dasu Abba,inda daga k’arshe suka yanke shawara akan abin da ya dace...


Bayan tasha bacci sosai mai had’e da wasu har gitsatsun mafarki ne ta farka tana farkawa ta tashi tafara tafiya a cikin d’akin idonta yana a rufe,tana kiran NU’AIMA,NU’AIMA idonta a rufe yake dan haka bata iya bud’e kofar ba.
Kamar daga sama NU’AIMA tazo wucewa bakin d’akin saboda zata d’auki abu,tanason komawa gida,taji NI’IMATULLAH tana ta kiran sunan ta ai da sauri ta sauka k’asa cikin tsoro gashi duk su yayan nata sun tafi ganin Paris tayi a zaune a farfajiyar gidan su Abba da Abbas sai surutu suke tayi masa amma ina hankalinsa baya wajen. Cikin hanzari tafad’a jikinsa tare da cewa”luv ni take kira gatacen ta farka tana kirana.”
Da sauri ya tashi yana fad’in”really,to muje mana.”
Komawa bayan sa tayi ta lab’e tare da cewa”wallahi luv ni tsoro nakeji.”
“Kidaina jin taoho she’s your sister karkiji tsoron ranta and even your beloved besty. Jikin tane yayi sanyi sosai haka yasa ta bishi jiki a sanyayye suka nufi upstair d’in.
Da sallama ya murd’a handle d’in k’ofar ya shiga rike da hannun NU’AIMA datake tason komawa baya,Allah sarki NI’IMATULLAH har ta koma ta zauna a saman gadon tana hawaye..
NU’AIMA ce ta matsa kusa da ita tayi hugging nata. NI’IMATULLAH ce tace”Aima tsorona kikeji ko.”
Rufe mata baki tayi da tafin hannunta sanan ta cigaba da hawaye. Paris ne yace masu ya isa,yanzu sudai fatan su bai wuce ace NI’IMATULLAH ta samu lafiya mai d’aureww ba.


Bayan sun koma gida,Abba yayi mutuk’ar tausayawa yanda NI’IMATULLAH take cikin wanan ma wuyacin hali,haka suka sauna gaba d’aya a palor suna tattaunawa sosai INAYATULLAH taji tausayin NI’IMATULLAH d’in daman ita NU’AIMA tana cen amma gobe zata dawo gida,Abba ne ya dubi NA’EM sanan yace”NA’EM nasan d’ane kai mai biyayya duk abin da nace kayi kana amsa min,inason na tambaye ka cewa akwai wacce kakeso zaka aura ina nufin wadda kake soyayya da ita.”
K’asa yayi da kansa cikin girmamawa yace Daddy ni bana kula yan mata ai,dayawa suna sona amma har yanzu banga wacce ta dace dani ba.”
“Masha Allah,yanzu ina fatan idan na maka zab’i ka karb’esa hannu biyu biyu.”
“Insha Allah.”
“Inaso za’a d’aura maka aure tare da yan uwanka sanan zan maka komai da komai da uba yake wa d’ansa and ka zab’i gidan da zaku zauna,sanan zansa a had’a mata laife a dubai insha Allah.”
Batare da NA’EM ya tambayi cewa ko wacece wacce Daddy zai aura masa ba ya tashi yabar wajen,yasan dai duk zab’in da Daddy zai masa to tabbas nagari zaiyi masa,dan haka shi baya buk’atar ma sanin ko wacece kuma insha Allah koda musaka ce Daddy ya aura masa zai rungumeta hannu biyu biyu zai yi masa biyayyya saboda shi Assli ma mai biyayya ne..



Daddy ne yakira Daddyn dubai ya fad’a masa cewa NA’EM ya amince da zab’insa kuma daman yasan cewa zai amince saidai shima yanaso Daddyn dubai yasa malam ko kuma shi da kansa ya fad’awa NI’IMATULLAH saboda kar a shiga hakk’inta.
Ya amsa masa da insha Allah zai fad’a mata ko yasa malam d’in...


Bayan sun gamawa waya daddy bai zarce ko ina ba sai gidan su NU’AIMA daman sunyi waya da malam akan zasu had’u,bayan ya shiga sun zauna sun gaggaisa ya fad’a masa abin da yake tafe dashi kuma yanaso Baba malam ya fad’a mata saboda shine Ya raineta ya kula da rayuwarta har takawo yanzu. Baba malam ne yace yayi komai ne dan Allah kuma babu damuwa idan shi Daddyn dubai ya fad’a mata da kansa saboda yar sace...

Sawa yayu aka kirata ta saukko tana murna taga dadddyn ta gaishe dashi tayi sosai sanan ya fara magana kamar haka”NI’IMATULLAH ina neman Alfarma a wajen ki?”
D’ago da kanta tayi a hankali sanan tace Daddy wanan kuwa wace irin Alfarma ce,babu alfarma a tsakanin mu ka tambayeni komai kace na maka Daddy a shirye nake da nayi maka shi.”
“Nagode Allah yayi mikk albarka.”
Ameen suka amsa ita da baba malm
“Daman ba komai bane illa inaso Ki fad’a min kina da wanda kike so.”
“A’a Daddy.”
“Alhmdlh idan bazaki damu ba inason zan d’auara miki aure ranar lahadi mai zuwa.”
“Aure kuma Daddy ranar lahadi fa mai zuwa bikin NU’AIMA Daddy da’an d’aga.”
“Sai ayi taron tare mana ai bikin yayenkine daman so nake na had’e.... yana magana yaga kalar idon NI’IMATULLAH ya canza sosai,yayi wani jaa sai kuwa ta murtuke fuska tare da fad’in”kai wanene da zaka aurar da matata.”
Baba malam ne yace”kai wanene mara kunya.”
Ana cikin haka kamar daga sama saiga malam musa”kai wanene da bazaka fita daga jikin wanan baiwar Allahr ba kabarta ta huta.”
“Saboda ni masoyintane kuma nine mijinta.”
“To indai kai masoyintane yakamata kayi mata adalci ka fita ka barta ita mutum ce kai aljanini ne.”
“Eh nasani amma bazan iya barin matataba.”
“Kasanni nasanka karan mu dakai babu dad’i.”
Aikuwa yayu shiru baice komai ba,ganin haka sai malam Babba ya fara karatu ai kuwa caraf aljanin ya cafe ya cigaba,bayan yakai Aya ya kyalkyale da dariya.”
Malam musa ne yacewa Baba malam bari kaga,wani ruwa ya d’aukko ya watsa mata sai ihu sosai tafarayi sanan tayi lakwas...


Juyowa malam musa yayi ya kalli Daddyn dubai sanan yace”Alhji ba’a buk’atar a ringa fad’a mat za’ai mata aure,a shawarce,kawai a d’aura auren batare da an sake fad’a mata ba,idan yaso idan aka gama aikin zuwa wata shida idan komai yayu normal idan batasonsa ai kar ayi musu dole sai a rabasu,amma yanzu wanan aure ne na gaggawa dan haka dole sai anyishi.”
“To Malam mungode Allh ya saka da alkhairi insha Allah za’ayi komai kamar yadda kace.”

SHAFI NA TALATIN DA BIYAR 35


Washe gari kamar yadda akayi al’qawari haka da Malam Musa haka ya k’araso kano shida sabir cikin yardar Allah,ya samu tarba ta mussaman sosaia su Daddy suka karramasa daman su haka al’adar su take basu da wulakanci dan sun kasance Allah yayi musu dukiya sab’anin wasu masu kud’i,wad’an basu sani maba suna kartramawa bakle kuma wanan.

Kwanan sa d’aya a masaukin da aka bashi duk da cewa su NI’IMATULLAH da NU’AIMA duk basu san da wanan abubuwan ba,dan yanzu haka ma NU’AIMA tana gidan su NI’IMATULLAH sakamakon daman acen take kwana tunda suka tare gashi biki yana ta k’aratowa dan kuwa saura kwana bakwai,suna zaune suna fira NU’AIMA takirawa waya akan cewa akawo musu d’inkunanan su da suka kai,bayan ta gama wayar ne ta kalli NI’IMATULLAH tace”rabin raina anya bazamu basa event ba,kinga bani da lafiya sosai.”
“Baza’ayi haka ba rabin raina ko ba’ai dinner ba plsss ayi kamu nd mother’s eve,kinga fa yanzu kin murmure babu wanda zaice ma kin kwanta wani ciwo.”
“To shikkenan rabin raina yanda kikace haka za’ayi.”
Mai gyaran jiki ce ta k’araso,inda NI’IMATULLAH ta kyalkyake da dariya tana fad’in”wanan amarya wanan amarya,ace gyaran jikin ma bai samu isasshen lokacin saba sai yanzu.”
NU’AIMA ce ta zumb’uro baki tace”shi Daddy ya dage sai anyi bikin baza’a d’aga ba to Allah ana gama bikin zan wuce garin su momy dan Wallahi nima sai an min gyaran da akewa kowa a fam d’in mu sanan zan dawo.”
“Hhhhhhh sorry rabin raina to amma dai da yayana zaku tafi dan wallahi nima na wallace bazaki barmin d’an uwa yana gararanba shi kad’ai a titiba.”
“Ikon Allah Wayyo kai rabin raina kin bandariya sosai tabb to in tafi dashi yayu min mai,Allah babu inda zani dashi.”
“Hhhhh to shikkenan mu zuba mu gani,ki taso mu sauka kafin wanan surutun naki yayi yawa kisa ta koma.”
Murmushi tayi sosai ta sakko daga kan gadon tana tunanin ikon na Allah wai NI’IMATULLAH ce zata kasance k’anwar mijinta k’anwa ma ta jini wacce suke uwa d’aya uba d’aya...


Malam musa ne yace yana so yaga NI’IMATULLAH batare da b’ata lokaci ba Daddyn dubai yayi mata waya yace tanaga gida ne ko gidan su NU’AIMA,ta fad’a masa tana gida dan NU’AIMA ma ai tana nan,yace to shikkenan gashi nan zuwa da bak’o,tace Allah ya kawo su lafiya...


Anacan anawa NU’AIMA gyaran jiki ita kuma ta shiga kitchen tana shiryawa Daddyn dubai da bak’on da yace zasu zo dashi abinci...

Cikin hanzari ta kammala komai ta shiga tayi wanka,zata fito kenan daga d’akin taji gabanta ya yanke ya fad’i taji k’irjinta yana bugawa kanta ya fara juyamata ta fara gani biyu biyu,da hanzari ta k’ara bakin bed d’in ta kwanta dan kuwa idon ta har ya rufe.
NU’AIMA ce taga su Daddyn dubai da kuma Daddyn ta a wanan karon Abba Muhammad baya nan,sai kuma wani bak’o wanda batasan kowa ne bane saidai shima d’in bazai wuce abokin su daddyn taba. Sannu da zuwa tayi musu sanan takoma ta shiga kitchen ta d’auka ma NI’IMATULLAH tana can d’in ganin bata nan yasa ta d’aukko musu abinci da abin sha da NI’IMATULLAH tayi ta kawo musu,Daddyn dubai ne yace”sannu yarinyata Allah yayi miki albarka.”
Ameen ya Allah ta fad’a kanta yana k’asa. Daddyn sune yace mata ina NI’IMATULLAH?”
“Daddy tana sama tana shiryawa.”
“Ok kirawota.”
“Shikkenan Daddy.” Tafad’a tana haurawa sama.
Tana shiga d’akin tayi turus sakamakon ganin NI’IMATULLAH da wata irin kwanciyar da bata saba ganin tanayintaba ga towel na jikin ta duk ya jik’e kuma bata cireba,da sauri ta k’arasa tana kiran sunan ta tare da ruk’e hannunta.amma ina shiru tayi tayi tayi magana amma batayi magana ba kuma idonta a rufe yake,ganin haka yasa tayi maza ta d’aukko hijab ta samata sanan ta sake ruk’o hannunta sai taji kamar ba hannun mutum ta tab’a ba cikin hanzari da tashin hankali ta sake kallon NI’IMATULLAH,wata mahaukaciyar k’ara NI’IMATULLAH ta saki wacce saida dodon kunnen NU’AIMA ya kusa dod’ewa sanan tace cikin muryar da batata ba”Ki fad’a masa yabar gidan nan yabar gidan nan idan bahaka ba saina k’ona gidan nan kuma na kashe shi.”
Cikin rawar jiki da gudu NU’AIMA tayi baya ta saukko daga benen tana tuntub’e tana nishi,tabbas sunji ihun NI’IMATULLAH kuma sunji abin da take fad’a.”
Daddyn dubai ne ya rik’e NU’AIMA yace ta kwantar da hankalinta daidai kuma lokacin su Babah jummai da malam da murja suka shigo,kowa tsayawa yayi ya cirko cirko ga NI’IMATULLAH asama tana ihu da kururuwa kamar zakanyar datayi kwana ki a daji bata ciba...

Baba malam ne cikin hanzari shida su Daddy amma banda malam musa suka haura sama.
Haka suka shiga suka tarar da ita tana ta ihuuuu ta yaye hijab d’in da aka sa mata da baya suka koma inda Daddyn ta ya nufeta da nufin ya gyra mata hijabin nata,aikuwa batasan sadda tayi wancakali da shifa ya koma gefe.
Baba malam ne yanufi wajen shima amma shima ya kasa komai dan shifa ta wancekalar dashi haka a Daddy yayi nufin yaje shima haka,ganin haka su babah da NU’AIMA suka rufe d’akin da sauri suka barta ita kad’ai tana ta kurari tana yink’urin tashi da kalar wata tafiya kamar ta giwaye,cikin kuka ta d’auki waya ta kira Paris da Yayanta suzo dan ana cikin tashin hankali...

Malam musa ne ya zauna yayi mata topi a wani sulba sanan yace saidai a samu wanda zai iya shiga yaje har gabanta ya zuba mata a baki da kunnunwata sanan ya shafe mata a fuska,amma indai har tab’arar dashi bazasu lafa ba,nagane ifritun da yake kanta domin kuwa mun tab’a fafatawa dashi a wata ruk’iyya abin babu dad’i, malam musa yana cikin maganar NA’EM ya fara shigowa,yana shigowa yaga kowa yayi carko carko,Daddyn sane yace NA’EM gwada d’aukar ruwan addu’ar nan ka nufi NI’IMATULLAH tana sama d’aki na biyun k’arshe a kulle yake kayi hankali idan zaka iya ka shafe mata shi a jikinta da fuskarta,cos naga kamata rukiyya ranar nan may be kai zaka iya,amma sai kayi taka tsantsan sosai.
Batare da musawaba ya karb’i sulbar malam d’in sanan yacewa sabir ya rik’e masa wayar sa,ya amshi mukullin ya nufi d’akin.. ihuuu take sosai kamar kwanyar kansa zata fit saboda shid’in ba ma’abocin san hayaniya bane amma yau ya tinkari wani ihu wanda yake jinsa tamkar a wani gumurzun yak’i,tabbas banda NI’IMATULLAH ce kuma daddynsane ya sasa da ko za’ayi mai bazai tunkari wanan ihun ba,yana cikin tafiya yana sak’e sak’e ya k’araso k’ofar d’akin yasa mukullin ya bud’e yana shiga ya ganta babu hijab ga towel d’inma ya zame ai da sauri yayi baya,ya kwallawa NU’AIMA kira akan tazo amma NU’AIMA saboda a tsorace take ga tashin hankali yasa bata zoba ta k’ara shigewa bayan Daddyn ta saman ya sake yi mata tsayawa yace”dallah malama kizo kigani,yama kasa sake lek’a d’akin.
“Wallahi yaya bazan iya zuwa ba zata kasheni ne.”ana cikin haka saiga Paris ya shigo yana kiran maike faruwa cikin rawar murya NU’AIMA tace”NI’IMATULLAH ce tana sama tace sai ta k’ona gidan nan wai zafi yake mata.”
Malam musa ne yaja Daddyn suka fita daga palorn suka koma harabar gidan,aikuwa sai ihun NI’IMATULLAH ya fara yin k’asa.
NA’EM da yaga ai paris muharramin tane amma kuma shima bai kamata ya ganta a haka ba sai kuma ya sake cewa NU’AIMA”NU’AIMA wallahi ranki zaiyi mummunan b’aci nace kizo ki samata hijab ba kaya a jikin ta shine kike wani iskanci wallah sai nayi tafiyata.”
Paris ne yace dan Allah kije kisa mata luv.”
“Wallahi tsoro nakeji kada ta kasheni luv.”
“NI’IMATULLAH aminiyar kice kuma k’awarkive yar uwar kice ko zata cutar da kowa bazata cuceki ba.”
Jin wanan kalamai na paris yasa jikin NU’AIMA sanyi,aikuwa ta nufi inda yayan nata yake kiranta..
NU’AIMA tayu mamakin yanda ta k’arasa har wajen da NI’IMATULLAH take kukan nan nata amma batayi mata komai ba,haka ta saka mata hijabin ta kulle towel d’in sosai a k’irjinta,cikin rawar murya tace”yaya ka shigo,saboda tana jin tsoron kartayi mata wani abun,yana shigowa sai taga NI’IMATULLAH tana ja da baya,tana k’ara mak’alewa a jikin NU’AIMA,NU’AIMA batasan sadda tayi fitsari ba a wajen,NI’IMATULLAH ce tafara cewa”kada ki bari ya shigo tsoro yake bani dan Allah kayi hakuri na daina na daina.tafad’a tana kallon inda NA’EM yake sake tunkarota.
Dunkulewa tayi a waje d’aya ta saka kanta tsakani cinyoyinta ta farar rawar tsoro gani take tamkar zaki yana tunkarota,da hanzari ya k’araso yacewa NU’AIMA ta d’ago mai da fuskarta.”Wallahi yaya bazan iyaba kad’aga da kanka,Tafad’a tare da tashi ta ruga da gudu tabar d’akin.
Babu yanda zaiyi a haka ya d’agota a hankali yafara zuba mata ruwan tana ta nishi sosai kamar zakanya ta sake zabura aikuwa ya saki jiki ya ruk’oya sosai ya kwantar da ita kan k’irjinsa yana shafa mata ruwan har ta sulale ta fad’i a wajen...

D’agota yayi inda jikin ta yay babu nauyi ya d’auraka a saman gadon tsayawa yana kallon fuskarta,tasha wahala sosai ta bashi tausayi haka ya lullub’eta da blanket ya rufo k’ofar d’akin ya sakko k’asa.....




LA’ILAHA’ILLAHLAHU WAHDAWU LASHARIKALAHU,LAHUL MULKU WALAHUL HAMD WAHUWA ALA KULLU SHAI’IN K’ADIR.✍🏻
NI’IMATULLAH 



By haajara abla


A.W.A💫



SHAFI NA TALATIN DA HUD’U 34

Murmushi yayi mata wanda tajishi sosai har cikin zuciyar ta,sanan ya d’aga baki yace”ina tayaki murnar ganin d’an uwan ki tare da Abban ki.”
“Nagode sosai masoyina,amma saidai.”
“Nasani kina cikin alhinin tunawa da rayuwar mahaifiyar kiko,insha Allah itama nan bada jimawa ba za’a ganta na miki al’qawarin muddin tana doran duniyar nan ko a ina take insha Allah sai nadawo miki da ita.”cike da jin dad’in kalaman SAHEED d’in ta matsa tare da hugging d’insa sosai,kwanciya tayi lamo a k’irjin sa tana wani irin ajiyar zuciya batare datace komaiba.

Bayan duk wanan maganganun datake da Paris da NU’AIMA duk a gaban su takeyi,gabad’aya ita ta manta cewa da wani Paris ko NU’AIMA sakamakon ganin SAHEED ,NU’AIMA taji tsoro sosai,kuka take sosai k’asa k’asa,paris ne ya jawota jikinsa ya rungumeta sosai sanan yace taso muje.”
Fitowa sukayi tsakar gida a lokacin wajen k’arfe sha d’aya na dare hasken taurari sun haskake k’aramin tsakar gidan,NU’AIMA ce ta d’aga kanta ta kalli taurarin nan sosai gwanin burgewa da ban sha’awa sanan tace da Paris”Luv maike faruwa ne da NINU dan Allah.”
D’an yatsansa yakai saman labb’anta yayi mata da alama tayi ciru,cikin wani yanayi ya d’auke daga labb’atan ya kama hannunta tare da cewa”nakasance ina cikin farin ciki sanan ina jin shaukin had’uwa da tar uwata,amma a yanzu nagano akwai babbar matsala sosai domin kuwa sun bud’e mata ido.”
“Mai kace Luv su waye suka bud’e mata ido.” Kiyi shiru NU’AIMA insha Allah da safe zamu tattauna lamarin nan,and know i want get back to ur house.” And u know that it’s late Luv nd.”
“I’m sorry luv,but there is no way that i can sleephere.”
“Ohhh God,amma ai yayi dare,nd wa zai kaika gida.”
“Idon’t know gashi ana zafi u know bazan iya bacci ba,nd inaso na baki dama keda rabin raina kuma ku kwanta,kinga ai bai kamata mu kwana tare ba.”
“Hakane luv,amma ka kira yaya NA’EM mana sai ya kaika gida.”
Farar dabara lemme call him.
Batare da damuwa ba ko wani abu NA’EM yace gashi nan zuwa...
Komawa d’akin sukayi nan suka tarar da ita akan katifarta har tayi bacci...
Janyo blanket d’in yayi ya lullb’eta,”she weak at all.”Yafad’a yana kallon NU’AIMA.
Cikin muryar shagwaba NU’AIMA tace”yess she weak,wallahi ina tausayin yadda tayi gwagwar maya tun batason maine duniyar ba,inasonta Sosai luv,fatana Allah ya bata miji nagari wanda zai zame mata uwa da uba agareta.”Tafad’a tana goge hawaye. Matsawa yayi kusa yana goge mata hawayen nata tare dacewa”calm down please luv,aiyanzu muna tare da ita she’s not alone,dan haka muna tare da ita har abada ki daina damuwa...

Ba’afi ten10mints ba saiga NA’EM ya k’araso kiran Paris yayi,Paris yace to gashi nan fitowa,gud nyt yayiwa NU’AIMA sanan yace itama sai ta wanta zai tafi,haka kuwa akayi ta kwanta itama ya rufeta da blanket sanan ya gyarawa su Abbas kwanciya ya rufe musu d’akin yayi addu’a yayi sallama ya fita a gidan..



Bayan ya shiha motar NA’EM ne suka fara tafiya,daman shi NA’EM ba ma’abocin yawan magana bane amma da yaga shirun Na Paris ya fuskanci akwai matsala sai yayi gyaran murya ya dafasa tare da cewa”lafiya bro what going on,u look so worried,what’s the matter,I think to day is your day nd how u look like this.” Wata doguwar ajiyar zuciya yayi kafin yace”I’m excited to day,but same I’m really worried.”
“Worried for what?”
“For my sister NI’IMATULLAH she need help soon.”
“Help for what,plsss Salman go to stared to the point.”
“About those spirits,wallahi sun bud’e mata ido I’m told u akwai wani SAHEED a cikin su,ina ganin shine babban su sometimes inajin yadda take kiran sunan shi and nama tab’a ganin su tare,ya bud’e mata ido sosai ako da yaushe yana zuwa kuma kamar soyayya suke.”
“What subahanallah soyayya between spirit and,I don’t get it,tabbas NI’IMATULLAH tana neman taimako sosaida sosai,aikasan a wancen karan munyi magana da wani a jikin nata,amma babu damuwa insha Allah gobe zamu tattauna zamu fad’awa su Abba ma duk abin da yake faruwa za’asan abin yi.”
“Amma NA’EM banason Daddyn dubai yace zai d’aga hankalin sa ko ya shiga cikin damuwa saboda a wanan lokacin duk duniya babu wacce yake son gani cikin koshin lafiya kamar NI’IMATULLAH da kuma mu.”
“Insha Allah zan san yadda zan musu maganar sanan akwai wani abokina da mukayi school tare a madina,akwai Abbansa sunayin wanan maganin amma matsalar a jigawa yake amma duk dai ba matsala bace insha Allah,zataji sauk’i kuma za’a rabata dasu insha Allah.”
Allah ubangiji ya yarda...



Ana haka ya kaisa gida shi kuma y wuce gida...



Washe gari dayake malam Yafad’a musu duk abin da ta faru na sabon abubuwan arzikin da ya samu haka yace su shiga su fad’awa mak’ota yau zasu tashi wanda yake da dama sai yayu musu rakiya...



Aikuwa wajen la’asar gidan ya cika,su momy kuwa tuni sukayi can gidan suka sa aka dad’a wankesa da gyara komai sanan sukayi abinci sosai na alfarma na tarar bak’i. Alokacin akad’bi mutane a mota su babah jummai da yan uwanta su murja da ya’ya’anta kamar suyiwa NI’IMATULLAH sujjada haka sue ji,haka suka nufi sabon gida...
Gidan babbane sosai gashi ya had’u ya tsaru sama da k’asa ga kuma wani b’angare a gefe inda shine murja ta d’auka akabawa Babbah k’asa NI’IMATULLAH da k’annenta sama,dui da yayi musu yawa sosai amma sunce baza su takura musu ba,ga kuma guest room a gefe,gida dai ya had’u sosai gashi an wadatashi da shuke shuke abindai sai wanda yagani,ga kayan d’aki Na alfarma tamkar dai gidan amarya,amaryar ba ba tawasa ba....



Taro yayi taro kowa yana son barka ana musu Allah ya sanya Alkhairi kafin dare kowa ya tafi gida Amma banda su momy da Daddy ya kirata yace karsu tafi su zauna a gidan bayan isha’i zasu hallara za’a tattauna...

Bata tafi ba dan haka tsuka zauna har lokacin dasu Daddy suka k’araso....


Bayan anci ansha anyi sallah ne,malam ya bud’e taro da addu’a,bayan yayi ya gama ne kuma yafara godiya shida matansa akace babu damuwa yabar wanan godiyar,cewa akayi su tashi su shiga ciki matan sanan banda momy kada’ai...
Aikuwa tashi sukayi suka shiga ciki.
Paris ne yafara magana akan matsalar y’ar uwar tasa,bayan yakai aya malam ya fara bayani”tabbas nasan da ita,nayi iya bakin k’ok’arina dan ganin sun rabu da ita amma ina,kuma tunda nake da ita basu tab’a yin maganaba,kuma ina mata ruk’iyya shine babban abin da yae bani mamaki yake sani a wasiwasi.
Na’en ne yace”Allah gafarta malam ni sun min magana,munyi magana dasu,nan ya basu labarin yadda sukayi magana dashi anan momy ma tak’ara da cewa”tuna sanda NU’AIMA take wanan zargi Tafad’a min nakeso mu gana da ita amma Allah baiyi ba,amma yanzu mene mafita.”
Daddyn dubai shi kuwa harda kwallah yana ayyana maybe ma acikin daren da suka fita ne suka b’ata anan ta had’u dasu. Abba Muhammad ne ya fara masa nasiha sanan yayi shiru.
NA’EM bayan ya fad’a musu wanda yake tunanin zai iya mata magani,daddy ne yace ya kirasa yana son yayi magana dashi,aikuwa cikin nasara wayar ta shiga,bayan ya kirasa yafara masa bayani akan k’anwarsace ,haka yace bari ya kaiwa Abban nasa wayar,bayan yaji abubuwan da suke faruwa yafara nashi jawabin”da alama duk wanan bayanin na fahimta kuma idan na fahimta sun aureta kuma dama haka suke bazasu so rabuwa da itaba amma inshaa Allah zanzo kano sai nazo naganta.”
Daddy ne yace “dan Allah malam kayi mana wanan Alfarmar gobe in Allah ya kaimu zan yankar maka ticket ku tawo kaida Sabir d’in,malam dan Allah ayi min wanan alfarma......





HASBUNNALLAHU WANI’IMAL WAKIL.✍🏻
NI’IMATULLAH 



By haajara abla



ANNURI WRITERS ASSOCIATION 💫
(Annuri is the best).




SHAFI NA TALATIN DA UKU 33

A wanan daren ne kuma kawai ta d’auki Abba ta goyawa NI’IMATULLAH ta kama hannunta suka fara tafiya,tafiya suke sosai a cikin daren batare datasan inda suke zura k’afarsuba,tabbas NI’IMATULLAH tagaji sosai saboda yarinya ce ga kuma goyo dan ma Allah yasa yaran bashi da nauyi,sunyi tafiya mai d’an nisa NI’IMATULLAH ta durk’ushe tana kuka alamar tagaji amma da yake babu hankali a tare da rahma sai ta kamata da duka cikin tashin hankali sosai take tafiya cike da gajiya cike da kuma wahala haka suka tunga jefa k’afarta,wanan tafiyar da suke babu k’akk’autawa yarinyar shekara sha biyu da goyon yaro dan shekara d’aya abayana ta sun share tafiyar kilomitoci  sakamakon haka yasa kan NI’IMATULLAH fara juyawa nan tafara ganin dishi dishi,aikuwa nan ta sulale k’asa sumammiya...

Abinka da mahaukaciya ita ko a jikin ta,amma kuma wani ikon Allah sai ta kwance goyon dake bayan NI’IMATULLAH ta goya sanan ta sab’a NI’IMATULLAH a kafad’a taci gaba da tafiya. Batasan iya kacin tafiyar datayi ba ta samu gidin bishiya ta zauna misalin wajen kiran sallar asuba na farko,daga wanan zaman datayi bacci kuma yayi awan gaba da ita...

Babu abinda NI’IMATULLAH tasani sai farkawa datayi ta ganta a gidan maigarin wani kauye da suka tsinci kansu a ciki.

Mai garin ne ya tara wad’anda suka ganota wasu na neman cutar da ita tunda bata da hankali gashi alama bak’uwa ce dan kuwa k’auyen basu da yawa yawanci kowa ya san kowa ne hakan yasa aka kaisu wajen mai gari,a lokacin da aka kai NI’IMATULLAH da mahaifiyar ta wajen maigari a kuma wanan rana ce Baba malam ya kawowa babansa ziyara wanda yake shine mai wanan garin,Baba malam ya nemi alfarmat da idan har aka cigita ba’a samu y’an uwan taba to yanaso a basa ita da yarinyar tata insha Allah shi mai kulawa nedasu,mussaman da yaga yarinya k’arama amma tana da hankali da tarin tarbiyya tamkar wata babbar mace,haka kuwa akayi kusan sati biyu ba’a samu yan uwantaba duk da cewa an gane mahaukaciya ce amma ita ba duka take ba,dan kasancewarta mahaukaciya bai hana Baba malam ya d’aukesu da ita da yaranta ba zuwa garin kano...

Ansha gwarama sosai kafin matansa su amince saidai da ya tabbatar musu dole Rahma zata zauna a gidan haka suka hak’ura,amma fa a lokacin sun fuskanci matsi sosai da kyama saboda rahma mahaukaciya ce,bata zuwa ko ina saidai idan haukan ya tashi sai an k’ukk’ulleta a gidan...

Saida cikin ta yayi wata goma cif cif sanan wataranar litinin ta tashi da nak’uda,duk da cewa tana wanan rashin lafiyar amma bai hanata jin nak’udar datake ba,a cikin matan malam babu wanda ya kalleta sai Baba jummai inda taga tabata tausayi matuk’a su a zargin suma wani ne yayi mata cikin shege,kuma duk suna zargin su NI’IMATULLAH da Abba suma shegu ne,dan kuwa akwai wad’anda babu Allah a lamarun su kodan neman duniya sai ace mutum ya kusanci mahaukaciya dan biyan buk’atunsa NI’IMATULLAH ta fuskanci k’alubale sosai ta wanan fanni...


Cikin ikon Allah Rahma ta haifi santalelen yaronta,bayan ta haihu Babh jummai da NI’IMATULLAH suka gyara wajen Baba jummai tayiwa yaron wanka sanan ta dafa ruwan zafi,ta nunawa Rahma hanyar bayi tace taje tayi wanka,aikuwa malam yaji dad’in kulawar da Baba ta bata sosai da sosai,kuma haka ce ta ringa faruwa har Rahma tayi kwana tayi kwana talatin ke kace tana da hankali tadawo ras,malam yana bata rubutu da addu’o’i kala kala ko a dace amma ubangiji bai rubuta ba haka dai suka ringa fama...


A wanan rana da sifiya Alhaji Umar yayi sallama a ba’angaren Rahma ya shiga ciki,kasancewar jiya ba’a d’akin ta ya kwanaba,dubawa yayi sama ko k’asa babu Rahma babu kuma NI’IMATULLAH babu yaro,cike da tashin hankali ya fito da gudu yazo sashen Atine tare da tambayar ta ko taga Rahma. Sai da tayi wata uwar shewa tabbas tsafin ta yaci sanan ta juyo ta kallesa tace”ajiyar ta kabani da zakazo kana tambayata naga wata mahaukaciyar matarka.”
Baisan sanda ya wanke ta da lafiyayyun maruka ba tare da cewa”ya isheki haka,ya isheki wallahi kika sake kiranta da mahaukaciya saina sassab’a miki kamanni,matatace kuma abar sona ina sonta,saidai aniyarki ta biki,kuma duk inda kika b’oyeta wallahi kiyi gaggawar fito da ita idan bahaka ba wallahi sai kinyi nadamar dabaki tab’a yintaba.”
Wata shewa ata sake zubawa kamar wacce ya zugata tarea da fad’in “ahayye nanate,ana wata ga wata,to ni bansan abin da ya faru ba dan haka ka cireni daga sabgarku wallahi,sanan ni kamara akan nace mata mahaukaciya toba mahaukaciyar bace,babu komai wallahi sai kasan ni kamara.”
Cike da jin zafi yace”zaki rama ne.”
“Wallahi ka sake marina saina rama ubanane kai da bazan ramaba.”
Ganin tsayawa ma ya biye mata b’ata lokaci ne yasa ya fita waje da suri...
“Allah ya sauki tsiya lafiya ta fad’a tare da dok uwar bud’a...


Tun daga wanan lokacin yake neman Rahma da yaransa babu ita babu labarin ta,ofisoshin yan sanda babu wanda baije ba a garin kaduna amma babu ita babu labarinta gidan tv gidan radio amma ina labarin d’aya ne basu sameta taba,haka ya hak’ura ya fawwalawa Allah a kullum idan yayi sallah sai ya rok’i Allah ya nuna masa matar sa da y’ansa kafin ya mutu. Hakan yasa bayan wasu watanni ya tafi k’asar dubai inda ko waiwayar gida ya kasa yi,ana haka babansa ya aura masa wata yar uwarsa itace momy,yazo hutu ya tare da matarsa dan kuwa Atine batasani ba saidai kawai  ganin kishiya tayi,kuma babu yanda ta iya haka tayi asirin kuma da yake Allah baiyi zai kamataba sai a banza take,shima yanzu da yake ya dage da addu’o’i ba kamar daba to ko tayi baya shigarsa....


A kwana a tashi babu wahala a wajen Allah,yayinda Abbas ya shekara guda a kokacin Abba yana da shekara biyu da watanni kusan biyu ko uku awanan haline kuma suka wayi gari babu Rahma babu dalilinta,NI’IMATULLAH tayi kuka tayi kuka sosai tamkar idanuwanta bazasu sake ganuwa ba,haka ta hak’ura ta rungumi k’annenta ta rungumi k’addadrarta,yarinya k’arama ta zama tamkar wata babbar mace dan ita take kula da rayuwar k’anenta akwai matsi sosai na babu,akwai k’uncin rayuwa na rashin iyaye akwai gori sosai amma ya kasance ko waje bata fita saboda haka take rayuwa kamar mujiya,a haka malam ya samu aka sakata wata makarantar gwammanati,aka kaita SS2,saboda kuwa ta shiga JSS1 ne ta daina zuwa makaranta,Allah ya mata kwakwalwa sosai dan haka ta zamo ita take d’aukar firts class a wanan makaranta,hakan yasa bayan tayi jarabawarta ta WAEC da NECO gwammanaty ta biya mata scholarship ta fara karatu a college inda Allah yayi har tayi graduation....




Bani da wani babban buri nima Daddy da ya wuce naga mahaifiyata ta bayyana Daddy babu inda malam baisa anyi cigiya ba ko an duba amma ba’a gantaba,kullum ina kwana ina tashin da tunanin watarana Allah zaidawo mim da ita,ina kewa sosai da sosai ina kewar mahaifiyata ina kewar zama da ita,magana da ita,nayi kewarta sosai da sosai...” Tafad’a tana sake fashewa da matsananci kuka...
Paris ya kama hannunta yasa a nasa yana murzawa Alamar rarrashi,shima kuma kukan yakeyi,jin labarin mahaifiyar sa sosai ya tausayawa wanan rayuwa da suka tsinci kansu a ciki kuma shima yana fatan ganin mahaifiyar tasa darai da lafiya ana cikin haka su Abba da Abbas suka shigo sun dawo daga islamiyya,da sallaama suka shigo ganin anyi baki suka tsugunna gaida su, Daddyn dubai yana ganinsu yace tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah subahanahu wata ala,yaran ya janyo ya rungume a jikin sa yana ta matse kwalla yana rok’an ubangiji ya bayyana masa matar sa abar kaunarsa.

Zuciyoyi sun raunana a wanan rana ida nuwa sun zubar da hawaye sosai,da jin labarin su NI’IMATULLAH kowa saida ya tausawa rayuwar su ita da mahaifiyar....

Lokacin sallar magarif har ya wuce dan haka su Daddy suka fita masaalaci su dukan mazan sukayi sallah akabar matan suma suna yin tasu sallar....


Bayan sun idar da salla ne Daddyn dubai ya kalli malam tare da cewa”Allah gafarta malam idan bazaka damu ba inaso ka koma gidana dake sharad’a na mallaka makashi halak malak,sabon gida ne babu kowa a ciki sai mai gadinda yake kula da gidan,ba’a buk’atar ku tafi da komai akwai komaia gidan,zansa akai maka motoci biyu da driver sanan insha Allah za’a fara ginin sabuwar makaranta inda zaku koma nan kaida d’alubanka... baba makam zai fara godiya Daddy ya katse shi da cewa”babu godiya a tsakanin mu,duk abinda zanyi bazan tab’a saka maka abin da kayi min ba,nayi ne kawai dan na kyutata maka,Allah ne kada’i zai saka maka abin da kamin idan ka shiga ciki kafad’awa matan su shirya saboda a gobe nakeso ku koma idan babudamuwa...

Bayan kyuta da Daddyn dubai yayi masa Abban Paris ne shima yayi masa kyutar zunzurutun kud’i naira miliyan d’ari.shi kuma Abban su NU’AIMA yace zaisa akawo masa sak’o har gida insha Allah,dan anan yayi waya akan kayan abinci da kuma sittiru na alfarma da aka cika baba malam dasu har ya rasa bakin magana,tabbas wanan magana gaskiya ce,da Allah yace duk wanda ya shuka Alkhairi hak’ik’a zaiga Alkhairi wanda kuma ya shuka sharri to tabbas shima zai girbi abin da ya shuka....


Sallama suma su momy sukayiwa su Baba jummai tare da tarin kyututtuka,murja duk an rud’e tabbas yau tagane d’an adam koya yake ba abin wulak’antawa bane...


Su momyne sukayi sallama suka tafi gida da niyyar gobe zasu dawo,shikam Paris da NU’AIMA sam sun kasa koda motsi hakan yasa momy tace bari tabar NU’AIMA ta kwana Paris kuwa shina da kwana yace zaiyi,yana so suyi fira da yar uwar sa sosai,yanaso yagansa akusa da ita yayi kewarta tunbaisan taba,shima ayau yanajin dad’in ganin jininsa a tattare dashi,haka su Abbas suka zauna suna ta masa fira kala kala saboda ance musu ga Abbansu nan ya dawo ga kuma babban yayanasu... 

Suna cikin fira tana ta dariya yana bata labarinsa,nadariya tayi dariya na takaici tayi duk suna tare da NU’AIMA,suna ta fira a tsakar gidan inda fitilar shekarau yagaza ta haske gidan tamkar ankawo nefa,suna cikin firar kuma aka kawo wuta,anakawo wutar nan tayi tozali dashi zaune kusa da ita,cikin farar shadda mai d’aukar ido yayi kyu sosai sai zuba murmushi yake,cike da murnan ganinsa a fili tace”SAHEED kazo tayani murnar ganin mahaifina da d’an uwanane ko?”.....




YA HAYYU YA QAYYUM BI RAHMATIKA ASTAGISU,ASLIHILI SHA’ANI KULLAHU WALATAKIDNI ILA NAFSI D’ARFATA AINI.✍🏻
NI’IMATULLAH

SHAFI NA TALATIN DA TAKWAS 38

Kiran NU’AIMA ne ya shigo wayarta,aikuwa cikin Murmushi ta d’aga tare da sallama”Assalamu alaikum matar yaya.”
NU’AIMA da wata dariya ta kufe mata takuwa saki kayarta sanan tace”NINU bakya rabo da abin dariya wallahi,wai matar yaya jiki dan Allah.”
“Hhhh to mene wani abin dariya ana bayan gaskiya Na fad’a matar yaya,yayan ma masoyi ina jin dashi sosai dan haka ki kuka min dashi sosai.”
“Uhm kanki akeji,ni ba wanan ba nakira inji a ina aka kwana zancen tafiyar mu gobe kada ki mana fa wanan halin naki dan haka wallahi ki shirya.”
“Uhm kibari wallahi ba’a son raina za’ai wanan tafiyar ba,kawai dai dan babu yanda zanyi ne.”
“Kiyi hakuri Rabin raina shi mai hakuri ako da yaushe shine a sama,sanan duk wanda yayi hakuri zai samu nasara gagaruma a gaba,dan haka kiyi hakuri ubangiji bazai tozarta kiba.”
“Nagode NU’AIMA da tunatarwar ki agareni ubangiji ybar zumunci yabar kaunar mu har zuwa ajanna,ameen ya Allah.”
Suna cikin waya saiga paris ya k’araso gidan su NI’IMATULLAH aikuwa kamar yasan tana b’angaren Babah nan ya nufa,yana shigowa palorn kuma ya ganta a zaune tana waya.”
“Amarya bakya laifi.”
Yafad’a yana k’arasowa kusa da uta tare da ja mata kumatu.”
Wayar tasa a speaker sanan tace”matar yaya kina ji ko,ga angon ki nan yazo zai fara sani magana.”
“Hhhh kai rabin raina dan yazo ya taya ki fira shine zakice haka.”
Tsoma baki yayi tare da fad’in “ai ki barta niba wajen ta nazo ba,wajen su Babbah da su Abbas nazo ehee.”
Dariya sukayi gaba ki d’aya sanan NI’IMATULLAH tace”ai zuwa gobe iyanzu ma bazaka ganmu ba dan jirgin mu ya d’aga kai da ganin mu sai an cike form.”
Gyara zama yayi sanan yace”bangane ba ina zaki kuma ma naji kina magana kamar harda matata NU’AIMA kike nufi ina zaki d’aukar min ita ki kaimi.”
“Hhhh tambayi momyn su Ra’isa itace zata baka labari saboda tare da ita za’ayi tafiyar.”
“Ke NI’IMATULLAH banason wasa bangane ba wai wace tafiya za’ai.”
“Kai bro wai daman momy bata fad’a maka ba,nima yau take sanar dani zamuyi tafiya gobe insha Allah zuwa sudan.” Da sauri yace”amma dai banda matata ko?”
“Kaji ka bro to ai matar taka ce ma mai tafiyar dan kuwa zamu gyaran jiki ne.”
“Haba haba yaya momy zatayi min haka,duo masu gyaran da suke kano da nigeria basu isa su gyara kuba har sai kunje wani wajen.”
NU’AIMA ce tayi magana”kai luv ai ba dad’ewa zamuyi ba sati d’aya fa zamuyi.”
“Mtwsss kina sake cemin sati d’aya zakuyi ke a tunanin ki zan iya sati d’aya ban ganki bane?”
“I’m sorry luv I know it’s hurt amma ya zamuyi ya kama ne.”
Bayan duk NI’IMATULLAH tagama jin ta bakin su sai ta kyalkyale da dariya har ta manta rabon datayi dariya amma yaukam sun bata dariya sosai da sosai dan haka cikin jin dad’in yanda yayan nata yake kula mata da masoyiyar ta tace”wowww bro yanzu dai ka kira momy da daddy ko kaje ka samesu kace kai gaskiya bazaka iya bari NU’AIMA tayi nisadakai ba.”
“Mtwsss zanje Na fad’a mata ai ba wani abu bane,idan ya zamadole sai an muku gyran jikin kuzo na kaiku anan kanon ma ayi muku basai kunje ko ina ba.”
“Nifa kasan yaya wallahi ni badan momy ta matsa ba ba inda zani,amma ya zanyi,sanan kuma ita momy kasan suna da yan uwa sosai acan so inaganin can d’in yan uwantane keyi kuma sun iya sosai fiye da yanda baka zata ba,sai an maida maka da NU’AIMAN ka tadawo asalin buzuwarta sai ta koma tamkar India.”
Dariya NU’AIMA tayi sosai sanan tace”nikam lamarin ku ya wuce min shairin dan haka nidai Sai anjima luv Sai anjima rabin raina.”
Dariya sukayi su duka sanan suka ajje wayar..


Sunyi fira sosai kuma ya nuna mata. Harga Allah baiso tafiyar nan ba,haka tace yayi hakuri insha Allah kamar yau zasu dawo...


Lokaci bayan lokaci kanta yana sara mata sosai,washe gari tana had’a kayan ta sosai kan ya tubure dakyar ta iya tafiya gidan momyn Ra’isa dan suna ta mata waya su NU’AIMA har sun k’araso dan kuwa dole da INAYATULLAH za’a tafi baza a barta ita kad’ai ba...
Cike da mamaki suke kallonta gaba d’aya babu alamar ma zasu iya tafiya da ita a halin da take ciki,momyn Ra’isa ce ta k’ara kukerar da NI’IMATULLAH ta zauna dakyar sanan ta dafa ta tace”lafiya NI’IMATULLAH maike damunke maike faruwa ne,kodai mufasa tafiyar tunda bakyaso.”
Tunawa tayi da jiya sanda NU’AIMA take ce mata”dan Allah ki taimka muje NINU wallahi ina son wanan tafiyar sai nakejin ta daban duk a cikin tafiye tafiyan da nake,sanan ni daman ban tab’a zuwa sudan ba,amma inason zuwa wallahi,dan Allah ko dan ni kada kice bazakib.”
Sanan ta tunada maganar momyn ra’isa na cewa”daman inaso naje naga gida saboda Allah yayiwa babban yayana rasuwa wadda muke uwa d’aya uba d’aya na kwana biyu banje ba sanan Allah baiyi naje ma,inason naje naga fam d’ina.”
“Lafiya wai NI’IMATULLAH tunanin mai kike haka ina ta magana.”
“Babu komai Anty wallahi kaina ke ciwo tun jiya kamar zai rabe.”
“Kiyi hakuri kici gaba da addu’a kinji ki yawaita karanta ayatul kursiyyu sanan ki ringa fad’ar LA’ILAHA’ILLAH’ANTA SUBAHANAKA INNINKUNTU MINAZZALUM,kinji doter.”
“Nagode Anty insha Allah zan kasance mai yi a koda yaushe daman ina yi wallahi Anty Amma zan linka.”
“Tam shikkenan my doter,sauran yan uwanta duk sun baibaye ta suna ta mata fira abin ya bata sha’awa sosai taji feeling d’inta ya dawo tana enjoying yanayin duk da cewa ubansu d’aya sai take ji tamkar uwa d’aya uba d’aya suke,suna cikin fira saiga su Paris da Daddy sun shigo da sallama.
Amsawa sukayi sanan Daddy yace”babu lokacin b’ata lokacin dan haka momy ku taso ku had’o kayan ku.”
Shi kuwa Paris sai cika yake yana batsewa shi gaba d’aya ma haushin momyn data tsuro da tafiyar yakeji saboda har cikin ransa baison yayi nisa da su d’un.”
Momy ce ta kalle sa sanan tace”kai kuma fa lafiya kake ta cika kana batsewa wane ya tab’a min kai.”
Cikin shagwaba yace”momy ai kece,yanzu fisabillilah sai ki kwashe su duka ku tafi ni kuma ku sani a ina,dan haka nima ayau sai na koma wajen Abbana dubai,daman ya ce min gobe zaizo dan haka zance yayi zaman sa dannima gani nan tawowa.”
Cike da mamaki momy take kallon ikon Allah wai yau paris ne yake magana yana ta zuba haka tabb lallai rayuwa abar tsoroce.
“Calm down yarona,dan zan tafar maka da amaryar ka shine kake ta wanan kumbat bakin shikkenan ka shirya sai mu Sai ticket baka da matsala.”
“Sai da momy ta fad’i haka sai yaji kunya ta rufe shi,amma duk da haka sai yaji he feel yaje wajen nan yana jin zuciyar sa tana raya masa son zuwa sosai inda ita kuma NI’IMATULLAH fargaba ta cika zuciyar ta,kunya yaji sai yace”aa babu komai buku shirya tafiya dani ba dan haka na hak’ura adawo lafiya.”
Gabad’aya dariya sukayi har Daddyn inda suka shiga mota biyu d’aya daga momy sai daddy da driver d’aya kuma paris ne yake driving NU’AIMA agaba NI’IMATULLAH da INAYATULLAH. Fitowar su keda wuya babu jimawa suka k’arasa airport inda suka tarar da Daddyn su NU’AIMA da momy tsaye suna jiran su,k’arasawa sukayi aka shiga gaisawa inda har jirgi ya tashi sukayi musu sallama da fatan Allah khairi sanan suka dawo gida...


NI’IMATULLAH babu abin da take kalla face sararin samaniya inda tayrari sukayi ado a saman ta tana kallon yanda gajumare ya kwanta abin sha’awa kalar sama colour ne mai jan hankali sosai kuma daman shine fav colour nata,tunani ta shiga yi sosai kuma ta rasa mai ma take tunawa,can NU’AIMA dataga shirun yayu yawa sai ta mintsineta tare da fad’in”hiiii.”
Murmushi NI’IMATULLAH tayi sanan tace”hello.”
“Uhm banson tunane tunane nan naki rabin raina.”
“Babu komai zan daina watarana insha Allah,ki ringa min addu’a.”
“Ako da yaushe ina miki rabin raina kuma bazan iya gazawa da yi miki ba.”
“Nagode sosai NU’AIMA nagode da kulawar ki agareni a ko da yaushe kece kike tausata nagode da Allah ya bani ke a matsayin k’awa kuma yar uwa ina k’aunarki sosai fatana ki samu farin ciki mai d’aurewa a rayuwar aurenki.”
“Ameen ya Allah rabin raina ai babu wanan a tsakanin mu,ina fatan kema Allah ya baki fiye da haka rabin raina insha Allah nasan ma kin samu.nasan zai kula dake sosai insha Allah bazaki kuka ba.”
“Amma rabin ran kinsan wanda za’a aura min ne?”
“Wallahi bansani ba rabin raina da nasani da tuntuni Na fad’a miki amma kinsan su Daddy bazasu tab’a miki zab’in banza ba insha Allah zaki farin ciki mai d’aurewa zaki manta da duk wasu k’alubalen rayuwa da kika shiga zaki samu tarin nasarori sosai rabin raina.”
“Amma Aima ina cike da tsoro da fargaba.”
“Insha Allah duk zai wuce kamar ba’ai ba sanan ki cire fargaba ko tsoro a ranki.”
Bata bata amsaba sai kwanciya datayi a kafad’un NU’AIMA har bacci ya d’auke ta....

SHAFI NA TALATIN DA TARA 39

Cikin yardar Allah suka sauka a k’asar sudan,inda yan uwan su momy tuni sunzo tarbarsu,haka cikin mutunta juna da son juna suka d’auke su zuwa gida.
A masaukin bak’i aka ajje su inda suka samu tarba ta mussaaman daga wajen yan uwan momy,gaskiya suna da kirki sosai sunci sunsha dan haka babu abin da zasu ce musu Sai godiya,saida sukayi wanka sanan suka ci abinci suka samu waje suka kwanta,duk da cewa tafiyar babu wata gajiya amma su NU’AIMA da yake an saba da hutu haka aka kwanta ana bacci ita kuwa ko kad’an NI’IMATULLAH bata gaji ba,dan kuwa ita cikin jin dad’i tahau jirgin har sukazo kamar dai kar su sauka suyi ta tafiya a cikin sararin samaniya tana ganin taurari da gajimare suna d’ebe mata kewa.
Zaune take ita kad’ai tayi ta gumi tana ta tunane tunane har barcci mai nauyi ya d’auke ta...
Mahaifiyar ta ta hanga kusa da ita tazo tana kallon ta tana ta murmushi a lokacin datake kwance tana bacci a time d’in tayi juyi sai taji tamkar ana kallon ta,tana bud’e ido kuwa sai sukayi ido hud’u da ummin tata cikin hanzari ta d’ago ita kuma sai murmushi take mata,kamar zatayi kuka tace ummina ina kika tafi kika barni tsawan shekaru watanni da kuma makwanni a wanni,ina numfashi batare dake ba,ummin tata zatayi mata magana kenan taji daga sama ana ce mata”baccin yamma NI’IMATULLAH ku tashi,NU’AIMA ku tashi babu kyu.”
Bud’e ido tangararo akan momyn su ra’isa. Kasa shiru tayi sai cewa take”ummina kina ina dan Allah kada ki tafi kada ki sake tafiya kibarni ni kad’ai dan Allah mu tafi tare duk inda zaki zanje ummina dan Allah..”momy ce ta rufe mata baki da hannunta sana ta rungumeta ta zauna kusa da ita tara da fad’ar”Subahanallah shiyasa ba’ason bacci bayan la’asar wallahi bashi da amfani,kiyi addu’a mafarki kikayi NI’IMATULLAH.”
“Ba mafarki bane momy,naganta fa ta ganni,ba mafarki bane yanzu fa anan anan fa zatayi min magana kenan aka tasheni.tafad’a tana nuna wajen.
“Tam kingani besty mafarki ne dan Allah ki kwantar da hankalin ki insha Allah zaki ga momyn ki kuma kun kusa had’uwa da ita da yardar Allah.”
Kuka take sosai tana fad’in”bestie inajin wani sabon yanayi tunda na shigo k’asar nan yana min yawo a jiki ina jin mahaifiyata kamar tana kusa dani,ya ubangiji ka bayyana min ita.”
Momyn Ra’isa ce tace”addu’a zakiyi tayi insha Allah idan tana kusa dake Allah ubangiji zai bayyana miki ita....

Zama sukayi suna ta bata hak’uri suna lallab’ata har dai aka samu ta nutsu tayi shiru kuma bata sake magana ba sai ma addu’a datake a cikin zuciyar ta..



Washe gari tun da safe suka shiga cikin gidan gaida mutanen gidan,babban gidane sosai ga part daban daban a cikin gidan dan kuwa part yakai goma da alama dai family house ne,wani b’angare suka shiga inda iyayen momy suke,sun tsufa sosai amma Allah ya bar mata duk su biyun uwa da uba suna raye ga kuma k’annen ta da yayyenta da duk jiya suka zo daa daddare sunsha kukan tunawa da mutuwar babban yayan su da yayi wanda bata zoba...

Bayan sunzauna suna fira suna maganar anjima mai gyaran jiki zatazo,kafin tazo bari na shiga b’angaren yaya mu gaisa da matar da ya aura saboda ban santaba. 
Shid’in baifi shekara biyar kwata kwata da yin aure ba tun da matarsa ta rasu bai sake aure ba sai yanzu..


Bangaren matar ta shiga da sallamarta sallama ta sake yi sosai sanan ta shiga palorn shiru taji sai kuma taga mutum kamar akan kujera da alama tayi tagumi ta shiga tunani..
Shiga tayi ta k’arasa kusa da ita tare da dafa kafad’arta da cewa”Anty,Anty,Anty.”
Firgigit ta d’augo ta kallo wacce ke kiranta da Anty,kallon ta tayi sosai saboda ita bata san da zuwan taba sanan gidan ne kowa yana b’angarensa sai ita datake shiga b’angaren iyayen mijin nata dan d’ebe kewa sanan suma suma matan gidan lokaci bayan lokaci suna zuwa...

Cike da mamaki ta kalle ta,sunkuyar dakai momyn Ra’isa tayi dan matar ta bata tausayi sosai daga gani alhinin rashin mijin ta take kuma gashi basu samu haihuwa ba balle d’an ya d’ebe mata kewa.
“Anty kiyi hakuri idan kina tunanin nan haka zai iya cutar dake,addu’a kawai zakiyi masa,saboda itace kaunar da zaki nuna masa.”
“Wani hawaye ta share sanan tace mata nagode,amma bangane kiba?”
Taji hausarta tana futa sosai sab’anin yan gidan da bakowa ma kejin hausaba.”
Momyn Ra’isa ce tace”sunana Aishatou nice k’anwar marigayi da nake nigeria da aure.”
“Ohhh naso na tunaki yana bani labarin ki.”
Haka sukayi fira sama sama ta d’ebe mata kewa sosai har sukayi sallama ta koma b’angaren ta tarar da Hamra tazo har anfara musu gyaran jikin...

Uktu yar autar su momyn Ra’isa da akayiwa aure shekara biyu da yake a inda take anan mai gyaran jikin take tare suk tawo,haka suka samu lokaci sosai suna fira da uktu bayan an gama gyaran jikin,gashi nan har sun fara canzawa abubuwa kala kala ake musu na can sudawa,ga wani tsumi da ake basu kala kala,sosai momy ta dage dan ganin ta gyara y’ar tata dan tana d’aukar NI’IMATULLAH a matsayi y’a tana son ta sosai kuma har cikin zuciyar ta taji tana sonta,kamar ko da yaushe kullum sai ta shiga sun gaisa ita da matar marigayi har suka saba,suna d’aukar a wanni sosai da sosai suna fira,sosai take fad’a mata tayi kewar nigeria duk da tasan batada kowa acen sai wanda ke ruk’on ta wanda a wajen sa ne ma tayi auren amma tana jin kewar nigeria har take jin ina mu su tafi da ita amma ba hali.
Washe gari da yamma jirginsu zai d’aga hakan ne yasa matar marigayi tace bari tazo dai su gaisa da amaren ta samusu albarka sanan ta musu yar nasiha kafin su tafi,dan gashi biki ya rage saura kwana biyu su fara,duk wasu shirye shirye sun kammala komai acan har IV an raba,sun rabawa k’awaye suma da yan uwa duk ta hanyar online,yan uwan su momy kuwa duk takai musu dan yan matan yan uwansu NU’AIMA an cika gidan tun jiya su kawai ake jira...


Tare suk fito da Momyn Ra’isa daga b’angaren marigayi suka shigo b’angaren masaukin bak’in.
Haka kawai taji wani kala taji jikin ta ya mata sanyi taji wani farin ciki yana tun karota ai kuwa cikin tafiyarta ya nutsuwa da har yanzu bata canzaba ta shiga palorn,zama sukayi a kujera inda suka fara gaisheta ta amsa tana sa musu albarka motsi kad’an Sai NU’AIMA ta d’ago ta kalleta saboda wallahi kamar ta b’aci kamar su d’aya da NI’IMATULLAH wallahi har tafiyar,cikedai da mamaki take kallonta.
Momyce tace”ina y’ata ta shiga kuma.”
