SHAFI NA TALATIN DA BAKWAI 37

“Yanzu kana nufin rabuwa zakayi dani SAHEED ko mai kake nufi,amma kasan da cewa ni bazan iya rabuwa dakai ba sanan bazan iya auren kowa ba idan bakai ba.”
“Bahaka bane NI’IMATULLAH nayi duba da wasu abubuwan kuma nayi nazari tabbas malam musa yana da gaskiya dan kuwa ya tuna min abinda na manta shekara da shekaru,bazaiyiyu mu rayu a inuwa d’ayaba NI’IMATULLAH saboda ina cutar dake sosai ya zama dole sai mun rabu.”
“Ni baka tab’a cutar dani ba a rayuwa sai ma taimakoo na daka sha yi a alokuta daban daban,soyayya ta agareka ina jinta sosai soyayya ce ba wacce za’azo ace lokaci d’aya za’a rabamu ba na’iya d’auka.” Tana kuka take magana.
“Kibar kuka dan Allah tabbas zamu rabu,saki auri jinsinki nima kuma zan auri nawa,saidai ana miki magani kuma agogo yana dawowa baya saboda wancen ifritun,nasan insha Allah zasuyi nasara akansa,nima koda nak’i fita yanzu tabbas watarana zan fita,banaso a ranar lahira Allah ya kamani da wanan laifin NI’IMATULLAH,kimin afuwa nima ina sonki har abada kuma na miki alk’awarin zan nemo miki mahaifiyar ki nakawo miki ita har inda kike da yardar Allah,daga wanan rana dana dank’a miki mahaifiyar ki a hannunki bazaki k’ara ganina ba har abada.”
Sake fashwa atayi da kuka tana fad’in “idan kace haka inaga ka kawo k’arshen numfashi nane a doran duniyar nan SAHEED.”
“Bahaka bane NI’IMATULLAH amma zaki gane bayan komai da komai ya faru,na barki lafiya Allah ya k’ara miki lafiya.”
Daaga nan ya fita bata k’ara ganin kyallinsa ba maida kai tayi ta kwanta akan gadon nata lamo,a haka har bacci ya kwasheta..



Tafiya take batare da tasan inda take tsomar kafarta ba,tayi tafiya mai nisan gaske sanan ta samu wani saman dutse ta zauna dan kuwa duk da bata cikin hankalin ta amma akwai ciwo jikinta yana mata ciwo sosai saboda d’anyen jegoce,hkaa ta kwana kan dutsen baci basha washe gari da asuba ta farka cikinta yana wani kalar juyawa tana neman ina abinci ko abinsha domin ji take tamkar y’any’an hanjinta zasuyi tsalle su diro waje. Tafiya tafara yi tana laluben ruwa,to gashi ta rasa hanya dan kuwa daji ma take sake shiga,tun tana tafiya da k’arfinta har k’arfin ya k’are inda taje daidai wani d’ank’armin ruwa da yake a tsaye bamai tafiya bane ta sulale ta fad’i sumammiya...

Sanyin asubar da yadake ta shine yasa ta tashi nan tayi arba da wanan ruwan aikuwa tasaka hannu tafara sha ganin bazata iya jurar d’ebowar ba yasa ta kafa kai tana sha sosai harsaida cikin ta ya cika sosai,d’agowa tayi tafara tafiya aikuwa tafiya ta kasa yiyuwa saboda azabar yinwa gashi ta cika cikinta da ruwa aikuwa ciki ya kulle nan ta durkushe a wajen tana ta kuka,tana zaune wajen wasu fataken hanya sunbiyo ta dajin sunyi mamaki sosai ganin mutum a wajen kuma da alama mace,sun d’auka aljana ce dan haka kowa yaja dabaya suka saita mashi sanan babban su yace mutum ko aljan.. jin tashin magana yasa da sauri ta d’ago fuskarta ta kallesu tace”mutum ce ni,yinwa ta nuna cikinta tana fad’in ku taimakeni da abinci.”
Cike da mamaki da har sunce kawai su wuce su kyaketa sai wani daga cikin su ya kalleta yaga tabashi tasayi sosai nan ya ciro wani ragowar abincin sa ya bata ai kafin kace kwabo ta cinye tana kallonsa cikin jin dad’i kamar ta cinye sa shima,ganin haka yasa sauran suma jin tausayin ta duk suka ciro ragowar nasu suka bata,aikuwa duka ta cinye batar komai ba,ganin haka yasa d’ayan ya k’aras akusa da ita yace”baiwar Allah yaya akayi kikazo nan,mai ya kawo ki wanan dajin mai had’ari da baya rabo da yan fashi ko muggan namun dawa bakya tsoron su cutar dake?”
Wanan tambayoyin da yake mata a ciki bata iy amsa ko d’aya a saboda ba hankali a tattare da ita amma tasan jin yinwa tabbas...

Ganin ta zura masa ido tana kallonsa yasa yaja da baya sauran sukace tunda dai mun taimaka mata ku tawo mutafi bamusan taba kada muje ta cutar damu. Gaba sukayi suka fara tafiya har sun d’anyi nisa wanan bawan Allahn da ya tambayeta yaja ya tsaya sanan yafara magana”bai kamata mubaro baiwar Allahr nan ba a wancen wajen,na tambayeta batayi min magana ba amma nagano abubuwa da dama a idanuwanta dan Allah ku jirani,bazan iya barin taba dole zan daukkota bazan barta a wajen nan ba..

Aikuwa da gudu ya ruka inda su kuma suka kafa sansani dan jiran dawowar sa gudu yake sosai har ya k’araso bakin wajen,yayi sa’a ganin tana kwance tana takurewa alamar sanyi kuma da alama ma sanyi take ji,aikuwa cikin hanzari ya k’arasa inda ya cire rigar malum malum d’in datake jikinsa ya yafa mata sanan ya d’an kamo hannuta yasa a kafad’ar sa yace tawo mu tafi,bud’e ido tayi ta kalle sa batare da ta tanka masa ba suka fara tafiya..

Yazo ya samesu a inda ya barsu sanan suka cigaba da tafiya Sabi’u daman a hanyarsu ta komawa gida suke...

Bayan sun koma gida ne matar sa ta tambaya ya bata labari daga farko har k’arshe,kallon ta tayi sosai ta kalli cikin idanuwan ta tabbas itama ta gani akwai abin ban al’ajabi sosai a tare da wanan baiwar Allah,waje suka bata ta zauna sanan aka kawo mata tuwo da ruwa aikuwa tas ta cinye cike da tausayi Harira take kallonta sanan tace”mai gida amma tak’i yin magana.”
“Eh nima Na fahimci akwai damuwa,amma koma mainene karki manta nan tazo gidan share hawaye.”
“Wanan haka yake mai gida..”




Kiran wayar momyn su Paris ce ya tasheta daga baccin datake yi,harsaida ta katse bata d’auka ba sanan bayan ta bud’e ido ta shiga kiranta da kanta,aikuwa ringin biyu ta d’aga”Assalamu alaikum momy ina yini.”
“Wslm lafiya kalau doter yakike.”

SHAFI NA ARBA’IN DA UKU 43

Washe gari misalin k’arfe biyu na rana u NU’AIMA suka shirin dan tafiya gidan make’up ta gidan su NI’IMATULLAH suka biya,inda momyn su Ra’isa ta tsare su nan ma saida ta musu gyaran jiki sai wajen 4 suka tafi gidan kwalliyar saboda dinner ce,da NI’IMATULLAH tace bazata ba ummin ta ta fad’a mata wasu kalamai masu dad’i da sauk’in fad’a hakan yasa ta tashi ta bisu suka tafi.
Allah ne kawai zai sakawa momyn Ra’isa da alkhairi dominkuwa mutum ce samun irin ta a wanan lokaci sai an tona,ta tsya ta jajurce dan ganin ta gyra yar mijin ta tamkar kuwa yartace zatayi wanan auren haka take ji kuma har cikin zuciyar ta batada nufin cutar dasu,sosai momy takanyi mamakin wai kishiya ce haka abin yana bata mamaki sosai har takanyi kuka a b’oye idan tatuna da halin data tsinci kanta saka makon kishiyarta a tune. Bata fatan ko sake had’uwa da ita a wanan doran duniyar dan ta gallaza mata sosai tasata wahala tamkar baiwa ta rabata da komai nata,tana cikin wanan tunanin ne Paris yayi sallama cikin d’akin ummin nasa,amsaw tayi cikie da wani Annuri da ya baibaye zuciyar ta”wa’alaikassalam parsi sannunka.”
“Yawwa sannu ummina,ya kuke fatan kum tashi lafiya ya kwanan su Abbas.”yana mata magana yana yi mata tausa a k’afafuwan ta wani irin farin ciki ne ya sakata hawaye a wanan lokacin.”
Jin bata bashi amsa ba yasa ya d’ago ya kalle ta,gajin hawayen dake kan face d’inta yasashi idan hankalin sa yayu dubu ya tashi,share mata hawaye yayi Yana fad’ar”ummina dan Allah ki daina wanan hawayen,taya zan juri ganin ki a irin wanan yanayi ummina plssss it hurt me dan Allah kibari.”ya fad’a yana share mata hawaye.”
“Bawai kuka nake ba paris ina kukan farin cikin kasancewa ta daku ne a karo na biyu a lokacin da nake buk’atar ku a kusa dani,ashe daman wanan rana zatazo paris,bazan tab’a dainayiwa mamanka addu’a ba da ta kular min dakai har ka kawo ya war haka,ka zama cikakken mutum mai hankali ga ilimi bazan daina godiya da yiwa su Abba addu’a baharsai randa Allah ya d’auki numfashina,ruhina zai kasance dasu,Allah ubangiji yaji k’an mamanka ya saka mata da gidan Aljanna.”
Shima hawaye ya share yana jan majina kamar wani yaro yace”ameen ummina ameen,ni na miji da yakamata ace ni nayi rayuwar da y’ar uwa ta tayi amma ina Allah bai tsara hakan ba,NI’IMATULLAH tun tana yarinya taga rayuwa kuma tayi gwagwarmaya da rayuwa inda saida ta fad’i k’asa,da ikon Allah kuma ya d’agota,ina tausaya mata sosai amma ummi na tabbata daga gobe kukan NI’IMATULLAH ya k’are domin kuwa tayi sa’ar samun mijin da idan aka duba cikin dubu dakyar za’a tsamo guda biyar kalar su Insha Allah ubangiji ya bawa NI’IMATULLAH kawai abinda zamuyi mata shine mu tayata da addu’a Allah ya bata zaman lafiya da zuri’a d’ayyiba.

Koda ummi ta dawo saida Daddyn dubai ya d’auketa for the medical checkups,an mata kuma anga tana cikin k’oshin lafiya masha Allah haka aka dawo da ita gida kuma Daddyn dubai yayi gargad’i akan kar wanda ya fad’awa ummi abinda yake faruwa da NI’IMATULLAH saboda insha Allah da tayi aure malam musa zaidawo ya basu wasu magungunan da kuma addu’o’in insha Allah zasu barta har abada,dan haka yace baya buk’atar a sanar da ita dan kuwa bai kamata ta tarar da wanan ba hankalin ta zai iya tashi tana ganin Allah ya yaye musu damuwar su..


Biki yayi biki kowa da kowa ya hallara amare kaai ake jira dan har yan uwa da abokan arzik’i sun fara cashewa a filin rawar da aka tanada dan amarya da ango. Misalin k’arfe 9:00pm amaren suka iso inda a wanan karon NA’EM baizo ba tunda daga gidan kwalliya suka wuce,NU’AIMA tayi tayi akan ta kira yayan nata amma NI’IMATULLAH ta hana,tace bai kamata ta ringa takura masa ba dan taga ba abinda yake damunsa ai salon ma yace sun rainashi jiya yaje ya rufa mata asiri sanan yauma suce yazo ai abin yayi yawa,suna k’arasa suka fito ana ta shirye shiryen da tsare tsaren yadda amaren zasu shiga saiga motar gidan su NU’AIMA ta iso,juyawa sukayi suka kalle ta inda suka NA’EM ya fito ya zagaya ya bud’ewa momy da sauran yan uwanta k’ofa sun fito suka nufi wajen bikin,inda suka hango momy tanawa NA’EM magana amma basusan mai take ce masa ba.

Fara tafiy sukayi suka fara shiga inda NI’IMATULLAH ce abaya dan tace su barta a baya ita da bata da ango.

Tafiya ta farayi kanta a k’asa inda tana shiga taji an sake mata tafi MC yana ta kod’asu d’agowa tayi taga wai ma dawa suka jero,aikuwa idan ta ya sauke akan face d’in NA’EM dataga yana ta mutmushin dole yana d’an kama hannunta duk dan abin da momy ta fad’a masa na sai yazo yayi hakan.”

Bayan amaren sun zauna ne aka saka musu wani sautin dake sa nutsuwa sosai da shiga yana yi.
Paris ne ya kalli NU’AIMA tare da cewa”Wowwwww luv kinyi kyu sosai,u look so cutie like a queen.”
Dariya ta d’anyi sanan ta harareshi, ana haka har dj ya kirasu tsakiyar fili d’aya bayan d’ayan su su NI’IMATULLAH aka fara kira ita da NA’EM inda suka tsaya a tsakiyar filin aka sakar musu sautin shuraim dake tashi na perpect wani kalar hayak’i na soyayya da wani tartsatsin da yake tashi ya k’awata wanan lambun inda har yasa NI’IMATULLAH mantawa da halin datake ciki na yan sakanni inda ta sake rik’e NA’EM suna rawa a wani kalar yanayin wanda nakasa banbancewa ni haajara soyayya ce ko kuwa maine.

To Alhamdulillah haka dai aka sha shagali har aka gama amare suka nufi gida.

Bayan sun dawo ne NI’IMATULLAH tayi wanka sanan ta fito tayi sallar isha’i dan itace kawai batayi ba,zama tazo tayi kusa da ummin ta tana gyara mata blanket d’in data lullub’a,saida ta gama gyara mata sanan tasake gyara zama tayi shiru,ta rasa tunanin ma mai zatayi,a wanan lokacin da ummin ta t bayana yafi kyutuwa ace tana kusa da ita tana bata kulawar da ‘ada bata bata ba amma gashi kamar wasa wai gobe zata bar gidan da sunn nmata aure,wani hawaye ne ya fara sulalo mata,aiuwa abinka da sarki kuka,nan tafara kukan inda har saida ummin ta farka ta tashi zaune taga kan gadon,janyo NI’IMATULLAH tayi zuwa cinyarta inda ta kwanta akan cinyarta,tana shafa mata kai tana magana”mainyasa zaki kuka,bayan gani a kusa dake sanan ga babban abin Alkhairi dazai faru dake a gobe,nasani ked’in jarumace,yarinyata ba raguwa bace,kiyi hakuri ki kwantar da hankali ki indai nice a ko ina kike ina kusa dake bazan yi nisa dake ba.” NI’IMATULLAH ta katseta da”ummina amma tare zamu zauna a gidan ko,kinga daman ni yamin girma wallahi ban sababa bazan iya zama ba.”
“Insha Allah zaki saba kuma zaki zauna kiyi hakuri kinji yarinyata,inason na miki nasiha da kiji tsoro. allah kisan cewa daga yanzu rayuwar ki ta canza,kiyi k’ok’ari ki sauke shima zaiyi k’ok’ari ya soke manzon Allah S.A.W ya umarce mu da muyi aure saboda ibada ne,kuma aure an gina sane dan k’auna da biyayya dajin kai kiji tsoron Allah ki sani ibada zaki,kiyi da hakuri da mijin da Allah ya bkinkiya masa biyayya insha Allah zakiga Alkhairi sanan ki rik’e sirrin mijin Ki NI’IMATULLAH kuma ki kula da mijin ki kada kiyi wasa wajen sauke hak’insa a kanki,ki kula da abincin sa abin shansa gurin kwanan sa,ki kasance mai sauk’in kai,a duk lokacin da mijinki ya umarce ki kada ki gaza NI’IMATULLAH nasan ki nasan halin ki wanan ba komai bane amma ki sani rayuwar aure rayuwace da dole sai anyi hak’uri a cikin ta zakiga abubuwa kala dabn daban dan haka wani dole sai kinyi kamar biki gansu ba wato ma’ana sai kina d’auke kanki,sanan kina kai zuciyar ki nesa,Allah yayi miki albarka ubangiji ya albarkaci aurenku ya baki zuri’a d’ayyiba.”

Ahaka ummi tana bada labaru maban banta da nasiha kala kala har bacci ya d’auketa..



Washe gari momy ce tazo ta tasheta tun da safe akan ta dawo akwai abinda zata mata,bayan sunje b’angaren ta ta mata nasiha sosai mai ratsa jiki sanan ta bata wasu abubuwa tasha sanan ta bata wani tayi wanka,tayi mata wankan turare bayan tayi wanka,wasu fararen kaya ta d’aukko mata ta ajje mata,inda lokacin da tafi to daga wanka tace tasasu,sai abubuwa take kamar wacce kwai ya fashe mata a ciki,dan ma gaba d’aya d’aurim auren a massalacin alfur’qan dake nasarawa kusa dasu anan za’ai...

Dakyar ta iya saka kayan nata saboda gaba d’aya gabb’an jikin ta duk sunyi sanyi tana saka kayan wani zazzab’i ya lullub’eta aikuwa a d’akin momyn ta kwanta ta lullub’a tana karkarwa..

Bayan an d’aura auren ne,kowa yana ta farin ciki especially NU’AIMA datake ganin kamar ta fisu farin ciki ta auri zab’in zuciyar ta sanan kabari mai dad’i yazo mata cewa NA’EM shine mijin NI’IMATULLAH,kiran wayar ta take dan tafara sanar mata amma bata d’aga ba.
A d’akin ummi tabar wayar gashi itama ummi ta fita,taje duba NI’IMATULLAH da kuma bak’i da suka zazzo.


Paris ne ya shigo d’akin d yake momy ta fad’a masa NI’IMATULLAH Na zazzbi ganin yanayin datake ciki yasa waya ya kira likitan su,ba jimawa yazo yayi mata allurai har bacci ya d’auke yace kuma zuwa anjima zata tashi babu wata matsala kawai zazzab’in ne.

 Misalin k’arfe biyu momy ta shigo ta tasheta dan tayi sallah kuma ta shirya su tafi gidan su Babah dan lokacin yana ta sauri gashi ita tana kwance.
Haka kuwa akayi ko bin takan kiran da NU’AIMA tayi mata batayi ba kawai ta saka wayar a jaka tasa mayafinta bayan tayi sallah motoci wajen biyar sukayi zuwa gidan Baba malam inda aka cigaba da shan shagalin bikin har yamma tayi inda ake ta k’ok’arin kai amaren...


Kuka sosai NI’IMATULLAH keyi saboda batason rabuwa da umminta dakyar momy ta rabasu tana basu hakuri itama ummin kuka take sanan tayi mata addu’a Allah yasa ta tafi a sa’a haka suka sata a mota suka nufi gidan auren ta da ita....

SHAFI NA ARBA’IN 40

Ummiii...na... ke... kafin ta k’arasa kawai ta fad’i a wajen sumammiya,itama kuwa luuuuuu bayn ta tashi ta sulale k’asa sumammiya,cike da tashin hankali momy take kiran sunayen su,NU’AIMA tafara kuka tana girgiza NI’IMATULLAH,ruwa INAYATULLAH ta d’aukko a gorar ruwa da sauri momy ta  b’alle murfin ta zuba musu wata ajiyar zuciya suka saki a lokaci guda,aikuwa suka fara kallon kallon inda NI’IMATULLAH ta fashe da kuka sosai sanan ta matso kusa da mahaifiyar tata,inda ta bud’e mata hannu takuwa rungumeta suna kuka su biyun mai rikita zuciya,kukan farin ciki dana bak’in ciki suke,da farko kukan farin cikin ganin juna sanan suna kukan bak’in cikin adadin shekarun da suka shud’e batare da sun ga juna ba...

Cike da ganin ikon Allah dabaya k’arewa momy dasu NU’AIMA suke kallon ikon Allah,tabbas godiya ta tabbata ga Allah subahanahu wata’ala buwayi gagara misali...
Bayan sun tsaigata ne momy tayi godiya ga Allah da ya nuna mata wanan ranar inda batare da bat’a lokaci ba ta tashi ta shiga gidan dan sanar da mahaifanta abinda yake faruwa..

“Nayi mutuk’ar kewarki umminaa kinyi nisa dani a lokacin da kike buk’atar kulawata,nagode wa Allah da ya sake had’ani dake kafin numfashina na k’arshe ina godiya da Allah da yasa kafin nabar duniyar nan na ganki.”
Cikin share hawaye Rahma tace”ina jarirai na guda biyu NI’IMATULLAH,tabbas NI’IMATULLAH Allah abin godiya ne,naji dad’i sosai da Allah ubangiji ya k’addara saduwar mu kafin barina duniya naji dad’i da bazan iya misaltashiba,Wallahi NI’IMATULLAH koyau na mutu ruhina zai samu salamar ganin ki,nayi farin cikin dana sameki cikin k’oshin lafiya gashi har kina shirin aure,NI’IMATULLAH nasan kin fuskanci matsaloli da dama a rayuwa k’addara ta rabaki dani a lokacin daya kasance nizan kula dake a lokacin da yadace kina kusa dani cikin kulawata amma saidai k’addara ta riga fata,Allah bai k’addara haka ba,bansan mai ya faru ba NI’IMATULLAH saidai Na bud’e idona na tsinci kaina a gidan wani bafatake da yazama tamkar uba a gareni yayi min komai dabazan tab’a mantawa dashi ba har k’arshen rayuwa,na manta da abubuwa da dama kafin ganin ki,nayi rayuwar dani kaina bansantaba NI’IMATULLAH haka tamu k’addarar ta kasance,ina godiya ga Allah da ya nuna min wanan ranar sanan kuma ina rok’an ubangiji da a wanan lokacin yabarmu tare har iya tsawon numfashi na na k’rashe...” sai ta fashe da kuka inda itama NI’IMATULLAH kuka take sosai su NU’AIMA ma duk kuka suke na tausayawa rayuwa irin tasu NI’IMATULLAH tabbas kowane bawa da kalar tasa k’addarar a rayuwar sa...

Bayan an had’u a babban palor da daddare ne akabawa iyayen momy takaitaccen tarihin Rahma sanan NI’IMATULLAH ta fad’a mata tarihin abinda ya faru har ganin yayan ta datayi sanan aka fad’a mata mijinta shine yake auran momy.sosai Alhajin su momy yayi mata nasiha mai ratsa jiki sanan yayiwa momy ma dasu gabad’aya,ya kuma shirya cewa su bari tunda abin ya zama haka jibi in Allah ya kaimu sai su tafi har ita Rahma saboda iyalanta duk suna can....

A wanan dare basuyi bacci ba dan kuwa NI’IMATULLAH a b’angarrn mahaifiyar ta ta kwana sun kwana tana bata labarin abubuwa kala kala da suka faru na alajabi da tausayawa a rayuwar ta,ta tausayawa rayuwar yarinya k’arama kamar NI’IMATULLAH amma ta jajurce har takawo yanzu,sanan take bata labarin amincim su da NU’AIMA sanan kuma ita paris zai aura,tayi kuka sosai tayi kuma murna sosai da Allah ya k’addara za’ayi bikin y’ayan ta tare da ita..

Ranar dazasu tafi an had’a musu goma sha tara ta arziki amare kuwa ko haka aka barsu ba makeup sunyi mutuk’ar kyu sunyi kyu sosai abin ban sha’awa haka cikin kewa suka baro gida inda da momy da ummi tamkar ya da k’anwa daman kuma sun saba ummi ta saki jikin ta sosai bayan su uktu sun rakasu airport sanan suka musu sallama inda suka shiga jirgi,inda jirgin ya tashi sai nigeria

KANO

Shirye shirye ya kan kama hatta gidajen amaren angama tsara mus anyi jere da komai da komai,Daddyn dubai ne ya siyawa Paris gida shi kuma Abbansa ya d’auki nauyin lefe da duk wani abu da za’a buk’ata a bikin,Daddyn su NU’AIMA sunyi shawara da Daddyn dubai inda a unguwa d’aya gidan NU’AIMA da NI’IMATULLAH ya kasance dan basuda nisa inayace ma tayi nisa ba laifi.


Su Abba Muhammad duk antawo biki sun sauka a hotel inda gidan momy ya cika da yan uwan Daddyn dubai gidan su NU’AIMA ma ba’a magana inda gidan malam ma ba’a barshi a baya ba dan sunyi gayya suma biki na mussaman...


Kamar yadda aka tsara bayan su NI’IMATULLAH sun dawo,za’ayi komai da komai a gidan Daddyn bayan an daura aure za’a mayar da ita gidan malam dan kuwa acewar su Baba malam da Babah jummai su zasu kaita har gidan ta....



Su Momyn NU’AIMA ne suka tawo airport tarbarsu inda gabad’aya suka wuce gidansu NU’AIMA,Momyn NU’AIMA tayi mamakin ganin su da bak’uwar fuska gata fara sol kamar ka tab’a jikin ta jini ya fito taganta tamkar dai balarabiya to a gaskiya tana ma zaton yayar momyn Ra’isace...
Abinci kala kala aka tarbesu dashi,inda suka zauna a palorn,saidai momy ta lura NI’IMATULLAH na kusa da wanan matar kuma ta rik’e hannunta tamkar dai wani zai kwace ta...

NU’AIMA da shegen surutu ta kasa hak’uri ta matsa kusa da momynsu tace”momy kinsan wace wanan?”
“Aa NU’AIMA naganta dai kamar balarabiya.”
Dariya tayi sanan tace”itace mahaifiyar NI’IMATULLAH.”
Cike da mamaki momy batasan ta zaburaba tace mahaifiyar NI’IMATULLAH kumake.... ai bata ankaraba taga kowa ya d’ago yana kallonta sunkuyar da kanta tayi dan taji kunya,inda ita kuma Rahma tayi mata Murmushi sanan tace”sunana Rahma.”
Masha Allah ta amsa inda momyn Ra’isa tace”wanan itace mahaifiyar NI’IMATULLAH kuma mahaifiyar sirikin ii Paris.”
Cike da mamaki take kallon ta,inda momy ta kwashe labari har yadda akayi ta auri yayanta ta fad’a mata,
“Hak’k’a godiya ta tabbata ga Allah subahanahu wata’ala.”
Nan sukayi jaje sanan sukaci abinci dukda NI’IMATULLAH da Rahma bata abincin suke ba,burin Rahma kawai taga yaranta da mijinta,ana wanan tattaunawar saiga Paris da NA’EM saiga kuma Daddy ya dawo nan dai aka ringa lailaye zance,misalin k’arfe uku kuma suka nufi gida.”
Tuni paris yayiwa Daddyn sa waya yace ya shirya masa surprise,ita kuwa Rahma sai kallon yaran da akace d’antane,sai kallon yanda suke kama da mahaifinsa take tamkar an tsaga kara...


Yana cikin gidan yana palornsa shida Abba Muhammad suna ta tattaunawa Paris ya shigo da Sallama.
Amsa masa sukayi yaje ya zauna kusa da Abbansa sanan yace”daddy yaudai mafarkinka zai tabbata.”
“Wane kuma mafarkina?”
“Hhhhhh kaidai ku zuba ido zuwa da safe zan baka mamaki.”
Dariya sukayi su biyun sukace Allah ya kaimu.
Tun a ranar NI’IMATULLAH tayiwa Babah jummai waya akawo su Abba da Abbas sanan suma gobe da safe suzo su duka.
Hak kuwa akayi da safe Daddyn dubai ya kammala breakfast a d’akinsa inda duk paris ne ya shirya dan kuwa Daddyn dubai a cikin iyalansa yake break,misalin goma ya kira Abba ai kuwa bai jimaba yazo inda suka nufi babban palorn gidan saboda Daddyn yaji ya k’agu yaga maine dan kuwa daren jiya kwana yayi yana mafarkin matarsa Rahma...

Suna shiga da sallama kamar yaddda ya saba aka amsa masa har yak’araso palorn bai kula da itaba saida yazo daf sanan yaji zuciyar sa tana bugawa inda itakuma tana bayansa tana ta share hawaye,juyawa yayi jin kamar kuka k’asa k’asa a bayan sa,aikuwa sukayi ido hud’u da ita.....

Rahh...haaaa...maaa.
NI’IMATULLAH

SHAFI NA ARBA’IN DA BIYU 42

Da sauri NA’EM ya fito daga cikin motar ya nufi inda yaga NI’IMATULLAH ta fad’i,a hankali ya bita da kallo gashi ba kowa a wajen haka ya matsakusa da ita bashi da zab’in da ya wuce ya d’auketa yasata a mota su tafi,hannun sa yasa guda biyu ya rik’ota da nufinsa na yakaita mota,amma ina wani zabura tayi zata kufce daga rik’on da yayi mata,cikin hanzarai ya sake rik’eta,nan tafara fisge fisge tana neman gudu daga hannunsa,samu yayi dakyar ya rik’e ta sosai yajata har wajen wata k’atuwar bishiya datakewaye wajen inda babu wanda zai hangosu,hannusa yasa ya k’ara matso da ita jikinsa duk dan kar ta kufce saboda abin har yana nema yafi k’arfinsa....
“Ka sake mu ka sake mu,mai yasa kake nema ka shigar mana hanci da k’udundune mai yasa kake nema ka rabani da iyalina,to k’aryar ka tasha k’arya nayi alk’awarin indai ina raye bazaka tab’a aurentaba.”
Gabad’aya yayi mamakin jin abin da take furtawa,bai tsaya wani dogon tunani ba yasa kansa wajen kunnen ta na dama ya fara karanto wasu addu’o’i da wasu ayoyi na cikin alk’ur’ani mai girma,wata irin birgima take sosai a k’asan abin tausayi sai kace ba ita taci kwalliya ba d’azu kamar wata balarabiya kamar a d’auketa a gudu,amma yanzu itace kwance out of her sense sai shure shure takeyi a wajen.
Gani yayi tayi lagwaf jikin ta ya saki,nan ya samu damar sakin ta ya tashi ya nufi mota ya d’aukko gorar ruwa inda yayi wani karatu a cikin ta,sanan ya fito ya nufi ind take ruwan ya fara bata a bakin sanan ya shafa mata a fuskarta,wani irin hamma tayi sai kuma takama wani kalar abu numfashin ta yana ta d’aukewa tana k’ok’arin fisgosa,dakyar tace  wayyo Allah zan mutu bana numfashi.”
Tana gama fad’ar haka bayan damk’o rigarsa datayi ta saki a lokacin kuma idanuwan ta suka rufe.
Innalillahi wa’innailahi raju’un shine abinda yake fad’a ya rasa ma mai zaiyi nan take kuma dabara ta fad’o masa ai tuni ya d’auke ya kaita mota yasata a sit d’in gaba ya kwantar da kujerar,saboda yana tsoron ma yasata a seat d’in baya babu mai rik’eta,bayan ya kwantar da ita ne ya fara danna k’irjinta yana nemo numfashi amma ina ko motsi batayi hankalin sa yayi mutuk’ar k’ara tashi dan haka tuni wani tunani ya fad’o masa inda ya daura bakinsa daidai nata tare d hura mata iska mai k’arfi,ai kuwa cikin ikon Allh ta sauke wata ajiyar zuciya tare da bud’e ido a hankali.
Alhamdulillah ya fad’a a fili sanan ya k’ura mata ido,itama d’in shi take kalla dakyar ya d’auke face d’insa daga saman tata sanan ya koma yayi realize a driving seat.
Wani hawaye ne ya zuraro mata sanan dakyar ta bud’e baki tace”kayi hak’uri Yaya NA’EM.”
Tausayi sosai ta bashi yaarinyar gaskiya tana shan wahala sosai,bud’e bakin sa yayi tare da cewa”babu komai kar ki damu Allah ya baki lafiya.”
Sai a wanan lokacin ya gano cewa ashe NI’IMATULLAH Daddyn sa yakeson aura masa,yaji duk abinda aljanun yarinyar nan suka fad’a sanan ya tariyo maganar Daddy akan taimakon da zaiyi na auren da za’a masa da kuma yadda ya ringa nanata masa daya rik’eta da amana kada ya cutar da ita saboda yarinya ce mai hankali da nutsuwa.
Shi kansa yarinyar tana basa mutuk’ar tausayi sosai kuma yayi alk’awarin tabbas indai itace zai rik’e ta da amana fiye da yanda Daddyn sama ya buk’ata.
Motar ya tada yayi baya sanan ya fara tafiya slowly akan titin tare da sakin wat k’ra’a mai dad’i a motar,kwanciya ta sakeyi ta rufe idonta batare da sanin ko ita kanta mai take tunani ba,saidai wani snyin ni’ima da yake shigarta ga wata nutsuwa datake ratsa zuciyar ta gabad’aya ciwon kan ta nemesa ta rasa,sai wani shauk’i dabatasan ko na menene ba da yake ratse zuciyata sa at thesame time ma shima NA’EM he feel thesame what she feel yana jin wata nutsuwa sosai datake ratsa zuciyar sa ga sanyin AC da ya had’e da sanyin turaren su su biyun abin yayi sosai very attractive.


Saida ya kaita har gidan momy sanan yayi parking a wajen,ganin bacci ya d’auketa sai ajiyar zuciya take na kukan datayi yasa kawai ya tsaya yana ta kallonta,sai wani Murmushi yake wanda yake ratsa duk wani sassa na jikin sa,Allah ya gani yana jin tausayin ta sosai kalli yadda take bacci,da alama Na wahalar datasha d’azu ne am kuma bai kamata na tashe taba,haka dai ya zauna ya k’ulla wancen ya kwance wancen har wajen 11 na dare,inda wayar NI’IMATULLAH ta fara k’ara wacce k’arar wayar tata ce ta tasheta,dubawa tayi taga wace take kiranta,kallon screen d’in wayar yayi inda akasa rabin raina,har ya fara tunanin wane kuma rabin ranta,sai tunanin sa ya katse a time d’in datace “hello Assalamu alaikum NU’AIMA.”
“Wa’alaikissalam NINU ina kika shige ne an tashi daga wajen bamu ganki ba?”
“Kaina ke ciwo NU’AIMA shine yayan ku ya kawonk gida.”
Dariya tayi sanan tace”ayya sannu NINU nifa nasan duk abin da yake damunki.”
“Hmm ina jinki maike damuna.”
“Far gabar auren mana.”
“Uhm to mai yasa bazan yi fargaba NINU bansan wanda zan’aura ba,bansan wane kalar halaye gareshiba ban tab’a ganin saba bamu tab’a zama ko sau d’aya mun tattauna domin mu fahimci juna ba taya bazan damu ba NU’AIMA I cant take it any more.”
Sai tasa kuka.
“Dan Allah kibari besty wallahi ni ban fad’i haka ba dan kiji babu dad’i ko dan ki kuka dan Allah kiyi hakuri amma nasan insha Allah mijin ki zai kasance tamkar ke,zai kasance mai kyu mai nutsuwa da sanin ya kamata mai addini kuma d’an boko sai soki fiye da yadda zaiso kansa nayi miki alk’awarin insha Allah bazaki kuka ba dan haka I don’t want this kinji.”
“Shikkenan nagode NU’AIMA zan shiga gida.”
“Ok babu damuwa muyi waya.”
Duk maganar dasu NU’AIMA sukeyi duk NA’EM yana jinsu,ya gane cewa itama NU’AIMA batasan wanda k’awar tata zata aura ba,amma bazai fad’a mata ba zai mata surprise gashi duk maganar datake fad’a da irin yadda take karanto qualities na mijin NI’IMATULLAH tamkar dai tasan Yayan nata ne amma zai dai gwada tambayar ta yaji idan ta sani.”

Sallama yayiwa NI’IMATULLAH inda ya tambaye ta zata iya takawa zuwa gida,Tafad’a masa zata iya zuwa dan haka saida ya shigar da ita har wajen parking k’ifar palorn sanan ya juya ya nufi gida...


Bayan NU’AIMA sun gama waya ne taji tana son ta tambayi Luv d’inta ko yasan mijin da k’anwar tasa sata aura,gashi kuma yanzu suka rabu,haka ta shiga gida da tunanin idan sunyi waya zata tambaye shi,dan yanzu itama hankalin ta ya dawo kan sanin wanda yar uwar tata zata aura...


Misalin k’arfe sha biyu NA’EM ya dawo gida inda bai tsaya ko ina ba ya nemi a shiga palor yace hassana ta kira masa NU’AIMA,sama tahau ta kira NU’AIMAn
NU’AIMA jin wanda yake kiranta yasa ta tashi ta fito daga d’akin nasu da yacika da bak’i yan mata ana ta shan shafta,palorn sa ta nufa inda ta samesa yana shan tea,zama tayi bayan ta masa sallama sanan tace”Yaya gani.”
“Am NU’AIMA daman abinda yasa nasa aka kiraki shine wata tambaya nakeso nayi miki?”
“Tam shikkenan Bro Allah yasa na sani.”
“Am kinsan wanda NI’IMATULLAH zata aura?”
Sake gyara zama tayi jin wanan zancen ne tace”no bro I don’t know about him,but kasna shi ne,dama yau nake so na tambayi Paris nasna yasanshi.”
“Eh Aa bansanshiba,yanzu kira paris d’in muji ko yasansa.”

Kiran wayar sa tayi,inda ya da’ga tarea da cewa”luv good nyt ya nabarki.”
“Lafiya kalau,am daman so nake na tambaye ka amma d’azu shaf na manta.”
“Aha I’m hear u.”
“I’m luv kasan kuwa mijin da NI’IMATULLAH zata aura ko kaima su Daddy basu sanar dakai ba?”
Dariya yayi sanan yace”kina da abin dariya luv,taya san kasa sanin mijin da kanwata rabin raina sata aura taya zan kasa sanin wanda zan basa aurenta,wanda zasuyi rayuwa tare.”
“Uhm luv shine ka sani amma baka fad’a min ba.”
“I’m sorry kawai inaso na miki suprise ne kuma kema jibi in Allah ya kaimu ai zaki sani karki wani damu.”
“Yanzu bazaka fad’a min ba luv.”
“I’m sorry sai jibin.”

Haka dai takashe wayar bai fad’a mata ba,komawa yayi yayi luf a kujerar da yake kai,ita kuma ta tashi ta koma d’akinsu...


Ya shiga tunani abubuwa kala kala a ransa shima yanason sani amma kuma baisan yadda zaiyi ya sani ba kafin ranar.....

SHAFI NA ARBA’IN DA D’AYA 41

Rah...maaa.. ya fad’a da muryar sa dake rawa,kuka ne sosai ya kufce mata,aikuwa tazo ta rungumesa sosai tamkar wadda za’a gudar mata dashi,haka shim kukam yake sosai yana neman yafiya da gafara a wajenta.
“Rahma ki yafe min,ki yafe min na cutar dake nakasa rik’e amanar da Allah ya bani Rahma ki yafe min dan Allah kice kin yafe min Rahma ko zan samu Rahma a wajen ubangiji.”
Wani abune da ya tsaya mata a mak’oshi taji kamar tasa ruwa ta korasa taji ya fad’a kutt taji sani kalar sanyi da annushuwa da farin ciki mara misaltuwa sun lullub’eta,cikin kukan farin ciki tace masa”babu abinda kayi min wallah Daddy,baka cutar dani ba ka sani kaddara ce Allah ya riga ya rubuta mana babu mai iya goge mana,kuma nagode wa Allah dayasake had’amu a rayuwar duniya.”
Tana fad’ar haka tana kukan farin ciki lallashin ta sosai yake yana d’an buga bayan ta,a hankali ta zame jikinta daga nasa sanan ta koma kan kujera ta zauna kanta a k’asa kowa na wajen yayi farin ciki sosai inda NI’IMATULLAH ma kuka take tana kwnce a jikin Paris sai kuma su Abbas suma da suke kusa da ita.
“Abbas ne yace”mai yasa kike kuka Anty Daddu ku daina kuka wanan wacece.”yaron yana ganin kamar dai ya tab’a sanin ta amma kuma ya manta.
“Wanan itace umman mu datayi tafiya to sai yau Allah ya dawo da ita,kuje wajenta kunji.”
Da gudu suka tashi suka nufi wajenta suna mata sannu da zuwa tare da tambayarta tsarabasu
Abbas ne yace”maamaa mai yasa kika dad’e bakya tare damu?” Kafin tayi magana su babah jummai da murja sukayi sallama cikin palorn inda malam ya tsaya daga farfajiyar gidan har saida aka masa iso sanan ya shigo.

Ganin abin farin cikiin da yafaru yasa kowa yayi farin ciki sosai na ganin mahaifiyar NI’IMATULLAH kana ganin NI’IMATULLAH kasan tana cike da farin ciki mara misaltuwa nan dai aka zauna ana maida yadda akayi da canzan d’anta da aka mata Abba ya bata labari da sauran abubuwan da suke faruwa,Daddy ne yace”yanzu wad’anda kika zona a gidan su,ko zaki iya tuna a wane gari suke?”
“Gaskiya yanzu dai bansan a wane jaji suke da zama ba saboda shi d’in bafatake ne ba mazaunin waje d’aya bane,bayan ya fito birni har Allah ya had’a ni da marigayi ba wani d’auki lokaci ba duka duka a sati guda akayi auren sanan mukayi musu sallama dashi da matar sa,yayi jinyata sosai har na warke dan Haka marigayi yayi masa goma shatara ta arziki sanan muka rabu.”
“Allahu akbar,Allah mai iko,shi kad’ai zai iya Haka Allah mun gode maka daka sake had’a wanan iyali akaro na biyu.” Malam Babah ya fad’a.
Haka cikin farin ciki suka watse inda Rahma ta zauna guest part na gidan kafin asanyadda za’ai gashi yan uwan mijinta ana ta murna da ganin ta dan kuwa sunzo bikin...

A wanan lokacin ne kuma suka tafi gidan k’unshi dan gobe zasuyi kamu.
Saidai NU’AIMA ta lallab’a NI’IMATULLAH sosai akan kamun dan haka tace itafa babu abinda zatayi tunda dai ance auren za’a dauara mata kawai a d’auara mata to dakyar ta shawo kanta har sukaje suka siya sak’i iti d’aya wanda zasusa a d’inke a komai suka sai komai iri d’aya.”
Sunyi dare sosai dan kuwa basu dawo da wuri ba.
A wanan wunin ne Daddy ya kira NA’EM ya fad’a masa komai sanan yace yaje ya gaida mamansu NI’IMATULLAH,aikuwa babu musu yaje ya gaisheta cike da ladabi da biyayya ta tarbeshi sosai da sosai daman Daddy ya fad’a mata shine mijin NI’IMATULLAH amma shidai NA’EM kawai ance yaje ya gaidata tunda shi baibi ba’asin wacce zai auara ba,tunda dai daddy ya takura akan zai masa aure ai shikkenan ya bashi wuk’a da naama.
Ta saaka masa albarka sosai da sosai hakan ya dawo gida matar gaskiya tana da shiga rai ta burgeshi sosai,kuma ya taya NI’IMATULLAH murnar ganin mahaifiyar ta.


A daren kamu haka ta kwana ba bacci sai juyi take ta rasa maike mata dad’i ji take kamar ana rura mata huta sosai a cikin zuciyar ta komai yayi mata bak’i gashi takasa ko rintsawa....


Ana haka har b’arawon bacci ya d’auketa,washe gari tun d safe NU’AIMA tazo akan su tafi wajen gyaran kai daga nan su amso sak’in da sauran abubuwa,babu musu suka shirya suka fita,basu dawao basai wajen uku na rana inda NI’IMATULLAH tace ita gaskiya babu wani wajen makeup da zata dan ita jiri takeji ma idan ta tashi,duk da haka saida NI’IMATULLAH ta kira mai kwalliyar tazo har gidan domin ayimusu,aikuwa sunyi mutuk’ar kyu sosaida sosai kamar a d’aukesu,ana ta tafiya wajen kamun dan a nan nasara event d’in yake...

“Ina kuma Zaki Habibty.”
“Uhm daman palor zani tunda naga shiryaw zakayi.”
“Amma Habibty daman baya kanki ki gyara mijin ki,ki shirya shi.”
Shiru tayi batace komai ba,dan ita bama kallon inda yake take yiba.”

Saboda tace masa yau idan zai futa ya ajjeta a gidan NU’AIMA.”

Kunya ce ta kamata ta rufe fuska sanan ta ruga da gudu cikin gidan tana ce masa”babu ruwana Yaya..

SHAFI NA ARABA’IN DA HUD’U 44

Godiya ta tabbata ga Allah subahanahu wata’ala tabbas NI’IMATULLAH kin shigo sabuwar rayuwa kiyi hakuri sanan kiyiwa mijin ki biyayya Allah yana tare dake Allah ya baku zaman lafiya tare da zuri’a d’ayyiba,Momy Tafad’a tana shirin juyawa dan tafiya zatayi,jama’a kuwa kowa yana yaba gidan ganin irin kyu da yayi tamkar ba a nigeria yake ba,NI’IMATULLAH ce ta rik’o hannun momy tare da kiran”momy dan Allh kada ku tafi,Babah jummai kar ku tafi dan Allah daku na saba kada su rabani dake.”
“Kiyi hakuri NI’IMATULLAH ko wace mace da haka take sabawa kinji kiyi hakuri.”
Haka kowa ya watse yabar NI’IMATULLAH ita kad’ai sai sheshek’ar kuka take saboda gaba d’aya kuan ma yanzu ya daina zuwa saboda tayi kuka tayi kuka har ta godewa Allah tayi shiru tayi jigum tana ta juya wayar datake hannunta tan wasa da yatsun hannunta,tana cikin wanan yanayin taji kamar an shigo d’akin datake.

Misalin k’arfe goma sha biyu ne na dare,yafi awa biyu a palor tunda yakai NU’AIMA haka su paris suka rakosa yana b’angaren sa,sanan sauran abokan suka sake raka paris tunda basu da nisa,ya rasa ta yarda zai shiga duk da tunda yaji cewa NI’IMATULLAH ce matar sa ya kasance cikin wani yanayi mai cike da jin dad’i saboda shi har ga Allah bawai son NI’IMATULLAH yake ba kawai yana mutuk’ar jin tausayin ta,yayi kwana da kwanaki baya baccin da daddare yana tunanin wane wanda wanan yarinyar zata aura a wanan hali da take ciki,kusan kullum sai ya ganta a mafarkin sa yana son taimakar ta amma kuma ya kasa gane abin da wanan mafarkin yake nufi sai yau ya fahimta ya gane mafarkin da yake...

“NI’IMATULLAH ina sallama kin kasa amsawa,ko na miki wani laifin ne.”

Gabad’aya wani kala take ji,mai yakawo Yaya NA’EM gidan ta,kuma taga kowa ya tafi amma mai yasa idan rakota yayi shi bai tafi ba?kuma mai ya shigo dashi har d’akin ta?tanaso tayi masa duka wad’anan tambayoyin amma kuma bakinta yayi nauyi harshenta ya gaza d’aukar koda kuwa kalma d’aya ce domin ya furta.
Shima ta b’angaren NA’EM haka nan yaji harshen sa yayi nauyi ya rasa ta inda zai fara mata bayani,ya rasa ta inda zai fara ce mata NI’IMATULLAH nine mijin ki,ni aka bawa ke,to taya ma zai mata bayani..
Haka suka zauna wajen minti talatin tamkar dari kurame.


Wani fitsari sosai take ji tamkar zatayi shi a pant d’inta dan Haka dakyar ta d’aga baki tace”Yaya ina ne toilet inason zan...

Bata k’arasa ba ya kamo hannunta har zuwa bathroom d’in saida yakaita sanan ya dawo ya zauna..

Abin mamaki yaga tayu alwala,har zata tada sallah,yace,am bara nayi alwalar nima nazo muyi.
Batare datace komai ba yayi alwala yazo suka fara sallah,sukam baza su iya irga adadin lafunan da sukayi ba har NI’IMATULLAH tagaji ta fara bacci sama sama saboda ta gaji,ganin haka kawai yasa yayi sauri. Sallamewa yayi masu addu’a sanan ya tashi ya koma palor ya d’aukko ledar kazar da ya siyo da fresh milk da sauran kayayykin tand’e tad’e.
Plat yaje ya d’aukko sanan ya dawo yana zubawa a plat d’in kansa yana k’asa yana magana”amma kiyi hakuri NI’IMATULLAH na barki da yinwa ki gafarceni.”

SHAFI NA HAMSIN 50

Da d’an sauri sauri ya k’arasa inda suke abinka ga tsoho dakyar yake tafiyar,tsugunnawa tayi kusa dashi tana kukan yaushe gamo”ya isa haka Rahma inace dai yanzu kece a kusa damu to ki share hawayen ki kibar wanan kukan bama so,ya fad’a da yaren su.”
“To Abba nayi shiru.tafad’a tana rufe bakin ta da tafukam hannayen ta yayin da wasu hawayen suke ambaliyar fitowa daga idanuwan ta.
“Rahma ina mijin ki da yaran ki suke?”da yake ba gani yake sosai ba,shiyasa bai hangosuba su kuma anyi tsit masu taya mamansu kuka suna yi,Arfa ce tace”Abbahh gasu can zaune bisa capet.”
Daddy neya k’araso har inda Abbahh yake ya tsugunna ya gaidashi yana kuka yake neman afuwar sa bisa laifin sa,na hanashi ganin yarsa da jikokinsa da sukai tsawon shekaru,su Abbah da Abbas suna kan cinyar momyn Ra’isa,aikuwa sukaga fili gidan babban nan suka tashi suna wasa gashi k’asa ce,daman sun saba sun son wasan k’asa.
Haka akayi ta neman yafiya kowa ya yafewa kowa sanan aka nuna musu jikokin su da takaitaccen labarin aurensu ana ta fita da sauran yan gidan haka aka gama abinci aka zuba musu a k’aton tire maza daban mata daban,ga abinci yaji nama sosai da sosai kuma yayi dad’i babu laifi,amma da yake ance sabo trken wawa,dakyar paris da NA’EM da NU’AIMA suka iya cin wanan abincin sukayi loma uku,NU’AIMA dan kar ace wani abu tayi loma hud’u aikuwa a ta biyar d’in sai amai haka ta ringa kwarara amai saida ta amayar da komi Paris hankalinsa duk ya tashi haka ma NI’IMATULLAH dan kuwa sai taji kamar tayi mata kuka dan ma kuwa tayi,tambaya yake inane asibiti mafi kusa zaikai matar sa a dubata.”
Ita kuma tace a’a ya bari lafiyar ta kalau kawai dai aman ne yazo mata,momy tafara fahimtar wasu abubuwan dangane da su NU’AIMA da NI’IMATULLAH dan kuwa ranar nan ma da NU’AIMA tazo dataci abinci saida tayi amai NI’IMATULLAH kuma batason k’amshin turare ko wane kalane,wacce ada take son turare kamar mai da kuma wanada momy ke amfani dashi.


Momy tayi Murmushi sanan ta lallashi paris akan yayi hakuri normal ne”normal kuma Anty kalli fa yanda take amai.”
“Karka damu insha Allah babu matsala..


Wasa wasa NA’EM yana kallon NI’IMATULLAH saida taco abincin dayafi k’arfin ta sosai haka ta cinye kuma abincin yayi mata dad’i sosai daman wallahi tun tuni kalar wanan abincin takeson ci,amma banu wanda zaiyi mata,gaskiya yan gidan nan su mata hajji da umra.

“Yana wajen ya bata amsa.”

Kunya ce da dariya ta kamata ta shiga gidan da d’an gudunta tana dariya.....

“Wayyo Allah,Allah ya saukeki lafiya rabin raina nagode sosai rabin raina Wayyo dad’i haka ta jawo shi ta kamasha tana ta jin dad’i tana kuma godewa NI’IMATULLAH...


A kwana a tashi babu wahala ayau ne tun dare NU’AIMA take faman nak’uda,inda paris ya kaita wani private hospital da take zuwa bayan sun dawo shine wanda suke zuwa shida NI’IMATULLAH haka ya kirawo su momy da ummi da Daddy amma ba’a fad’awa NI’IMATULLAH ba saboda halin datake ciki har saida NU’AIMA ta haifi santaleliyar yarinyar ta mai mutuk’ar kyu kamar su d’aya da paris kamar an tsaga kara... haka akayi ta murna saida suka dawo gida da yake lafiyar ta kalau sanan ta kira NI’IMATULLAH a waya take fad’a mata kamar dai wasa taji abin dan bata yarda ba,har saida kuwa tazo taga yarinyar mai kama da ita sak masha Allah jini yau gashi NU’AIMA ta haifi yarinya mai kama da ita ubangiji ya raya mana bisa sunna. Haka tayi mata addu’a sosai kuma akace baza’ai suna ba Sai NI’IMATULLAH ta haihu za’a had’e.
Cikin ikon Allah kwana uku tsakani NI’IMATULLAH ta haifi kyawawan yan biyu da kanta kuma itama cikin koshin lafiya gaba d’ayan su mata haka wanan family suka cika da farin ciki da godiya da Allah da ya nuna musu wanan rana.

Ranar suna ta zagayo aka sawa yarinyar paris sunan NI’IMATULLAH inda suke kiranta da Najwa,inda ita kuma twince d’in NI’IMATULLAH hassana aka saka mata Rahma sunan ummi kenan usainar kuma aka sa mata NU’AIMA inda ake kiran hasana da ummi k’arama ita kuma NU’AIMA Najma.
Haka aka sha taro cikin farin ciki da kaunar juna....

SHAFI NA ARBA’IN DA TARADA  4


Zama yayi kusa da matar sa inda suka gaisa dasu gani yayi NU’AIMA tana ta faman b’ata rai sai ya janyo ta jikin sa”wife maike damunki,naga kamar da damuwa yanzu a face d’inki,d’ago ido tayi suka fara had’a ido da NA’EM ya wulla mata kallon banza saurin dauke kai tayi gefe ta sunkuyar tare da cewa babu komai,”kin tabbata girgiza kai tayi sama,ita kuwa NI’IMATULLAH tuni suke ta fira dasu momy da ummin ta,a nan NA’EM yayi musu sallama shi zai wuce wajen aikin sa,idan ya dawo sai yazo ya d’auketa.

Zama tayi Sosai tasa ummi agaba akan sai ta bata labarin iyayenta,aikuwa harda momy a cikin jin labarin,dan kuwa tace tayi mutuk’ar kewar su sau d’aya suka je da Daddy kafin kishiya ta canza masa lissafi,Paris ne da bai wuce wajen aiki ba yace”aikuwa ummi tabbas zamu shirya tafiya kuma mu duka zamu har Daddyn idan ya dawo zan masa magana.
“To amma Salman ya maganar tafiyar ka dubai,kasan Abban ka bazai jirakaba ko.”
“Insha Allah momy indai yaji wanan tafiyar ce,zai min uzuri idan nadawo sai mu wuce.”
NI’IMATULLAH ce tad’an turo baki sanan tace”yanzu bro idan ka koma dubai sai kuma yaushe.”
Cikin tsokana yace mata sai ranar da Abbana zai dawo kano dindin din da zama to ranar nima zan dawo shikkenan.”
Kallo NU’AIMA tayi taga ko zata damu,amma ina sai wani Murmushi ma takeyi tana kallon mijin nata.
Girgiza kai tayi itama tana ta zuba murmushi kamar gonar auduga,ta godewa Allah daman haka take son k’awar tata ta kasance cikin farin ciki ita da mijin ta.(wanan itace abotar gaskiya ta tsakani da Allah,Allah yayi alk’awarin duk wanda ya faro abutar sa saboda Allah har ya mutu suna san junansu saboda Allah,to wanan abota zata iya kaisu gidan aljanna ,Allah ka sada mu da abokai nagari wanda zasu tunatar damu Allah,wad’an da idan mun kauce zasu tunatar damu,Ameen ya hayyu ya qayyum.)


Haka sukayi ta fira kala,kala,NI’IMATULLAH kamar ba ita ba,aikuwa har tsokana tasha wajen NU’AIMA sanan ta fad’awa ummi cewa Allah bata tab’a gani ba ko da wane NI’IMATULLAH tazauna tana magana haka kawai,amma kinga yanzu ta kamo nan ta kawo can.
Ummi tayi Murmushi Allah ya sani ita kanta tana son kasance wa da yaran nata kusa da ita,dan kuwa idan au Abbas ma suka sata agaba da surutu haka takeji dad’i yana ratsa sassan jikin ta.


Saida sukayi sallah sanan NI’IMATULLAH tacewa ummi zataje gidan malam NU’AIMA ta taso ta rakata.ummi ce tace ku dakata NI’IMA kin manta kuna da aure ina fatan kun tambayi mazajenku.”
“Kai ummi ai duk d’ayane tunda mun tambayeki.”
“Gidan ku,momy tace sanan tace”aa inna ya zama d’aya,zaki kira ki tambaya ko sai na hana tafiyar ko sun yarda.”
Momy tayi haka ne kawai dan ta tunatar dasu cewa wajibine duk inda matar aure zata fita sai da izinin mijin ta,tasan sun sani amma take k’ara tunatar dasu...


NI’IMATULLAH Tafad’a mus ta sanar dashi,NU’AIMA itace ta shiga d’aki tana kiran sa a waya sai da ya d’aga sanan ta fad’a masa yace babu damuwa Allah ya tsare hanya.


Motar gidan suka d’auka har driver ya shirya,NI’IMA tace ya barta zatayi tuk’i kawai.
Haka kuwa akayi ta shiga b’angaren driver ita kuma ta shiga b’angaren mai zaman banza,haka sukaje har gidan su babah jummai sukai horn mai gado ya bud’e sun tarar malam baya nan,suna shiga suka ga ya fiti zai fita a motarsa,tsugunnawa sukayi suka gaida shi sanan yayi wa NI’IMATULLAH ya jikinta ta amsa masa da sauki,yayi musu Addu’a ta Allah yayi musu albarka ya basu zuri’a d’ayyiba sanan suka tashi suka shiga gidan,da sauri hannatu da yanzu ita da kanwar tane a d’akin sama Na NI’IMATULLAH suka sakko suna mata Oyoyoooo kamar zasu cinyeta haka murja aka fito ana mata sannu da zuwa haka babah jummai ma,badai su koma ba sai yamma dan kuwa saida babah tace dole sai sunce abinci haka kuwa akayi sukaci suka zauna suka ringa fira sanan da lokaci yayi suka tafi.



Bayan sun koma gida da daddare kowa ya d’auki matar sa zuwa gida ,ita dai NI’IMATULLAH taga duk hanya d’aya suke bi,dan haka tacewa NA’EM “habibi.”
“Na’am habibty.”
“Habiby gaskiya yau sai ka fad’a min gaskiya,Allah na tuna ma a inda daddy ya siya muku gida a kusa da kusa muke da rabin raina ko.”
Murmushi yayi sanan ya kamo hannunta yace”tunda kince gaskiya Habibty to shikkenan bab damuwa kafin na shiga gida zan nuna miki.


Aikuwa with her surprise yadda ta tsammata sai gashi ga gidanta ga gidan NU’AIMA,aikuwa Yana tsayawa nuna mata batasan lokacin da ta fita ba kafin su Paris su shiga da motar ciki suka ganta tsaye akansu,sakko da glasss d’in yayi sanan yace lafiya rabin raina.
“Lafiya Kalau Alhmdlh,ashe daman yya ga gidan mu ga gidan ka shine kukaki sanar mana kodan kar innazuwa shikkenan nagode bazama ku sake gani naba.sai tayi k’ofar gidan nata a k’afa  inda NA’EM ya k’araao yayi horn mai gado ya bud’e masu ita ta shiga a k’afa shi ya shiga a mota yana ta kiranta,itama NU’AIMA sakkowa tayi tabi NI’IMATULLAH har can d’akinta tana ce mata itama bata sani ba wallahi,idan ta tambayi gidan ta sai yace babu yar tafiya,.
Ahaka dai da kyar mazajen nasu suka shawo kan har suka hak’ura sai can wajen k’arfe d’aya ma sanan suka rabu..


Daddy ya shirya tafiya zuwa k’asar su Rahma haka ya shirya inda zasu tafi da,momy,ummi,NI’IMATULLAH,Paris da matar sa,sanan NA’EM da kuma wata k’anwar sa. Haka kuwa akayi rana bata k’arya saida uwar d’iya taji kunya,haka aka shirya tafiya sati biyu masu zuwa sukaje sukayiwa su momyn NU’AIMA sallama sanan jirginsu ya d’aga sai k’asar 🇳🇪 daga nan ma da suka sauka a jirgi suka hau mota,sunyi tafiya sosai zuwa wani gari arleet,garin buzayene yana da nisa sosai haka suka k’ara inda rahma tunda suka shiga garin take kukan kewar mahaifanta datayi babu wanda ma yasa suna raye ko sun mutu sai Allah suna zuwa duk an canza kalar gine ginan gaba d’aya unguwar ma takasa ganewa,sai tambaya sukayai inda suka tambayi gidan mahaifin nata aka nuna musu,Allah sarki rayuwa ya tsufa tukuf aikuwa haka suka nufi babban gidan da sallama.
Suna shiga matan gidan suka amsa suna ta kallonsu,Arfa ce da gudu tazo ta rungume yar uwar ta tana kuka,”ko zanyi shekara dubu bangankiba dole idan kikazo ko wace irin canzawa kikai na gane ki yar uwata.ta fad’a da yaren su.”
“Itama sosai ta rungumeta suna kuka.”
Sauran matan gidan duk basu gane suba sai kuma wata mata tsohuwa datake zaune akan tabarma a tsakar gida tace”muryar wa nakeji kamar Rahma.”
“Rahma Rahma kina ina matso kusa na ganki.”
Wata budurwa ce ta shigo tana fad’in zaki fara ko,kullum Kita kirahn Rahma Rahma kina cewa zata dawo amma har yau bamu gantaba,ta fad’a da hausar da bata fita sosai,ai da gudu ta saki Arfa ta nufi inda maman nata take ta rungumeta sosai tana kuka,matan gidan gabad’aya suka fito akayiwa su NI’IMATULLAH da suke tsaye sunan sharar kwalla aka shimfid’a musu k’aruwar dadduma,sanan ita NI’IMATULLAH ta fita tace su Daddy su shigo dan Tafad’awa matan gidan basu kad’aibane suna tare da iyayen ta,aikuwa matan gidan sukace tayi masu iso mana su shigo...
Ana haka saiga wani tsoho ya fito daga bayi tare da cewa”murya wa nakeji kamar Rahmaa.”wata budurwar yarinya ce tace eh Abbah ka k’araso itace.”.......

SHAFI NA ARBA’IN DA TAKWAS 48

Da asuba ya tashi yayi Alwala yayi sallah sanan ya tasheta itama tayi alwala tayi sallah wani bacci take ji sosai yana fisgarta dakyar tayi sallar sanan ta koma ta kwanta,kallon ta yayi da alama gajiya ce narke a jikin ta dan haka shi bai koma ba ya zauna yayi karatun al’qur’ani mai girma yayi azkar,bai tashi daga wajen ba sai wajen 7 dan haka ya fito ya nufi kitchen dan yana so yayi musu breakfast da kansa saboda bayason ya tasheta saboda ganin halin da take ciki na gajiya,kamar dai a time d’in da suke a madina ya iya girki sosai wani lokacin ma shi yake dafawa kansa abincin duk da yake ci,dan haka girkin ba sabonsa bane dan haka bai bashi wahala ba,haka ya gama yazo dining ya jera komai da komai,bayason dai ya tashe ta kawai sai ya shiga d’akinsa yayi wanka ya sako jallabiya ya fito ya nufi d’akinta,yana zuwa ya tarar tana baccin amma tayi juyi alamar ta tashi,bud’e idonta tayi inda sukayi ida hud’u dashi ya sakar mata murmushi tare da fad’ar”sabahul khari habibty.”
Murmushi tayi masa cike da wani kalar abu yau data farka dashi mai fusgar zuciya,”sabahul nur habibi.”
“Fatan kin tashi lfy masoyiya.”
“Lafiya kalau kamar yadda ka tashi.”
“Masha Allah Alhmdlh yanzu dai bari nazo na kaiki bathroom dan kiyi wanka koma dai nayi miki.”
Murmushi tayi tana rufe ido kafin ta ankare taga har yazo ya rungumota har zuwa bathroom d’in.
“Please habibi kabarni Allah zanyi.”
“No may be baza ki iya fitar da jikin ki ba,kamar yadda zanyi.”
Ya kaita ban d’akin tafara brush sanan tace”plsss habibi babu kyu mutane biyu a bathroom fa.”
Kafin ta k’arasa maganar batasan sadda yasa bakinsa a nata ba yana kissing d’in a wani kalar mood datakasa banbancewa,tunda take bata tab’a enjoying wani kiss like na NA’EM ba,he’s very sweet nd memorable,itama batasan sadda ta fara bada kai ba,amma ganin NA’EM ya fita hayyacin sa yanaso ya zarce nan ta fara kuka tana cewa dan Allah ya fita ya barat she will do it bye her self.
Bai jitaba har saida yakai aya sanan yayi mata wanka and he took her to bedroom sanan ya koma band’akin shima yayi wanka at second time...
Kafin ya fito tayi saurin saka kaya saboda yau NA’EM ya kasheta da yawa da so samu ne ma yanzu ya kuma amma tana jin tsoron yadda taga yana yin nasa,bayan ya fito ne yace ta shafa masa mai Batare da gaddama ba ta d’auki man ta fara murzashi a lallausan hannunta sanan takai shi kan tausasan k’afafuwan sa tafara shafawa a wani kalar yanayi,haka suka kammala sanan suka fito a tare inda yayi huggin nata har suka k’arasa.”


“Wowww it’s amazing daman habibi ka iya girki wowww gaskiya har na k’aku naci,daga ji da gani zaiyi dad’i sosai Wayyo habibi kaji wani k’amshi da yake tashi kamar dai ba’a nan ba.”
“Ohhh habibty kiyi a hankali da zuzuta wanan abinci haka kar naje ki kasa ci.”
“Like how,ai bazan tab’a kasa ciba habibi,gaskiya I’m very happy Allah ya saka maka.”
“Ameen ya Allah,tun da nagana ma na d’ibi na malam nasa aka kaimai kinsan anjima zai wuce.”
“Allah sarki malam Allah ya saka masa da alkhairi ya saka masa da gidan Aljanna.”
Ta fad’a tana kallon NA’EM.
“Kasan mai habibi ina jina kamar wata sabuwar hallita a duniyar nan ji nake kamar an sauke min wani nauyi da da nake jinsa a kaina,ina jina tamkar yau uwata ta kawo ni duniya,nagode habibi duk wanan ya faru ne just because of u bazan daina addu’a Allah ya faranta maka ya haskaka rayuwar kaba kamar yadda ka haskaka tawa.”
“Ohhh habibty to maine na kukan sorry ya fad’a yana rungumota sanan yace ki share hawayen ki kici abinci.”
Kasa yin shiru tayi saida yace “ok bazakiyi shirun ba ko,tam shikkenan zan miki kuwa abin... kafin ya k’arasa tace nayi shiru,haka yayi saving nasu tafara bashi a baki har suka kammala.
Komawa palorn sukayi suka zauna akan kujera twos seater inda ya sake janyota jikin sa and itama kwantawa tayi a k’irjinsa ya kunna kallo suna cikin kallo bacci ya d’auke ta Murmushi yayi sosai ya sake gyara mata kwanciyar a jikinsa.

Misalin k’arfe biyu yasa oder ai kuwa har gida aka zo aka kawo masa inda ya fita ya amsa,a time d’in ta tashi dan ta shiga ma tayi sallah.
Zuwa yayi d’akin ya kirata yace ta fito suyi lunch,ai kuwa ta  fito gani yayi ta zura hijab.
“Ahh madam ina zaki naga kin saka hijab.”
Zun bu’ra k’aramin bakin ta tayu tare da cewa”zani gidan ummina ne.”
“Hhhhh kuma biki tambayi mijin kiba zaki fita.”
“Ayya nifa shaf na sha’afa wai inada aure.”
“Ohhhh hakane to bara na tabbatar miki,kawai ya biyota da niyar d’aukarta da gudu ta fita tana fad’ar”wallahi wasa nake maka kawai dai inaso naje na gaisa da malam ne sanan na kai masa abincin sa.”
Dariya yayi batare da yace komai ba.

Zuwa tayi ta d’ibi abinci ta zuba a flask sanan ta fito ta nufi inda malam musa yake,tana zuwa ta shiga da sallama yama gama shiryawa kawai tafiya ta rage masa,shiga tayi da sallama cikin jin dad’i ya amsa sanan ta zauna ta fara gaida shi tayi masa godiya mai tarin yawa ta bashi abinci sanan yace shi da yanzu ma zai tafi airport tace masa dan Allah yaci abinci sai suzo su kaishi.


Haka kuwa akayi bayan ta koma take fad’awa NA’EM cewa malam ya gama shiri,anan ne tace tanaso ta d’an yi masa siyayya saboda tanaso tayi masa isani sosai yace babu damuwa sai su tsyaa a super market dan siyayyar.

Haka akayi kuwa suka fito malam ya fito ya shiga gidan baya suka fita aka barwa mai gadi jiran gidan kamar yadda suka tsara haka sukaje suka masa siyayya ta shatara ta arziki sanan Daddyn dubai daman yace a tura masa account nasa shi kuma Daddyn su NA’EM jiya ya gana dashi,haka dai suka kaishi airport cikin girmamawa sukarabu suna godiya da taimakonsa.


Suna hanyar su ta dawo tace masa”habibi dan Allah ka kaini gidan ummi na ganta.”
“No ba yauba.”
“Dan Allah fa nace habibi nayi kewar ta.”
Fara kuka tayi sanan yace gobe zan kaiki.”
“Tam amma habibi saida mu fita da sassafe saboda zani gidan malam,nayi kewar su.”
“Shikkenan babu damuwa duk zaki ke insha Allah..


Bayan sun koma gida ne ta zauna a palorn tayi tagumi,yana zuwa yace”lafiya habibty.”
“Babu komai kawai tunanin rayuwa nake.”
Daukar ta yayi tare da cewa banason kiyi tunanin rayuwa kawai nafi son a koda yaushe ki zama kina tunani na. Ya fad’i haka daidai lokacin da ya ajje ta kan bed ya rufa akanta ya fara aika mata da sak’onnin da suka wuce lissafin ta....



Washe gari babu irin shagwab’ar da NI’IMATULLAH batayiwa NA’EM ba shikuwa sai kallab’ata yake yi da wata irin kauna datake k’ara shiga tsakanin su gaskiya NI’IMATULLAH ta bashi farin ciki sosai ta sakashi a duniyar dabai tab’a tunanin zaijetaba Allah ya saka mata da alkhairi ya albarkaci rayuwar auren su...


NI’IMATULLAH bata sake cewa suje gidan ummi ba saboda ita kunya ma take ji sosai wallahi,dan haka saida sukayu sati d’aya suna more amarcin su sanan yace ranar monday zai koma aiki daga hutun da ya d’auka dan haka ranar monday d’in zai kaita gidan ummin ta...
Cikin zumud’i take ta shirye shirye amma shirin ma bayay yi mata sauri,sark’a take son sawa a wuyanta amma ta kasa NA’EM yana kallonta tana ta k’ok’ari kawia ganin sark’ar zata b’ata mata lokaci yasa tayi watsi da ita akan mirro d’in ta tashi,bata ankara ba taji NA’EM ya ringumota ta baya yana kallon su a mudubi tare da fad’i”u look like a queen,Murmushi tayi sanan tace”and u prince,hhhh nd ke kuma princess dariya tayi,sanan ya d’aukko sark’ar ya saka mata yasa mata abin hannu tasa d’an kunne sanan ya zuge matq zip d’in rigar,haka itama ta yashi suka shirya suka nufi gidan ummi...


Suna shiga palor taji surutun mutuniyar yana tashi,tana shiga taganta ita da momy ne suke ta fira ummi kuwa tana gefe tana Murmushi...

Sallama tayi sanan ta kallo NU’AIMA tace”Allah dai ya sake shirya min ke Ninu ko kunya bakyaji kinzo kinsa surukar ki a gaba sai zance kike zubawa.”
Dariya sukayi sanan shima ya shigo yana kallo NU’AIMA data tsuke bakin ta ganin da NA’EM ne yanzu sai ya yarfata...


Zama yayi a kusa da NU’AIMA ya gaida su momy da ummi suka amsa cikin fara’a da jin dad’i,saida hankali su ya d’auke daga wajen yaja kunne NU’AIMA ya murd’e yace”ke har yanzu bazaki hakaliba ko,a gaban surukar taki kike ta surutu ba kamun kai ko.”
Zatayi k’ara yace mata kika sake kika min k’ara sai na dakeki a wajen nan na fasa bakin ki,aku kawai... daidai lokacin paris ya fito rik’e da cup na lemo guda biyu yana fadin”ahhhh kuce manyanbak’i mukayi.....
